1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du//
2a1(rgyud): @##//sarba b+hu ta sa ma yo ka d+ha ki ni 'dzal
2a2(rgyud): sham ba/ra na ma ya u da ra tan tra/bod skad du/
2a3(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba/mkha' 
2b1(rgyud): 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog ces bya ba'i rgyud phyi ma /dpal 
2b2(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//sems dpa' sangs
2b3(rgyud): rgyas kun gyi dngos//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/
3a1(rgyud): @#//gsang ba mchog gi dges pa na//thams cad bdag nyid 
3a2(rgyud): rtag tu bzhugs/'dir ni rang 'byung bcom ldan 'das//gcig tu rab 
3a3(rgyud): tu phye ba'i lha//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma 
3a4(rgyud): bde ba'i mchog/chags med chags dang bral ba min//dbu mar yang ni mi 
3b1(rgyud): mi dmigs so//bud med kun sgyu ma'i rgya//'di ni gnyis med theg pa'i mchog/sgyu ma 
3b2(rgyud): dag ni thams cad pas//bud med sgyu ma khyad par che//ngo bo nyam kyi mthu dag gis//rang bzhin
3b3(rgyud): gyis ni de grub pa'o//'jig rten gsum 'dir bud med 'dis//gus par byed pa dag du 'gyur
3b4(rgyud): brnyed dang bde dga' kun gyis//ngan pa spyod pa 'ang 'grub par 'gyur//rang gi ngo bo yongs 
4a1(rgyud): @#//bsgyur na ྾bud med kun gyis sgyu ma 'grub//ngo mtshar sgyu ma'i phyag rgya 'di//mkha' 
4a2(rgyud): 'gro ma zhes bdas stan te//de ni nam mkha' 'gro ba zhes//khams la mngon du brtags pa ste//naM mkha' 
4a3(rgyud): kun du spyod 'grub pas//mkha' 'gro ma zhes rab tu grags//thams cad ma lus bde mchog gis//
4a4(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro ma zhes rab tu grags//de ni nam mkha' 'gro 
4a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas khams la rnam par brtags//kun du 'gro ba grub pa ni//sangs rgyas kun bdag mkha' 'gro ma'o 
4b1(rgyud): sham zhes bya ba bde bar bshad//sangs rgyas kun gyi bde chen yin//sgyu ma thams cad rab sbyor ba//mchog tu bde ba'i
4b2(rgyud): bde ba'i mchog/kun du kun nas thams cad du//bdag nyid rtag tu thams cad kun//sangs rgyas la sogs gtan g.yo
4b3(rgyud): kun||kun gyi dngos por bdag gyur to//sems dang sems dpa' ting 'dzin kun//byang chub spyod pa sna tshogs nyid//de bzhin
4b4(rgyud): bdud dang bdud las rgyal//de ni byang chub sangs rgyas yin//rdo rje rdo rje 'dzin nyid dang//de bzhin pad mo pad mo 'chang//nor
4b5(rgyud): bu nor bu 'dzin nyid dang//de dag gi yang rigs yin no//de nyid 'dod chags 'dod chags bral//'dod pa dang ni 'gro ba 'ang 
4b6(rgyud): yin||dus gsum srid gsum mchog gsum dang//'jig rten gsum dang khams gsum dang//rnam pa sna tshogs tha dad pa'i//
5a1(rgyud): @#//gnas pa'i thams cad de nyid do//'gro ba dag ni ma lus kun//thams cad du ni 'di 'gyur ro//de las swogs pa mtha' yas mchog/chos
5a2(rgyud): dbyings mnyam dang mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis mthas klags pa'i//dngos po thams cad de yin te//nam mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//
5a3(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba'i//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog go/bskal pa bye ba grangs med par
5a4(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad ma thob pa'i//phyag rgya|'i cho ga dang dag gis//tshe 'di nyid kyis thob 'gyur pa//de ni bdag gis brtag par bya//sangs
5a5(rgyud): rgyas kun bdag bde ba'i mchog/bdag nyid sha na du bsgrub pa las//sangs rgyas kun bdag bde ba'imchog/rnal 'byor yang dag ma yin sos//
5a6(rgyud): lha la dmigs par byed pa dag/rnal 'byor gzugs brnyan rang bzhin te//gzugs laswogs pa'i rnal 'byor skye//yang dag mchog sems rnal 'byor pa//lha
5a7(rgyud): la dmigs par byed par dag/rang gi dam tshig bdag sbyor ba//mi 'gyur dam pa 'grub par 'gyur//gzugs brnyan lugs ma sogs pa la//rnal 'byor 'byung
5a8(rgyud): bar mi 'gyur gyi//byang chub sems la rab brtson na//de 'ang rnal 'byor lhar 'gyur ro//byang chub sems ni rdo rje ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid yin//de bas thos
5b1(rgyud): cad bdag sbyor bas//sangs rgyas thams cad thob par 'gyur//bdag nyid sangs rgyas thams cad dang//dpa' bo thams cad bdag yin no//de bas bdag nyid lhar sbyor bas//
5b2(rgyud): bdag nyid rab tu bsgrub par bya//de yis sangs rgyas thams cad dang //dpa' bo rnams kyang thams cad dang//rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad du //tshe 'di nyid la bsgrub par 'gyur//
5b3(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpa' de skad ces gsungs so//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor bas//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma'i bde mchog las//de kho na nyid snang ba dam
5b4(rgyud): pa'i yul la 'jug pa ye shes kyi phyag rgya brtag pa ste dang po'o//'di ni rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas thams cadamanyam sbyor ba//
5b5(rgyud): mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' bdag sbyor bas//dbab pa thams cad rab tu 'grub//sangs rgyas thams cad gnyam sbyor ba//rnal 'byor dbang phyug
5b6(rgyud): bde ba che//de bas bde ba nyid kyis ni//sangs rgyas bde ba kun thob pas//sangs rgyas thams can mnyam sbyor ba//rnal 'byoradabang phyug bde chen po//'di la khrus dang
5b7(rgyud): snga ma mi dgos//dka' thub dka' bar spyad mi dgos//dka' thub sdom pa med pa dang//bde ba dang ni dga' bas 'grub//de bas ji ltar 'dod spyod cing//kun za de bzhin thams cad spyod//spyod pa ci
5b8(rgyud): dgar 'dod par byed//g.yo ba dag kyang ci dgar byed//langs pa dang ni 'dug pa dang//'chag gam ji ltar gnas kyang rung//dgod dam rab tu smra yang rung//gang na ji ltar spyod kyang rung//dkyil
6a1(rgyud): @#//'khor du yang ma zhugs pa 'am//sgrib pa kun dang ldan yang rung //rang gi lhar ni bdag sbyor na//bsod nams chung yang 'grub par 'gyur//de nyid rnal 'byor 'di yis ni//thams cad sgrub par 
6a2(rgyud): byed pa na//ngan zos ngan spyod thams cad kyis//nyes pa dag du yong mi 'gyur//bskal pa bye ba mi 'thob la//sangs rgyas dam pa thams cad te//phyag rgya'i cho ga gang dag gis//bsgrubs nas kun du 
6a3(rgyud): 'thob 'gyur ba//bdag nyid sgrub par byed pa'i mchog/de ni bdag gis brtag par bya//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' bdag
6a4(rgyud): sbyor ba//cha lugs ji bzhin bsgyur ba yi//thams cad sna tshogs phyag rgya'i bdag/bsod nams chung yang grub par 'gyur//gyur rgyud 'di bsgrubs pa tsam gyis ni//sangs rgyas thams
6a5(rgyud): cad byin gyis rlob//shin du byin gyis brlabs nas ni//sangs rgyas thams cad 'du bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor bas//sangs rgyas kun dang ma nyam par 'gyur//sangs
6a6(rgyud): rgyas kun dang mnyam par 'gyur//ngas rgyas thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//gang gis ldan pa 'di mthong zhing //rnam pa kun du mchod byed na//de yis sangs rgyas thams cad kyang //mthong zhing mchod pa
6a7(rgyud): byas par 'gyur//'di dang sangs rgyas thams cad la//mthong ba dang ni rig pa yis//dkyil 'khor dag du ma zhugs pa 'am//bden pa dag kyang mthong bar 'gyur//mthong ba dang ni rigs pa dang //mthong ba dang
6a8(rgyud): ni dran pa yis//sdig pa kun las rnam grol zhing //sangs rgyas dngos grub thams cad 'grub//'di dang sangs rgyas thabs cad la//rnal 'byor thams cad mnyam sbyor bas//bud med dag kyang rnam grol zhing /
6b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas byang chub reg par 'gyur//kun du kun nas thams cad ni//bdag nyid brtag par thams cad du//sangs rgyas thams cad dngos grub par//de yis bdag nyid mthong bar 'gyur//'di ni bdag nyid kun gnas
6b2(rgyud): pas//mchod pa'i sprin chen thams cad kyis//bkod par tshogs byam tshogs dag gis//de bzhin gshegs pa mchod pas mdzes//thams cad bdag nyid sbyor mchod dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' mchog nyid ni//sangs
6b3(rgyud): rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin te//bde ba'i mchog ni 'grub par 'gyur//rnal 'byor thams cad nye sbyor bas//dpal ni gzhir 'gyur 'grub par 'gyur//rnal byor kun gyi bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin
6b4(rgyud): gshegs//de yi nye bar sbyor ba ni//khams gsum ma lus thams cad do//rnal 'byor kun gyi longs spyod kyis//ji ltar bde bar bsnyen byed cing //rang gi lha'i rnal 'byor gyis//bdag nyid rab
6b5(rgyud): tu mchod par bya//rjes su sbyor bas mchod byed cing //rnal 'byor kun du bde ba dag/bdag nyid kun du mya byed na//shin du sbyor bas 'gu ba par 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid kyis//ra sa ya
6b6(rgyud): na bde ba 'dis//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal che dang //lang tsho nad med bde mchog 'grub//kun du kun nas thams cad du//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor bas//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/rtag tu de ni 'grub
6b7(rgyud): 'gyur|bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas de skad ces gsungaso//sangs rgyas thams cad nyam sbyor bas//mkha' 'gros sgyu ma bde mchog las//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' kun du kha sbyor ba'i rtag pa ste gnyis pa'o//
6b8(rgyud): ////'di ni rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje seMs dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas thams cad gny-i sbyor bas//mkha' 'gro sgyur ma bde ba'i mchog/dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i rnal 'byor pa//dpal
7a1(rgyud): @#//dpal ldan rang gi dam tshig bdam//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba'i//rnal 'byor dbang phyug bde ba che//dpal ldan rdo rje sems dpa' sbyor ba yi//mthu nyid kyis ni thams cad du//phyag 
7a2(rgyud): rgya bde ba chen po kun//so so rjes su rab 'grub 'gyur//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' sbyor pa yi//mthu nyid kyis na thams cad du//rnal 'byor bde ba mos pa kun//so sor rjes su rab 'grub 'gyur//dpal rdo
7a3(rgyud): rje sems dpa' sbyor ba yis//mthu nyid kyis na thamasacad du//bde ba mos pa'i dngos grub kun//so so rjes su rang 'grub 'gyur//dpal rdorje sems dpa'i sbyor ba yi//mthu nyid kyi na thams cad du//
7a4(rgyud): bde ba mos pa'i dbang phyug kun//so so rjes su rangs 'grub 'gyur dpal rdo rje sems dpar sbyor ba yi//mthu nyid kyin na thams cad du//nam mkha'i dbyings nas mthas klags pa'i 
7a5(rgyud): bud med mchog rnams skye bar 'gyur//de'i dam tshig de 'byung ba sdug pa dam tshig bdag rjes 'brang //shin tu sbyor dang sbyor chen po//thams cad bdag nyid dam tshig mchog//
7a6(rgyud): gcig du bzhin du gcig spyod pa//lhan cig spyod pas rjes spyod sdug//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma mchog du 'bar//thams cad dga' ba ma lus pa'i//phyag rgya 
7a7(rgyud): thams cad bde ba che//rtag pa dam pa lha mo yi//rgyal mos mchod pa thams cad mchog//dpal rdo rje sems dpar spyor ba dang //thams cad lha mo 'dus pas ni//ma lus bskal pa bzang po 
7a8(rgyud): kun//bde bar so sor rjes su 'grub//phyag rgya 'di yis btab pa las//rdo rje sems dpa' che ba'i lha//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klags pa//lha mo bde ba chen po kun//thams cad rnal 'byor 
7b1(rgyud): bde bas myos//sdig med stobs kyis de 'thon du//phan tshun du ni rab dga' bas//skyes bu'i tshul gyis dgar bar byed//dbang po kun las byung ba yi//mchod pa'i sprin chen thams cad kyis//
7b2(rgyud): bkod pa rab 'jam sna tshogs kyis//thams cad bdag nyid mchod par byed//rnal 'byor bde ba thams cad kyis//dbang bskur bde chen las byung ba//rin chen mtshogs rnams thams
7b3(rgyud): cad kyis//rab tu rmongs pas dbang skur 'gyur//lha mo kun gyis longs spyod pa//ci bder bar ni bsnyen bya ba//bdag ni che ba'i lhar sbyor bas//bdag la bdag gis rab tu mchod//
7b4(rgyud): rnal 'byor thams cad bde ba yi//rjes su sbyor bas mchod byed cing//rtag tu myang bar byed pa na//shin du sbyor bas rab 'grub 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad bdag nyid
7b5(rgyud): kyi//ra sa ya na bde ba des//dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i tshe 'grub cing//lang 'tsho nad med bde mchog 'grub//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku chen dang//sangs rgyas kun gyi gsung snyan pa//sangs
7b6(rgyud): rgyas kun gyi thugs che ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi mchod pa che//sangs rgyas kun gyi rgyal chen po//rdo rje 'dzin pa kun gyi bdag//'jig rten dbang phyug kun gyi gtso//rin chen dbang phyug kun gyi bdag//de dag 
7b7(rgyud): dang ni dga' spyod na//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng//lha mo thams cad grub chen po//'khor los sgyur ba rab tu 'grub//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klags
7b8(rgyud): par//dngos grub kun gyi bde mos pa//de la rgya che mchog gzhan med//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rnal 'byor dbang phyug chen po des//'di la tshe 'di nyid kyis ni//rnam par kun du mi 
8a1(rgyud): @#//'grub pa'i/dngos grub kun gyi bde mos pa//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klags pa//dbang phyug kun gyi bde mos pa//dam pa rgya chen 
8a2(rgyud): gang yang med//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog des//'di la tshe 'di nyid kyis ni//rnam pa kun du mi 'grub med//bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems 
8a3(rgyud): dpas de skad gsungs so//sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gre sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las//lha mo thams cad dang rnam nyam par sbyor ba//bskal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje dam tshig gi 
8a4(rgyud): rtag pa ste gsum pa'o////'di ni rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas thams cad nyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro rgyu ma bde ba'i mchog 
8a5(rgyud): lha mo kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//bskal pa bza' mchog 'dod pa ste//yang dag sbyor ba'i bde myong ba//'dod chags ther zug btan pa can//sangs rgyas kun gyi gzugs chen dang //dbyangs 
8a6(rgyud): dang dri dang ro dag dang //sangs rgyas kun gyi reg bde mchog//rang gi spyad pas de spyod do//sangs rgyas pha rol phyin grub pa//gzungs dang sa rnams de bzhin du//dga' bar spyod na lha dang mi'i//bskal pa 
8a7(rgyud): bzang bud med ci zhig smos//lha'i dngos grub dga' ba dang //sangs rgyas byang chub bde chen po//bskal pa bzang po bde myong bas//so so rjes su rab 'grub 'gyur//dngos grub thams cad las byung ba//
8a8(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi dpal//bskal pa bzang po'i longs spyod kun//rang gi spyad par de spyod do//rdo rje can gyi grub mchog dang //'jig rten dbang phyug mchog gi dpal//sangs rgyas dag la rgyas bde 
8b1(rgyud): dam pa;khrogs nas nye bar spyod par byed//khams gsum dag na ma lus pa'i//sangs rgyas rgyal srid chen po kun//rtsal gyi stobs kyis ngos 'thon du//phrogs nas nye bar spyod par byed//sangs rgyas 
8b2(rgyud): kun gyi grub chen po//mchog gi dbang phyug dam pa dag//kun nas phrogs te de spyod na//longs spyod gzhan lta smos ci dgos//mthu chen las ni bkra shis brtan//'phrog las 'grub dang dan las dga'//
8b3(rgyud): ngo 'thon stobs kyis phrogs nas kyang//de ni dad la sbyin par byed//ma lus 'jig rten gsum rgyal srid//'jig rten dbang phyug kun dang bcas//ngo 'thon stobs kyis phrog nas kyang//de ni dad pa sbyin par 
8b4(rgyud): byed;;tshangs pa 'jug sel dbang chen gyi//mchog gi dbang phyug daM pa dag//kun nas phrog te myur sbyin na//longs spyod gzhan lta ci smos//de yi mi sbyin ci yang med//sangs rgyas  
8b5(rgyud): don du bya ba kun//mi bya ba ni gang yang med//sbyin pa dang ni byed pa'ang yin//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' yang//phrogs nas kyang ni sbyin byed na//las mchog dag ni rab 'jam dang//
8b6(rgyud): dngos grub gzhan lta ci zhig smos//dngos grub mchog ni gdon mi za//sde rnams thams cad rab 'joms pa//sangs rgyas kun gyi grub chen po//'khor los bsgyur ba rab 'grub pa//sangs rgyas thams cad
8b7(rgyud): mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog gi//sangs rgyas kun gyi rdzu 'phrul che//rnam par 'phrul pa rab tu 'jug//la lar byang chub sems chen po//la lar spyod pa 'thun par 'gro//la lar dga' ldan
8b8(rgyud): lha dag las;dam pa shin tu 'pho ba'o//la lar skye ba rnam par dag//la lar nges par 'byung ba gsal//la lar byang chub chen por gshegs//la lar bdud las rab tu rgyal//la lar byang chub mngon 'tshang rgya//
9a1(rgyud): @#//la lar chos kyi 'khor lo bskor//la lar pha rol mu stegs rnams//chos dang 'thun par tshar spyod do//la lar 'jig rten gsum rgyal ba//grub pa kun gyi dbang phyug nyid//la lar rtog pa 
9a2(rgyud): thams cad mchog/grub pa'i dbang phyug bla na med//de la sogs pa mtha' yas mchog/sangs rgyas kun bdag bde mchog gi//'dul ba'i thabs su nges pa'i gar//rnam par 'phrul bas dges shing rol//
9a3(rgyud): dngos grub chen po kun gyi mchog/brtan pa dbang phyug che ba'i bdag/gzugs dang tshe dang kha tog stobs//dpal ldan rdo rje sems dpa' bzhin//'gro ba dag gis bsnyen bya zhing //de bzhin gshegs pa kun 
9a4(rgyud): gyis mchod//lha mo kun gyis dpal ldan mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub//sems can khams ni du ma dag/ma lus par ni phyag rgyas 'debs//lha dang byang chub dka' ba dag/brtan par 
9a5(rgyud): sems can rnams la sbyin//sangs rgyas kun bdag bdai mchog gis sems can kun gyi don byed cing //ma lus lus pa med pa'i bsal ba kun tu srid par gnas//bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje 
9a6(rgyud): sems dpas de skad gsungs so//sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las//sna tshogs thams cad kyi bskal pa bzang po mchog gi dngos grub ces bya ste//
9a7(rgyud): dpal dam tshig gi brtag pa ste bzhi pa'o////'di ni rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas thams cadamanyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu la bde ba'i 
9a8(rgyud): mchog/yang dag bsgrub pa gcig pu nyid//ji ltar 'dod pa bzhin du sbyar//rtog pa'i cho ga thams cad la//grub pa dam pa mchog gi mchog/sna tshogs las kyi sbyor ba yis//cho ga sna tshogs 'dod 
9b1(rgyud): pa yis ;sems can rnams ni 'dul ba dang//bde ba thams cad 'grub pa'i phyir //bcom ldan rnal 'byor de nyid ni//sangs rgyas thams cad rnam 'phrul bas//sems can don kun rnam 'phrul na//
9b2(rgyud): myur du shin tu 'grub par 'gyur//nyin re 'am yang na zla re 'am//yang na de bzhin lo rer ni//ji bzhin byin gyis brlabs nas kyang//sangs rgyas bde mchog gar bya'o//khams kyi ngo bo thams cad dang
9b3(rgyud): de bzhin srog dang brtsol las byung//rang gi lha'i cha lugs su//grub pa'i phyag rgya rab tu bya//dkyil 'khor gang dang gang rten pa//sna tshogs phyag rgya'i mchod pa yis//slob ma'i tshogs kun de dang
9b4(rgyud): der ;mchod pa byas nas der bsgrub po//de las gzhan yang rtog/kun gyi//sangs rgyas thams cad ram 'phrul ba//ci rigs pa ni sprul byas pa'i//gtam dag rab tu bshad par bya
9b5(rgyud): sems dpa' sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog//gsang ba'i mchog gi dges pa ni//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs//'di ni rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'ad
9b6(rgyud): rtag pa dam pa brtan pa ste//rtag tu da ltar byung ba bzhin//cho ga sna tshogs 'dod rnams la//las kyi sbyor ba sna tshogs kyi//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin lasogs//'dul ba'i phyir ni byas pa yin//sangs rgyas lasog+sa
9b7(rgyud): pa brtan g.yo kun//kun gyi dngos grub 'di 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//sgyu ma'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni//rnam pa sna tshogs kun gyi mchog//sems can kun
9b8(rgyud): gyi don mchog rnams//sangs rgyas lasogs 'dul bas 'grub//dpal rdo rje sems dpas dbugs phyung ba'o//'das pa'i dus na sngon 'byung ba//bdag nyid chen po brtag pa yi//de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs 
10a1(rgyud): @#//rabs las 'gro ba'i tshul ni sngon byung ba/de'i tshe sems can thams cad ni//shin tu stong pa nyid la brtson//'gags pa'i chos nyid la sbyor bas//'gags shing yongs su mya ngan 'das//rnal 
10a2(rgyud): 'byor de bzhin bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//grol ba thams cad rnam sbyang phyir//sangs rgyas sprul pa kun du bskyed//stong pa ma yin mi stong min//dbu mar yang ni mi dmigs te//
10a3(rgyud): shes rab pha rol phyin sbyor ba//thabs kyi snying rje bdag nyid rnams//de bas de ni snying rje'i thabs//shes rab pha rol phyin par gsal//rtog pa med pa'i chos rnams la//sems can don du yongs rtogs pa//
10a4(rgyud): rnam par mi rtog la mos pas//thams cad rtogs pas rtag par bya//brag cha yi ni sgra 'dra ba//chos kyi tshig tu de gyur te//de las theg pa chen po'o//yang dag yon tan rgyas pa'i blus//dus gsum pa'i dam tshig 
10a5(rgyud): gsum ste de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad bstod//mnyam med mi g.yo mnyam nyid mchog gi chos nyid can//thugs rje'i bdag nyid 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal sel bar mdzad//yon tan mtha' 
10a6(rgyud): yas dngos grub thams cad sbyin mdzad pa//dri med mi g.yo mnyam med dam pa'i mchog chos can//yon tan shin tu phra ba'i che ba'ang tshad med do//dpe ni mkha' dang yang bskrun du yod pa min//dngos grub mtha' yang 
10a7(rgyud): mnyam med dpa' dang bral ba'i rnams//sems can khams la dngos grub mchog rnams nges par brtsol//rtag pa dri ma med pa'i thugs rje'i shugs las byung //smon lam grub pa 'gag pa med pa'i chos nyid can 
10a8(rgyud): 'gro ba'i don grub rab tu brtson pa mtha' yas pa//thugs rje chen po'i bdag nyid rtag tu rnam par spang //tshad med tshad dang bral bar shin tu yongs rdzogs pa'i//bde bar gshegs pa'i go 'phang dam pa brnyes gyur kyang 
10b1(rgyud): khams gsum khams la dngos grub mchog stsol mdzad pa yi//thugs rje'i spyod pa mi g.yo 'gag par yongs mi 'gyur//e ma'o shin du bzang po'i chos nyid mchog dang ldan//mchog sbyin rnams kyi
10b2(rgyud): nang na go 'phang mchog brnyes na//khams gsum kun la rtag tu dngos grub mchog stsol mdzod//mgon po dus gsum sgrib pa med par rab mkhyen par//dam tshig mtshan gsum dngos grub dam pa bdag
10b3(rgyud): la stsol;'dis ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//yang dag yon tan brgyan chen te//lan cig tsam ni brjod pas kyang//sangs rgyas thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun bdag nyid sbyor ba//stod 
10b4(rgyud): ba'i rgyal po 'di nyid kyis//dus gsum rgyal ba thams cad kyis//dam tshig gsum po 'grub par 'gyur//sku ni lta bas mi ngoms pa//thams cad bdag nyid brtan gnas kyang//legs so 
10b5(rgyud): zhes ni grub pa yis//mchog rnams kun ni rab sbyin mdzad//thams cad bdag nyid 'grub dga' dang//bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs//sangs rgyas grub pa mchog kun dang//rnal 'byor
10b6(rgyud): dam pa'ang sbyin mdzad te//de nas de'i bsgrub pa dang//shin tu brtag pa'i dngos grub dang//gang gis srid pa 'di nyid la//dngos grub shin tu rtag thob bshad//rdo rje se gol togs pa yin//sangs rgyas thams cad
10b7(rgyud): bar bya;thal mo legs par sbyar ba yi//thal mo lan gsum rnam par brdab//dbang po gang dang gang lam gyur//de dag de'i ngo bor bya//shin tu mnyam gzhag sbyor ba yis//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor zhing//
10b8(rgyud): ji ltar dga' mos chags pa dang//bshad pa lasogs pa yis ni//ci nas yongs su ma skyor bar//rtag tu tshad med bzlas par bya//kun du kun nas thams cad ni//bdag nyid rtag tu rnam pa kun//sangs rgyas kun dang dngos
11a1(rgyud): @#//grub pa rab tu mthong shing mchog grub po//'di yis sangs rgyas thams cad dang //de bzhin dpa' bo thams cad dang //rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad du//tshe 'di nyid kyis 'grub par 'gyur//sangs rgyas 
11a2(rgyud): kyi yon tan bsngags pa brgya che ba//bstod pa'i rgyal po mchog ni lan cig blur blangs pa//kun du 'gro ba'i rnal 'byor brtag pa'i mchog//dngos grub me bzad bde ba thob par 'gyur//bstod pa'i rgyal po 'di yis ni//
11a3(rgyud): thams cad bdag nyid dbang phyug mchog//sna tshogs kun gyi rnam 'dul ba//de bzhin gshegs pa kun 'grub ste//de bas dam tshig gsum la sogs//rtog pa bla med thams cad kyis//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klags 
11a4(rgyud): pa'i//sems can khams ni rnam par grol//dpal ldan rnam par snang mdzad kyi dbugs phyung ba'o//'das pa'i dus na sngon byung ba//bdag nyid chen po brtag pa yi//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung rab la//
11a5(rgyud): 'gro ba'i tshul ni sngon byung ba//de tshe sems can thams cad ni//shin tu khro rtum gdug par 'gyur//shin tu gdug cing khro ba yis//'o dod 'bod pa ltung bar 'gyur//de nas 'phos nas sems can de 
11a6(rgyud): bag chags kyis ni mtshams sbyor ba'i//shin tu gdug pa chen por 'gyur//blo ni bdud kyis byin brlabs na//rims dang dug dang dbyig dug nad//phra men ma dag gnod byed gdon//bdud dang rnam par log 'dren nyid//shin 
11a7(rgyud): du gdug pa chen por 'gyur//gnas gsum po ni 'jigs byed cing //bsgrub pa po ni kun la'ang za//lha la sogs pa'ang 'pho bar byed//bstan pa dag kyang 'jig par byed//de nas dbang por bcas pa'i lha//tshangs pa'i drung du 
11a8(rgyud): nye bar dong tshangs pa 'jug pa sel dbang chen yang //rdo rje can la skyabs su dong //de nas bcom ldan rdo rje can//rdo rje sems dpa'i sbyor ba yis//rdo rje se gol phyag rgya ni//sangs rgyas thams cad bsdu bar mdzad//rdo rje 
11b1(rgyud): sems dpa'i sbyor ba yi//dpung pa gnyis ni rdo rjer bcings//rdo rje se gol gdabs pa yis//sangs rgyas thams cad bsnyung mdzad pa'i//de'i bka' tsam gyis skrag nas//'khor dang 'khor lo'i tshogs bcas pa//
11b2(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad gshegs gyur pa//gzhan lta smos nas ci zhig dgos//rdo rje se gol 'og pa yis//'jig rten gsum ni ma lus par//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin bcas pa//kun gyis til 'bru bzhin du rgyas//
11b3(rgyud): shin tu gdug pa'i gtum po la//zhi bas phan par mi 'gyur te//shes rab thabs kyi ngo bo yi//khro bor de bzhin gshegs kun mdzad//khro bor tshul can khros pas kyang //'jig rten gsum ni sreg byed na//sangs rgyas thams 
11b4(rgyud): cad khros pa yis//khams gsum ma lus smos ci dgos//de bas sna'i brtse mo la//yid ni brtan par yang dag bzung //sna'i dbugs ni dro ba las//rgyal bas phyag rgya'i sbyor ba phyung //bcom ldan de nyid rnal 
11b5(rgyud): 'byor pa//rdo rje khro bor gyur nas phyung //dpal rdo rje chen po he ru ka//'bar bas thal par rab tu 'jig/sems dpa' sngon po 'bar ba'i ngang //'od kyi dkyil 'khor dmar por 'bar//'jigs pa chen po'i 
11b6(rgyud): dus ltar drag/dur khrod kyi ni me dang 'dra//'bar ba chen po skra 'bar ba//thod pa mang po'i dbu brgyan can//'jigs pa chen po'i dus ltar drag/dur khrod bzhin du rab 'jigs pa//khro la sogs pa'i tshul ldan pa//zhal rnams 
11b7(rgyud): sna tshogs 'gyur ba dang //smin ma phro nyer bcas par 'gyur//'bar ba'i lta bas gar bcas pas//gu lang dbang chen nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshangs par bcas//gnas gsum dag ni 'bar byas na//thal ba bzhin du 
11b8(rgyud): rab tu 'jig/rdo rje rdo rje khwa traM kha//dril bu sgra ni bsil ba yis//thams cad phab nas slar yang ni//thal bar 'gyur ba'ang mtsho bar byed//de'i 'bar ba'i nyi ma las//'byung po'i grib ma mi bzad pa//bskyed nas ma lus bdud 
12a1(rgyud): @#//la sogs/bdug pa rnams kyi khrag za nus//rgyal po de ni de dag dang //lhan cig dur khrod rnam par rol//thams cad rdo rje mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro'i dkyil 'khor ba yis//shin tu gdug 
12a2(rgyud): cing khro drag pa'i//sems can khams ni rnam sbyangs nas//dag pa'i sangs rgyas zhing rnams su//sangs rgyas byang chub dag la 'god//rdo rje 'dod khro la sogs kyi//rtog pa med pa thams cad kyi//nam 
12a3(rgyud): mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'i//sems can rnams ni rnam par sbyang //dpal he ru kas dbyugs phyung ba'o//'das pa'i dus na sngon 'byung ba//bdag nyid chen po rtag pa yi//de bzhin gshegs 
12a4(rgyud): pa'i gsung rab la//'gro ba'i tshul ni sngon 'byung ba//de tshe sems can thams cad ni//log par lta la gnas par 'gyur////shin tu lta ba ngan pa yis//'o dod 'bod pa chen por 'byung //
12a5(rgyud): rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//byang chub sems ni rnam sbyor ba'i//pad mo chags par sbyor ba'i//rdo rje chos su gnas par 'gyur//ji ltar pad mo dmar po 'di//'dam gyi nyes pas mi gos pa//
12a6(rgyud): de bzhin bag chags khams gsum gyi//nyes pa sna tshogs rnam par dag//'dis ni kun du gzigs pas na//rgyal ba'ang dbang phyug nyid du 'gyur//dpal ldan spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang //rdo rje sems dpa' 
12a7(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs//'dod chags thams cad dag pas na//mchod sbyin dam pas bcom ldan 'das//'dul ba'i thabs ni sna tshogs kyis//sna tshogs pa'i 'dul ba spro//kun du spyan ras gzigs dbang 
12a8(rgyud): rgyal//pad mo gar gyi dbang phyug gyur//pad mo'i sgyu ma mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog des//pad mo gar dbang la sogs kyi//rtog pa bla med thams cad kyi//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'i 
12b1(rgyud): sems can khams ni rnam par sbyar//dpal pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug gis dbugs phyung ba'o//'das pa'i dus na sngon 'byung ba//bdag nyid chen po rtag pa yi//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung rab la//'gro ba'i
12b2(rgyud): tshul ni sngon 'byung ba//de tshe sems can thams cad ni//shin tu bdug cing bkam par 'gyur//shin tu 'jungs par 'gyur ba yis//yi dgas rnams dang dmyal bar ltar//de nas 'phos nas slar yang ni//
12b3(rgyud): shin tu dbul por skye bar 'gyur//bkres dang skom pa'i skyon rnams kyis//de dag phan tshun bsod par byed//shin tu bdug pa sbyang ba'i phyir//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//dam pa'i don ni nor chen
12b4(rgyud): po//rdo rje nyi ma shar bar 'gyur//nam mkha' snying po thams cad phyogs//kun gyi re ba yongs rdzogs mdzad//bud med la sogs rin chen mchog//mkha' las rin chen thams cad 'bebs//shin tu gdug cing 
12b5(rgyud): khro ba la//zhi bas phan par mi 'gyur te//shes rab thabs kyi nyi ma la//rab tu gdung ba kun bsgrubs pas//bya ba dang ni zlog pa dang //dbrog pas kyang ni khyad par du//rnam par byas na gnas gsuM gyi
12b6(rgyud): nor gyi tshogs kun bsdud par byed//'jig rten gsum rgyal la sogs kyi//rtog pa bla med thams cad kyis//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'i//sems can khams ni rab tu dkrol//dpal
12b7(rgyud): rdo rje nyi ma dbugs dbyung ba'o//'das pa'i dus na sngon 'byung ba//bdag nyid chen po rtag pa'i//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung rab la//'gro ba'i tshul du sngon byung ba//de tshe sems can thams cad ni//brtson
12b8(rgyud): 'grus shin tu chung bar 'gyur//shin tu brtson 'grus chung ba yis//mnar med pa'i dmyal bar ltung //rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//drag shul sbyor ba'i tshul
13a1(rgyud): dag du rta rta mchog dpal ni 'byung bar 'gyur//thams cad dag par mos pa yis//sdig med stobs kyis ngo 'thon du//pha rol gnon pa'i stsal dag gis//shin tu gdug cing khro gtum pa'i//sems can rnams 
13a2(rgyud): la mang po'i khams//'jig rten thams cad rab tu 'jig//byi ba chom po rkun po yis//thams cad rnam par sbyor bar byed//drid pa dang ni sgyu dag dang //ngo 'thon stobs kyis de bzhin du//dgra cha lnga yis brgal nas ni//
13a3(rgyud): de tshe 'jig rten kun las rgyal//skye 'gro gnas dang 'gro ba rnams//mtha' dag btul nas 'grub par 'gyur//bdul ba'i thabs rnams sna tshogs kyis//rang gi rgyal srid rtag tu skyong //g.yem pa rnams las g.yem par 'gyur//
13a4(rgyud): khro ba rnams las khro ba'i mchog//zhi ba rnams las zhi ba'i mchog//drag shul can la drag po gnon//sdig med stobs kyis nga 'thon du////dbang phyug dam par rab gnan nas//lha mo b+him phrogs nas ni//de tshe longs 
13a5(rgyud): spyod rang gis spyod//sdig med stobs kyis ngo 'thon du//mthu bo che ni rab mnan nas//rug mi yang ni phrogs nas ni//de tshe langs spyod rang gis spyod////sdig med stobs kyis ngo'o 'thon du//skye dgu bdag po rab gnas nas//
13a6(rgyud): lha mo rab tu zhi ba phrogs//de tshe longs spyod rang gir spyod//sdig med stobs gyis ngo'o 'thon du//'dod pa'i lha ni rab mnan nas//dga' sdug me'i dbang phyug ma//phrogs nas de tshe nye bar spyod//de la sogs pa mtha' yas mchog/
13a7(rgyud): chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas par//rdo rje las mchog rnam gzhan pa//mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu gnas//thabs dang stsal gyis bsgrub pa yis//
13a8(rgyud): drag shul sbyor bas bsgrubs nas ni//khams gsum rgyal srid ma lus pa//rdo rje 'dzin pa des 'thob nas//byang chub sems ni mtha' yas mchog//drag shul sbyor bas bsgrub par byed//thams cad dag par mos pa yis//sdig med 
13b1(rgyud): stobs kyis ngo 'thon du//sal gyis pha rol gnon pa yis//sangs rgyas byang chub rab tu 'brel//lha'i dngos grub dka' ba dang//sangs rgyas byang chub bde chen po//go 'phang mchog ni rtag par dag//rang 
13b2(rgyud): gi rnal 'byor gyis thob nas//sems can khams ni du ma dag//ma lus pa ni rgyas 'debs shing//mchi med byang chub dka' ba bdag//sems can rnams la rtan par sbyin//rta mchog rnam par sprul pa 
13b3(rgyud): yis;ma lus lus pa med pa yis//bskal pa kun du srid par gnas//sems can kun gyi don yang byed//de ltar rta mchog dpal la sogs//rtog pa bla med thams cad kyis//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi
13b4(rgyud): mthas glas pa'i//sems can khams rnams rab tu bsgrub//dpal rta mchog gi dbyugs dbyung ba'o//la sogs ma mtha' yas mchog//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//
13b5(rgyud): nam mkha'i dbyings kyis mthas glas pa'i//rang 'byung rnams kyi gtam gyis brtags//nam mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu gnas//grub pa'i dbang phyug rab grub pas
13b6(rgyud): thams cad bdag nyid 'jug par byed//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las//rtam gyi rta phyag rgya zhes bya ba'i rtog pa ste//lnga pa'o//de nas gzhan yang 
13b7(rgyud): thams cad du//rnam pa sna tshogs mchog 'gyur ba'i//sangs rgyas thams cadamanyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu la bde ba'i mchog bshad//dpal ldan sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos//rdo rje sems dpa' bde 'byung ba//gsang ba
13b8(rgyud): mchog gi dges pa na//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs//sangs rgyas kun gyi dam tshig dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' snang ba bde//de bzhin gshegs pa'i lhar rjes mchog//rin chen rgyal la sogs pas spras//
14a1(rgyud): @#//dril bu rgyal mtshan mchog ldan pa'i//bla re dam pa bres pas na//rdo rje tshig dang mchod pas sogs//glu dang sil snyan rnam par 'phrul//me tog bdug pa'i sbyor ba dang //
14a2(rgyud): mer me dril la sogs ldan par//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog bsgrub//yang na bdag gi gnas dag gam//me tog bskyed mos tshal la sogs par bsgrub//
14a3(rgyud): de ni dang por gnas bshams te//'jam zhing reg na bde 'gyur ba//sna tshogs pad mo ras kyis g.yogs//de ni thams cad dag pa'i rten//sangs rgyas thams cad nyam sbyor ba//sbyor ba'i dbang phyug
14a4(rgyud): rnam 'phrul bas//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyi lha//de nas rnam par 'phrul par mdzad//stan de dag ni thams cad la//ji ltar gnas pa bzhin du dgod//grub bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs 
14a5(rgyud): bzhis mdzes par byas//rdo rje rin chen pad mo sogs//sgo bsrungs dang ni ldan par bya//rang gi lha dang 'dra ba yi//bud med dag ni legs sgrub pa//bskal bzangs rang gi phyag rgya mtshan//
14a6(rgyud): tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor rtag par bya//mi'i tshal ni grub pa la//dmar chen po zhes bya bar bshad//ngo bo nyid kyi mthu dag dang //rang bzhin gyis ni de 'grub pa'o//rdo rje 'chu dang rdo rjer bcas//'tshal chen po 
14a7(rgyud): dag bsgrubs pa ni//mthu yis rab tu grub pa ste//pad mo snod kyi nang du gzhag//chags pa chen po bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas kun gyi rdo rje'i thugs//dam tshig 'di 
14a8(rgyud): ni 'da' bar dka'//de'i 'tshal dang khu ba dang //'tshal chen po ni bsgrubs pa dag//chags pa chen po las byung bas//dmar chen po zhes bya rab bshad//dmar chen po dgag pur bcas//tsan dan dmar 
14b1(rgyud): po sbyar ba dag//chos kyi dbus su gzhag pa ni//ra sa ya na kun slang ba//rang gi lha ni sbyor ldan pas//srin lag dang ni 'the po'i rtses//zhi ba'i btung ba'i bzhin sbyangs nas//rtag pa yi ni dngos grub thob
14b2(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid kyi//ra sa ya na bde ba yis//rdo rje sems dpa'i tshe dang bzhon//nad med pa dang bde mchog 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba'i//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog gis/
14b3(rgyud): 'dus pa der ni bzhugs pa dang//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng//kun du kun nas thams cad ni//bdag nyid rtag tu rnam pa kun//sangs rgyas la sogs rtan g.yo kun//kun gyi dngos par 'di 'gyur ro//
14b4(rgyud): 'dis ni bsrung ba byas pas dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' bzhin du 'gyur//rnal 'byor mkha' 'gro bde mchog gis//sangs rgyas thams cad bdag nyid 'grub//de nas khrus byas dri zhim lus
14b5(rgyud): ci 'byor pa'i gos dang brgyan//sna tshogs gos dag bgos nas ni//me tog phreng brgyan kha dri zhim//sna tshogs kun gyi bskal bzangs pos//slong bas dal gyis dal gyis su//gos dgab pa 
14b6(rgyud): yis sbyor ba yis//bdag gi 'byung ba skul bar bya//rdzogs par sangs rgyas nyi zla rnams//shar ba dag ni mthong ba yis//sdig pa kun las grol 'gyur zhing//bsod nams thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//des
14b7(rgyud): pa'i lam dang kun ldan pa//rnam par 'das pa'i sa rnam par brgyan//rtan zhing grub pa slong byed pa//kun nas slong ba'i bde ba ni//stong pa shin tu rnal 'byor yod med mthus//nam mkha' nyid du dam tshig nye bar
14b8(rgyud): gshegs te bder gshegs bde bar gshegs pa bzhes su gsol//rdo rje sems dpas bskal bzangs dpal khyod bdag//'di dag nyid myi glur blangs na//shin tu mya ngan yongs 'das par//shi ba'ang slar ni
15a1(rgyud): //slong 'gyur na//yang srid pa lta ci zhig smos//lam bzhi dang ni mnyam ldan pa//rnam par 'das pas mdzes par byas//sna tshogs 'brang ba rab tu 'grub//'jig rten gsum ni rab bsgrub pa//
15a2(rgyud): 'jig rten gsum gyi phyag byas pas//'jig rten gsum gyi ston pa po//'jig rten gsum la skyid sbyin pa//'jig rten gsum po bdag la bsgrubs//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal 'byor pa//
15a3(rgyud): lha mo kun dang 'grogs pa yis//sna tshogs kun dang bskal bzangs po//a ra li ni kun 'grub 'gyur//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'i//lha mo kun dang bde mos pa/
15a4(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag rdzogs//nyi ma zla ba gsal bar 'char//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' yang dang sbyor//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog 'byung //lan cig dmigs
15a5(rgyud): pa tsam gyis kyang //bde ba'i dngos grub kun 'byung ba//rtan zhing grub pa slong byed pa//sna tshogs 'brang bas de bzhin du//gan d+har ba dang sha da pa'i//glu blang sna tshogs blus bsgrub po//
15a6(rgyud): de nas de nyid dam pa yis//thar pa thams cad rmongs pa yis//rdo rje sems dpa'i bdag nyid la//glu'i yis ni bskul bar bya//rjes su chags pa'i rnam mang dga' ba'i//pad mo rnam par
15a7(rgyud): dag pa'i mchod pas gsal bar rjes su mchod byas pa//mchog tu rtag pa'i rnal 'byor bde bas bde ba 'grub par mdzod//kye ma mchog tu bde ba'i dam tshig ngo bo nyid//mchog tu bde ba
15a8(rgyud): shin tu rnal 'byor bskal bzangs mchog dges pa//rang gi dam tshig skyon med rtag la rol cig rol cig ho//kye ma mchog tu gsang bdag po rang gi dam tshig yin pa'i phyir//rab tu gsang ba'i
15b1(rgyud): lha mo mchog rnams so//'gro ba'i rgyud ni rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul ba gsal ba mkhyen//dam tshig thams cad dngos po rtan g.yo kun gyi ngo bor gyur//rgyal ba kun gyi bdag nyid rnal 'byor bde ba 
15b2(rgyud): mkha' 'gro sgyu ma ho//kye ma dam pa rtag pa'i dngos grub chen po rab tu bsgrubs la ston//dza hU~M ba~M ho//e ma bde mchog bde bas drongs//rnam par sprul/hU~M snang bar mdzod//a la la la ho//
15b3(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma sna tshogs kyis//slob ma dag ni gzhug du gsol//klu'i rgyal po 'di dag ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal la gnas//kun gyi sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog
15b4(rgyud): ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//glu 'di dag ni len pa na//dkyil 'khor sna tshogs thams cad du//sangs rgyas thaMs cad mnyam 'khrul ba//ma lus lus pa med par snang//rnam pa mang
15b5(rgyud): po'i 'dod chags rgyud kyi sems//ma lus lus pa med bar 'dul mkhas pa//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi sgyu ma bde ba mchog//sgyu mas thams cad rab sprul rnam par sprul//te nA
15b6(rgyud): te te hU~M/rnam pa mang po rab tu dga' ba'i rgyud kyi sems//ma lus las pa med par 'dul mkhas pa//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi rgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//sgyu mas sprul pa rnam par sprul//hU~M hU~M hU~M/
15b7(rgyud): rnam pa mang po khro'i rgyud kyi sems can//ma lus lus pa med pa 'dul mkhas pa//de bzhani gshegs pa kun gyi sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//sgyu mas thams cad rab sprul rnam par sprul//ru lu ru lu hU~M
15b8(rgyud): b+h+yo hU~M/rnam pa mang po srog kyi rgyud kyi sems can//ma lus lus pa med par 'dul mkhasapa//de bzhin gshegs pha kun gyi sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//sgyums thams cad rab sprul ram par sprul//hU~M sha
16a1(rgyud): @#//Sh+ya bi sha hU~M/de la sogs pa'i mtha' yas mchog//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis mtha' klas pa//rang 'byung rnams kyi klu'i rgya//nam mkha' 
16a2(rgyud): kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems dpal la ni gnas//grub pa'i dbang phyug rab grub pas//thams cad bdag nyid 'grub par 'gyur//glu 'di dag ni blangs pa dang //lha mo kun gyis rab skul 
16a3(rgyud): nas//nam mkha' sgyu ma bde mchog gis//sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid 'grub//dbang po kun las 'byung ba yi//mchod pa'i sprin chen rnam bkod pa//rab 'byam tshogs rnams thams cad kyis//kun gyis 
16a4(rgyud): bdag nyid mchod par byed//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog gis//dngos po kun gyi bdag nyid lus//ma lus lus pa med 'grub//ston pa'i phyag 
16a5(rgyud): rgya phab pa yis//bud med dag kyang bsgrubs nas ni//rdo rjer bcas pa'i mtshan mar 'dzin//rnal 'byor mar ni rab tu 'gyur//de dag rang gi lha rnams dang //'dra bar byin gyis rlabs 
16a6(rgyud): bsgrubs na//sngon med rol mo'i sil snyan gar//rnam par sprul pas 'grub par 'gyur//lha'i gzugs dang sgra dang dri//ro dang reg pa bde mos pa//bdag nyid rang gi lhar mthong zhing //mchod pa dag kyang 
16a7(rgyud): byed par 'gyur//lha'i mchod pa mnyam sbyor ba//bskal bzangs mchog gi 'dod chags can//rnam pa kun du skal bzang po//bde ba yang dag bsgrub par byed//sangs rgyas sgyu ma byin rlabs pas//rdo rje sems 
16a8(rgyud): dpa' mnyam sbyor ba'i//dngos grub kun gyi bde ba rnams//dkyil 'khor mthong dka' myong bar 'gyur//rdo rje sems dpa' sbyor ba yi//mthu nyid kyis ni thams cad du//sna tshogs kyis bskal bzangs po//
16b1(rgyud): rang gi bde ba nyid kyis 'grub//sangs rgyas kun gyi gzugs chen dang//dbyangs dang dri dang ro bde bas//rdo rje sems dpa' bdag nyid kyi//de yis rab mthong mchod pa byed//mchod pa thams cad mnyam
16b2(rgyud): sbyor ba|skal bzangs mchog gi 'dod chags can//sangs rgyas thams cad bdag nyid kyi//dngos grub ma lus lus med 'grub//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma'i bde mchog g+si
16b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid gar//rnam par rol pa mchog tu snang//de nas yang dag rang gsang bas//bud med dag ni bzhi rnams dang//gar gyi phyag rgya mchod pa yis//mchod pa thams cad dbul
16b4(rgyud): bar bya|rab tu dga' ba'i bdag la phyag 'tshal lo//de bzhin gshegs pa dam pa grub par mdzod//oM A hU~M de bzhin gshegs pa dam par grub par mdzod//o~M a hU~M dam tshig 
16b5(rgyud): dam pa grub par mdzad du gsol//mchod pa chen po thams cad dag par mdzod//oM a hU~M|mchod pa chen po thams can grub par mdzod//oMa hU~M/klu'i phyag rgya 'di ni dpal//rdo rje sems
16b6(rgyud): dpa' la gnas te//mchod pa ma lus lus med pa'i//rab 'byam las la rab tu rung//gang dag grub par ma gyur dang//mchod la dag ni mi 'byor ba//phyag rgya 'di yis mchod byas na//mchod pa 
16b7(rgyud): thams cad myur du 'grub//mchod pa'i sbyor ba 'di dag dang//lha mo bzhi po dag gis ni//me tog lasogs mchod byas nas//slob ma thams cad gzung bar bya//dngos grub 'dod pa ci 'dod par//snyim pa'i
16b8(rgyud): gar ni kun skyong nas//de dag gdong ni mdzes pa yi//ras kyis g.yogs nas gzung bar bya//dngos gru ji ltar thob 'dod par//slob ma me tog dor du bzhug//lha mo gang la lhung ba ni//lha de de la 'grub
17a1(rgyud): @#//par 'gyur;;de nas gdong ni dkrol nas kyang//yon gyi mchog ni blang bar bya //sangs rgyas rin chen dbang skur bas//ji ltar bar ni dbyang skur ro//dmar chen po dang ga bur bcas//tsan dan
17a2(rgyud): dmar po sbyar ba rnams//tshogs kyi dbus su gzhag pa la//g.yon pa mtshan 'dzin rdo rjer bcas//rang gi lha ni rnal 'byor ldan//srin lag dang ni the bo'i rtses//zhi ba'i btung ba bzhin myangs na//
17a3(rgyud): rtag pa'i dngos grub thob par 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba'i//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma'i tshogs kyis kyang//rgyas mi thebs mi rmongs te//de la gnod par mi 'gyur ro//de ni sa gna' ni
17a4(rgyud): sgegs byas nas//sangs rgyas thams cad 'thun pa yis//mchog tu rtag pa'i bstsal pa dang//dam tshig sdom pa sbyin par bya//sangs rgyas thams cad la mnyam sbyor zhing//sdom
17a5(rgyud): pa nyams por khyed byed pa//ji snyed sangs rgyas thams cad dang/byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje 'dzin dang//lha dag dang ni mi rnams dang//de bzhin rnal 'byor 'grub pa po//de dag la ni snying
17a6(rgyud): rings nas//snying la khrag chen 'thung ba zhig//sems can khams ni ma lus don//srid pa shin tu dag bya'i phyir//mi 'gog chos nyid sbyar ba yis//rtag pa nyid du sgrub par bya//bdag gi ngo bo yang spangs
17a7(rgyud): shing;dka' thub kyis ni gzir mi bya//ci bde dag ni bde bar spyad//'di ni phyi dus sangs rgyas yin//rnal 'byor longs spyod thams cad la//cis kyang mi 'jigs dga' bar gyis//sdig pa med kyis khyed ma 'jig+sa
17a8(rgyud): dam tshig 'da' bar dka' ba'o//grub pa cig pu gcig la spyod//lhan cig spyod dang rjes spyod sdug//sangs rgyas byang chub bla na med//yang dag nyid kyis rtag tu sten//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//
17b1(rgyud): de skad bka' bstsal dpal ldan gyis//yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dang //rdo rje can gyis bka' bstsal lo//rnal 'byor bcas dang gsang bcas pa'i//bdag gis ye shes rgyas sgrubs nas//
17b2(rgyud): ci lta'i tshul dang gar byed pas//slob ma rnams la bde ba sbyin//slob ma dam pa de phyin bcad//phyag rgya'i ye shes rjes bzung bas//rnam par snang mdzad mchog bzhin du//sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag 
17b3(rgyud): nyid 'grub//de nas ro dang zas dang gnas//glu dang sil snyan la sogs pa//de dag mgon po rnams la dbul//rdo rje bla ma chen po dang //bdag dang gzhan la 'dod dgus mchod//de nas bdag nyid rab sgrubs
17b4(rgyud): nas;kun gyi slob dpon rab gnas pa//ji lta'i tshul gyis 'jug pa yis//bden pa'i chos ni brjod par bya//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyi tshul//rtog pa thams cad rab 'grub cing //rtog pa kun la rab sbyor ba'i//
17b5(rgyud): ngo bo nyid phyir chen po'i mchog//sna tshogs las kyi sbyor ba dang //'dul ba'i bdag nyid sna tshogs pas//sems can rnams ni 'dul ba'i phyir//de las gzhan pa'i tshul du bstan//rnal 'byor de nyid 
17b6(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//thar pa thams cad rnam sbyang phyir//grol ba'i mkha' 'gro byung bar 'gyur//rang gi lha dang 'dra ba yi//rang gi byin brlabs bsgrub pa'i rgya//phyag 
17b7(rgyud): rgya bcing bzhin rjes 'thun pa'i//sangs rgyas sprul pa bsgrub par bya//gzhan yang rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das//dam tshig la sdang rnam sbyang phyir//thams cad rnal 'byor dbang phyug mchog//rdo rje mkha' 'gro
17b8(rgyud): 'byung bar 'gyur//gzhan yang shin du rnal 'byor pa//ma lus pad mo rnam sbyang phyir//bskal bzang dam pa mi 'gyur ba//pad mo sgyu ma byung bar 'gyur//gzhan yang rnal 'byor bcom ldana'adas//nor gyi mchog
18a1(rgyud): /ni rnam sbyang phyir //nam mkha'i kun gyi bdag pa che//rdo rje nyi ma dpal 'byung 'gyur//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//drag shul sbyor ba sbyang ba'i phyir//rta 'grin pha rol rab gnon
18a2(rgyud): pa|rta mchog rab tu byung bar 'gyur//rang gi lha dang 'dra ba yi//rang gi byin rlabs sgrub pa'i phyir//gzugs brnyan ngo bor sbyor ba'o//phyag rgya'i sbyor bas gar bya'o//dam tshig rnam pa sna tshogs kyis
18a3(rgyud): de ltar rnam par spyad na ni//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog 'grub//kun du kun nas thams cad ni//sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul thams cad kyis//bdag nyid rtag
18a4(rgyud): tu rnam pa kun//sangs rgyas la sogs rtan g.yo kun//kun gyi //dngos por de 'gyur ro//kun du sangs rgyas thams cad ni//sangs rgas 'phrul pa thams cad kyis//bdag gnyid rnam par 'phrul
18a5(rgyud): pa ston|lha mo kun dang 'dus pa yis//sangs rgyas zhing ni ma lus par//sangs rgyas sprul pa grub par byed//sangs rgyas sprul pa sgyu ma yis//sangs rgyas rnams kyi mdzad pa ston//
18a6(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad mnyamasbyor ba'i//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma gar byed pas//khams gsum dag ni ma lus par//dngos po kun du de 'gyur ro//pad mo sgyu ma rnam 'phrul pas//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin lasog+sa
18a7(rgyud): sna tshogs gzugs byed rim pa yis//'gro ba thams cad mgu bar byed//khams gsum pa'i rgyal srid che//'jal sdums g.yul 'gyed 'grub pa dag//rgyal srid sgyu ma rnam sprul bas//bya ba thams
18a8(rgyud): cad ston par byed//khams gsum pa'irgyal srid che//bdag brtul phod pas mnan nas ni//'gro ba thams cad rab 'joms pas//rta'i gar gyis ston par byed//de la sogs pa mtha' yas
18b1(rgyud): mchog chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis mthas klas pa'i//sna tshogs kun gyi bde mchog gis//nam mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin
18b2(rgyud): gshegs dpal dges rol pa rnam 'phrul ba//sna tshogs kun gyi rnam par rol//rnam pa sna tshogs skal bzangs po//rnam pa sna tshogs bde mchog gis//'dul ba rnam pa sna tshogs kyis//sems can
18b3(rgyud): khams ni rab tu sgrub//de la sogs pa mtha' yas mchog//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi nyam pa//nam mkha'i dbying kyis mthas klas pa'i//sangs rgyas thams cad 'byung ba'i mchog//nam mkha'
18b4(rgyud): gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rja sems dpa' la gnas te//grub pa'i dbang phyug rab grub pas//thams cad bdag nyid grub par byed//bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpa' de skad ces gsungs
18b5(rgyud): so||sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma/bde mchog las//sangs rgyas thams cadamanyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gros sgyu ma'i dkyil 'khor rtog pa ste//drug pa'o////
18b6(rgyud): de nas gzhan yang bshad par bya//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyi lha rnam pa sna tshogs kun gyi mchog//'khor lo thams cad rnam sprul pa'o//gsang ba mchog gi dges pa ni//thams cad bdag nyid
18b7(rgyud): rjes su sgrub//dben pa yi ni gnas dag gam//rang gi skyed mos tshal sogs par//kun du sna tshogs bskal bzangs po//kun du sna tshogs bde mchog gis//kun du sna tshogs dgos pa rnams//ci bder bar ni
18b8(rgyud): sgrub par byed//rten dang dgon pa gtsug lag khang //ri'i rtse nas thams cad du//rnam pa du ma sna tshogs pa'i//sangs rgyas dgongs pa bsgrub par bya//dur khrod dang ni lha rten dang //ma mo'i gnas la sogs
19a1(rgyud): @#//'dug na/dpal rdo rje 'dzin pa'i dgos pa dag//rnam pa du mar grub par 'gyur//pad mo'i skye ba'i tshal dga' dang//chu bo dag ni mdo dag du//dpal pad mo 'dzin pa'i dgos pa rnams//yang 
19a2(rgyud): dag par ni grub par 'gyur//rgyal po'i pho brang dga' ba dang//rgyal po'i skyed mos tshal dag tu//rnam pa sna tshogs du ma'i//rgyal po'i dgos pa sgrub par bya//'khor ni rnam pa sna tshogs dang//de bzhin
19a3(rgyud): chu bo'i 'gram dag gam//rgyal khams gzhan las grub par du//rdo rje rta ni bsgrub par bya//cha lugs rnam pa sna tshogs dang//sgrub pa rnam pa sna tshogs kyis//dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i dgos pa la//
19a4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor la sogs rab tu sgrub//sangs rgyas rnams kyi pad mo dmar//phyag na rdo rje sna tshogs pa//'jig/rten dbang gi kha dog dkar//rdo rje nyi ma dpal gyi gser//kha dog kun dang 
19a5(rgyud): ci 'byor pa//bla re rgyal mtshan rgyan gyi rnams//thams cad rab tu bsgrub pa dpal//rta mchog rdo rje can gyi'o//gnas da nga 'khor rnams ci 'dra dang//bkod dang 'gro ba ci 'dra dang//'khor rnaMs
19a6(rgyud): bkod pa ci 'dra bar//de 'dra'i 'khor lo mchog gis mdzad//rdo rje sems dpa' lha rnams kyi//'khor lo mngon sum mthong ba las//bsod nams rtag pa ci 'dra ba//phyis dris pa yang de bzhin no//
19a7(rgyud): dkyil 'khor thig btab shes rab can//phyogs kun mnyam nyid sgrub cing//shar nub byang gi phyogs dag tu//thig bzhi btab pa'i dkyil 'khor brgyad//dkyil 'khor yang dag ji lta bar//rab tu 
19a8(rgyud): rtag pa su zhig nus//rang gi lha'i rang bzhin gyis//dkyil 'khor rtag pa 'grub pa par 'gyur//bdag nyid rang gi lhar sbyor bas//dngos grub thams cad bsgrub par bya//cho ga lhag gam ma tshang nang
19b1(rgyud): nyes pa med par 'grub par 'gyur//kun du sna tshogs bskal bzang po//kun du sna tshogs 'khor lo can//kun du sna tshogs 'grub pa'i phyir//kun du dkyil 'khor kun du sna tshogs pa'o//dkyil 
19b2(rgyud): 'khor zlum po sangs rgyas kyi//rdo rje can gyi zla gam bzhin//pad ma dbang gi pad mo dbyibs//rin chen mchog gi rin chen dbyibs//ji ltar 'dod pa ci 'byor pa//ji lta'i dus dang ci bde bar//
19b3(rgyud): dkyil 'khor kun du gnas par dpal//rta mchog phyag rgya rdo rje yi'o//dkyil 'khor du ni ma zhugs shing //dmi tshag rnams ni spang ba dag//gsang ba yang dag mi shes pas//sgrub pa ci yang mi 'grub 
19b4(rgyud): po;srog chags bsad par mi bya zhing //dkon mchog gsum ni yong mi spong //byang chub sems dang mi btang ste//slob dpon skal bzang yang mi btang //kun du sna tshogs bskalabazang po;;
19b5(rgyud): bdag rang sgrubs pas bskal pa bzang //bdag med chos nyid dang ldan pa//dam tshig la sdang skal ba ngan//srid pa shin du bsgrubs pas na//bskal bzangs sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
19b6(rgyud): skal ngan nyan thos 'jig rten pa//shin tu yongs su mya ngan 'das;;rtog pa thams cad sgrub pa'i phyir//skal bzang phyag na rdo rje dpal//stong pa nyid la rab sbyor bas//rang rgyal rnams ni skal
19b7(rgyud): ba ngan;'dod chags thams cad rnam dag pas//skal bzangs spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang//log par lta dang ldan pas na//skal ngan mu stegs 'jig rten na//dam pa'i nor mchog sdud pa'i phyir//
19b8(rgyud): skal bzangs rdo rje nyi ma'i dpal//bdag gi bu la'ang ma chags pas//skal ngan dka' thub spyod pa kun//rgyal srid mchog rnams kun sdus pas//rta mchog dpal ni bskal pa bzang //bdagamed
20a1(rgyud): @#//yongs su 'dzin med pas//thub pas thams cad bskal pa ngan//bde ba thams cad bdag sgrubs pa'i//kun gyi slob dpon skal pa bzang //lta ngan dam tshig la sdang ba'i//slob 
20a2(rgyud): ma thams cad skal ba ngan//gsang ba'i don ni yang dag nyid//'dir ni gsal bar shes pa yis//sangs rgyas kun gyi bka' bzhin du//sangs rgyas kun du 'grub par 'gyur//kun gyi slob 
20a3(rgyud): dpon la gnas pas//'khor lo 'jug par byed pa na//dkyil 'khor 'dod pa'i slob ma la//gsang ba yang dag mtha' yas mchog//thob pa nyid du gsol brjod pa//kye'i khyod kyi su la dga' zhes 
20a4(rgyud): dri|skal ba bzang la bdag dga' zhes//'jug pa bas kyang smra bar bya//gsang ba'i yan lag 'di shes na//ji ltar 'dod pa rab tu bzhug//gsang ba yang dag mi shes na//g.yas 
20a5(rgyud): pa yis ni bzhug par bya//de ltar dkyil 'khor du bzhugs nas//de bzhin du ni yon phul te//gsang ba'i cho ga nyid ces pas//mi 'gyur ba'i mchog 'grub po//kun du sna tshogs bla ma la 
20a6(rgyud): yon mchog dag ni dbul bar nges//gang la yongs su btang dka' gang //de ni mchog du bshad pa yin//gang dang gang gi sdug pa'i mchog//yang na shin du khyad par can//de dang de ni bla ma la//de nyid mi zad 
20a7(rgyud): 'dod pas 'bul//yang na de ni 'dod 'gyur pa 'am//'on te sems la dgos pa dag//de sbyin yang na dgos par bya//de ni chung yang mchog yin no//de ni gal te mchog bdog la//de yang ngan pa'i yon 
20a8(rgyud): dbul na|kun gyi slob dpon brnyas pa na//de nyid rtag tu sdug bsngal 'thob//bu mo shin tu brgyan pa 'am//de bzhin chung ma sdug pa dag//phul nas rin gyis blu ba ni//sangs rgyas byang chub mchog gi 
20b1(rgyud): yin;mi chen bdag gis phul nas su//slar yang rin gyis blu ba ni//rdo rje 'dzin dpal sbyor ba'i//yon da mchog gi yon yin no//mu tig bzang dang nor bu dang//shin du dkar ba'i ras zung dang
20b2(rgyud): de bzhin rta ni dkar po dag//pad mo can gyi g.yon mchog yin//rgyal srid longs spyod gter rnams dang//gos dang rgyan gyi tshogs rnams dang//de bzhin mchod pa thams cad ni//rdo rje nyi ma'i yon
20b3(rgyud): yin no;shin du brgyan pa'i rta dag dang//glang po shin tu rgyan pa dang//shing rta rin chen gang ba dag//rta'i rgyal po'i yon yin no//khams kyi rang bzhin thams cad dang//srog dang rtsa'i de bzhin ste//
20b4(rgyud): sangs rgyas rin chen dbang skur ba//mkhas pas rnam pa sna tshogs rtag//spyi bo dang ni pral ba dang//ldan pa gnyis dang sa ltag par ru//sangs rgyas rin chen thams cad kyi//dbu brgyan mi
20b5(rgyud): 'gyur dam pa'o//khams kyi rang bzhin thabs cad dang//srog dang rtsi ba'i de bzhin de//thod pa rin chen dbu rgyan nam//sangs rgyas gtsug gi nor bu dag//kun du sna tshogs bla mar ni//
20b6(rgyud): kun du slob dpon kun la bya//rgyal srid thams cad rnam dag la//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//rin chen ngo mtshang gcig las byas//de bzhin sna tshogs rgyan gyis spras//sangs rgyas pad mo
20b7(rgyud): dbang skur bas//slob ma thams cad dbang skur ro//sangs rgyas rin chen rdo rje phyed//rdo rje'i rigs la rdo rje yin//spyan ras gzigs kyi rdo rje sku//nor bu 'dzin gyi nor bu yin//sangs rgyas rin
20b8(rgyud): chen pad mo dang//rdo rje nor bu de bzhin du//thams cad mchog ni rta mchog la//rang gi dbang skur sdus pa'o//blugs pa 'am ni byas pa 'am//rdung ba 'am ni bris kyang rung//sna tshogs rab tu
21a1(rgyud): /legs pa yi mtshan ma'i phyag rgya rab tu rtag//kun du sna tshogs bskal bzangs po//rang 'byung rnams kyi mtshan ma'i rgya//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'i//lha mo rnams 
21a2(rgyud): kyi bde ba mchog//gzhu dang mda' dang de bzhin du//skal bzang de ni bskal pa bzang //thod pa dang ni khwa tra~M kha//rdo rje rgya gram de bzhin no//pad mo rgya gram dpag med tshe'i//sangs rgyas kun gyi tshe sgrub pa 
21a3(rgyud): lha mo tshogs rnams thams cad kyi//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya tshogs bsgrubs bzhin//mda' dang de bzhin tshangs pa'i 'go//de bzhin 'khor lo'i phyag rgya dang //rdo rje dril shul phyag rgya'i mtshan//dpag bsaM 
21a4(rgyud): shing dang lag pa dang //pad mo rta 'grin 'phang 'thob//rta 'grin ljang gu de bzhin no//phag gi 'che ba grub pa dang //zla ba nyi ma de bzhin no//rin chen sna tshogs snod chen du//sangs rgyas 
21a5(rgyud): gzugs kyi ra sa yan//thams cad bdag nyid myong skal bzang //mtshan ma'i phyag rgya sna tshogs gzhag//gling bu dang ni pi wang kun//ku ma ta dang mu ra dza//me tog gdug pa mar me dang 
21a6(rgyud): sna tshogs dri rnams de bzhin no//de la sogs te mtha' yas mchog//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'i//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya dam pa'i tshogs//
21a7(rgyud): nam mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' nyid la gnas//grub pa'i dbang phyug rab grub pas//thams cad bdag nyid grub par 'gyur//chos gos dang ni lhung zed dang //de bzhin 
21a8(rgyud): du ni mkhar sil dang //dam pa'i chos kyi glegs bam dag//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya'i tshogs gzhan no//de bzhin lce'i phyag rgya dang //cha lang dang ni rnga dag dang //rdo rje dang ni rin chen snod//mtshan 
21b1(rgyud): ma'i phyag rgyas gos gzhan no//de bzhin sangs rgyas gzugs brnyan dang//me long dang ni rlung yab dang//rdo rje ser ba'i sprin dang bcas//de bzhin mig gi phyag rgya dang//rdo rje 'bar dang kha taM
21b2(rgyud): kha|thod pa kham dang de bzhin no//dmar chen dang snying chen dang//sha chen po yang de bzhin te//rnon po 'da' dang de bzhin 'dren//chang dang chu dang de bzhin te//lcug ma rlung gis skyod pa dang//ras ding
21b3(rgyud): de bzhin me dag ni//rdo rje khro bo'i rgyal po'i dpal//gdug pa thams cad za ba yi//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya grub pa'i tshogs//rnal 'byor dngos grub rab sgrubs pa'o//pad mo sna tshogs dmar po dang//
21b4(rgyud): ljang gu de bzhin dkar po dang//mthing ka ser po leb rgan dang//pad ma'i mtshan mchog rnam pa bdun//pad ma smyung po bud med rgyu de bzhin chos kyi 'khor lo dang//gsal shing dpal skyed rdo rje
21b5(rgyud): dag|pad mo mtshan ma'i tshogs gzhan no//gzhu dang mda' dang ral gri dang//de bzhin du ni 'bar ba'i me//dbang skur gter dang rgyal mtshan dag//mchod pa'i mtshan ma sdus pa'o//de  
21b6(rgyud): bzhin sna tshogs pad mo dang//rdo rje rgya traM de bzhin te//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya 'grub pa gnyis//rgyal srid thams cad 'joms pa ste//sangs rgyas thams cad la sogs pa//dngos po brtan g.yo ma lus kun
21b7(rgyud): rang 'byung rnams ni mtshan mi rgya//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'o//de skad bcom ldan de bzhin gshegs//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyis gsungs//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam par sbyor
21b8(rgyud): ba|mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las//dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi phyag rgya shes pa'i rtog pa ste/bdun pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bshad pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dpa' gyi lha//rnam pa kun du
22a1(rgyud): @#//tshogs kyi mchog/mchod pa thams cad rnam sprul pa//lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis dag gis//'dzub mo the'u chung mdzes par sgyur//dpal ldan g.yon pa 'gying ba'i thabs//g.yas
22a2(rgyud): pa ba dan gsum par bya//sre gzhur gyur pa'i steg pa'i mchog/bdag nyid rdo rje sems dpa' dpal//'dzum dang bcas pa'i mig gis lta//bsam pa g.yos pa'i phyag rgya'o//sangs rgyas rdo rje pad mo
22a3(rgyud): 'dzin|rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum pas//skad cig gis kyang phyag byed na//khengs pa gzhan lta ci zhig smos//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshangs par bcas//btud te rab tu phyag 
22a4(rgyud): 'tshal nas ta las rnams thams cad byed par 'gyur//rdo rje dpal gyi thal mo sgyur//bsam pa g.yos pas phyag byas pa'i//sangs rgyas mchod pa legs byas nas//ma lus lus pa med
22a5(rgyud): par 'grub|smin ma g.yon ma rdeg tshul gyis//ru lu ru lur bcas dgad na//tshangs pa nye dbang drag po bcas//khams gsum pa rnams phyag byed 'gyur//thal mo pad mo kha 'bus bzhin 
22a6(rgyud): rang nyid sbyor ldan snying kar bcas//'the po 'the chung 'dab ma bzhin//rgyas pa btud pa'i phyag rgya bya//lus kyi yan lag thams cad ni//sa la snyel te phyag bya ba//snying ka dang ni spyi gtsug tu//thal mo
22a7(rgyud): sbyar te phyag bya'o//dpal ldan rdo rje 'gying ba'i thabs//'dzum pa'i mig gis phyag byas nas//khams gsum po yang phyag byed na//mthung snyems gzhan lta ci zhig smos//phyag rgya 'tshal ba'i phyag rgyar 
22a8(rgyud): shes pa'o||gar gyi rgyud rnams ji snyed pa'o//de bzhin bya ba'i tshul ston kun//de dag rdo rje sems dpa'i rgya//rang gi don rnams 'grub byed pa'o//'gying dang bcas pa'i tshul byas nas//ji ltar tshul bzhin 
22b1(rgyud): gar byed cing ;skor ba'i gar rnams sna tshogs kyis//mchod pa sna tshogs dbul bar bya//lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis dag gis//'dzub po 'the'u chung mdzes bsgyur te//g.yon pa'i 'dzub mo
22b2(rgyud): khur tshur bcas//g.yas pa ba dan gsum par bya//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//sangs rgyas kun dang 'grogs pa dang//dngos grub chen po mchog kun gyi//dbang phyug chen po 'grub par 'gyur
22b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad dang 'grogs pa//dpal bskal pa bzang po zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//snyems 'dzin chen por bcas pa yi//sbyor bas de ni bteg nas su//'gram pa'i phyogs su yongs 
22b4(rgyud): sgyur nas ;spyi bo mtshams su gzhag par bya//thams cad 'bebs pa'i gar byed pas//gar byas de nas phab nas su//skal bzang hUM zhes khyab pa'i klus//mchod pa thams cad
22b5(rgyud): dbul bar bya//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//dpal bzang po bde ba chen po zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o //lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis dag gis//'dzub mo 'the'u chung mdzes sgyur
22b6(rgyud): ba'i;rdo rje thal mo sbyar nas su//de bzhin g.yon du sgyur bar byas//de ni sbyor bas bteg nas ni//spyi 'o'i mtshams su gzhag par bya //skal bzang oM zhes bya ba'i glus//mchod pa thams cad 
22b7(rgyud): dbul bar bya//dpal rdo rje thal mo sgyur ba sangs rgyas kyis mchod pa'o//lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyas dag gis//'dzub mo'athe'u mdzes sgyur te//rdo rje thal mo gyen du grol//mchod pa'i rtor
22b8(rgyud): ma kun gyi tshul;;dur khrod cha lugs gar byed pas//phyag rgya 'di yis gar byed cing//skal bzangs rdo rje drag por 'god//mchod pa thams cad dbul bar bya//dpal rdorje he ru ka'i thal mo ste//
23a1(rgyud): @#//mchod pa'i gtor ma thams cad kyi cho ga'o//lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis dag gis//'dzub mo 'the'u chung mdzes sgyur te//rdo rje rin chen thal sbyar nas//mchod pa   
23a2(rgyud): thams cad dbul bar bya//re ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa//dpal rdo rje rin chen zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//gyen du 'thor zhing 'thur du 'bebs//skor zhing de bzhin
23a3(rgyud): nyug pa dang/me tog gdug pa'i mar me mchog//dri'i mchod pa 'byung ba'o//gang ba 'am 'on te stong pa yang rung//yang na ci 'byor ba yis kyang//dam tshig nyams par sgrubs pa yis
23a4(rgyud): mchod pa thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//phyag rgya 'di rnams bcings nas ni//sangs rgyas thams cad mchod par 'o/gyur//dngos grub dbang phyug grub pas kyang//
23a5(rgyud): kun gyi bdag nyid mchod par 'gyur//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis mthas klaspa'i//rang 'byung rnams kyi mchod pa kun//gang 
23a6(rgyud): dag de yis ma byas shing//spel bar yang ni ma gyur ba'i//rlabs chen dam pa gang yang med//sangs rgyas thams cad gnyam sbyor ba//'byor pa'i dbang phyug bde chen dang//
23a7(rgyud): dngos grub las dang bde ba dag//'di yis mi 'grub gang yang med//mchod pa rab 'byam 'grub pa rnams//ma lus lus pa med par grub ces dpal//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 
23a8(rgyud): sems dpas gsungs//sangs rgyas thams cad nyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las//mchod pa thams cad mnyam par sbyor ba//dpal skal pa bzang po'i dam
23b1(rgyud): tshig gi rtog pa ste//brgyad pa'o//de nas gzhan yang bshad pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyi lha'i //kun du sna tshogs mchog rnams kyi//phyag rgya thams cad rnam sprul pa'o//
23b2(rgyud): sems dpa' sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog//gsang ba mchog gi dges pa na//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs//bde ba kun gyi zhen pa dang//dngos 
23b3(rgyud): grub thams cad rnam dag phyir//rdo rje sems dpa' bdeba'i mchog//zla ba nya ltar rtsa dmar sku//'dod chags kun la chags pa'i phyir//chags pa chen po bcom ldan 'das//dpal rdo rje sos
23b4(rgyud): dpa' phyag rgya 'di//dmar po sna tshogs 'bar ba'i 'od//khams gsum pa dag rin chen gzhan//sangs rgyas rin chen lta bu med//rin chen sangs rgyas kun gyi che//dbu rgyan can ni de bas mchog
23b5(rgyud): gzhan gyi nor ni mi 'phrogs pas//lha'i chu yis//dag pa ste//mthu dang ngo bo nyid dag dang//rang bzhin gyis ni de grub pa'o//thod pa'i phreng ba'i dbu rgyan dang//sangs rgyas gtsug gi nor bu
23b6(rgyud): can //kun nas nyon mongs 'jig rten dbang//mi 'byed la sogs de grub pa'o//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal 'byor ba//lha mo kun dang 'dus pa yis//kun du sna tshogs bskal bzang ba//mkha'
23b7(rgyud): 'gro ma ni thams cad 'grub//sangs rgyas rdo rje pad mo 'dzin//rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum pa//sangs rgyas btab rmongs byas slangs nas ni//las rnams kun la sbyor bar byed//phyag rgya 'di yis btab
23b8(rgyud): na ni//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng//lha mo kun mchog dpal dang ldan//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub//mchog tu dga' ba rab tu sbyor//skal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje sems dpas//bde ba'i mchog
24a1(rgyud): @#//rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//g.yas dang g.yon pa'i sbyor ba yis//bde ba'i cha lugs gar byed cing //sems dpa' dkyil mo krung bcas nas//dpung rnams thams cad 
24a2(rgyud): rab tu 'joms||dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung ngo//zla ba'i kha dog 'dzum pa'i zhal//dngos grub thams cad dag pa dang //pad ma'i 'dod chags rnam dag phyir//rla'i dbus ni 
24a3(rgyud): dmar la dkar||phyag mchog lo ma sngon po 'am//de bzhin bde ba'i dbang phyug dang //bde bar reg pa'i dbang phyug pas//dkar zhing ser ba'i rla dang ldan//lha rdzas sna tshogs kha dog 
24a4(rgyud): mchog||'bar ba sna tshogs 'khrugs pa'i 'od||pad ma sna tshogs la gnas pa//thams cad dag par rab tu gnas//sangs rgyas pad mo la sogs phyag rgya mchog//rin chen sna tshogs brgyan 
24a5(rgyud): dang gos||sangs rgyas thams cad 'dus pa yis//mi 'byed la sogs rgya byin bzhin//bya ba dang ni zlog pa dang //'phrog pa dag kyang lhag par mdzad//bdag la 'khyud pa'i phyag rgya 
24a6(rgyud): yis||yan lag thams cad 'grogs par 'grub/phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng //dpal ldan 'dod pa kun la dbang //rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub//
24a7(rgyud): mchog tu dga' la rab sbyor ba//skal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje bde ba'i mchog rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rnam pa sna tshogs dam tshig chags//bde ba 'bar ba'i gar byed pas//bdag la 'khyud 
24a8(rgyud): pa'i phyag rgya mchog//dbus kyi lha mo mchog chen po'o//phyag rgya 'di yis rnal 'byor pa//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro'ong ldeng //bde chen dbang phyug dpal dang ldan//rdo rje sems dpa' rabatu 'grub//
24b1(rgyud): mchog tu dga' la rab tu sbyor ba//skal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i dam tshig bde ba'i mchog rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//se ral kha'i tshul dag tu//rdo rje kha dr-i kha bzung ste//
24b2(rgyud): bdag la 'khyud pa'i phyag rgya ni//pad bcas rdo rje rgya khram mchog//phyag rgya 'di yis btab nas ni//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng //rnal 'byor kun gyi dbang phyug rgyal//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu
24b3(rgyud): 'grub||mchog tu dga' ba la sbyor ba//bskal pa bzang po dpal rdo rji rnal 'byor bde ba'i mchog rol pa zhe bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kham por grub//bdag la 'khyud pa'i phyag 
24b4(rgyud): rgya yis||rdo rje sems dpa' dpal sbyor ba//dbus kyi lha mchog chen po'o//phyag rgya 'di yis rnal 'byor pa//'dod pa bzhin du 'gre'ong ldeng //dpal ldan tshe dang gzhon grub pa//rdo rje sems
24b5(rgyud): dpa' rab tu 'grub//mchog tu dga' ba rab tu sbyor/bskal pa bzang po dpal rdo+e rje bde ba'i mchog rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//de la sogs pa mtha' yas mchog//chos dbyings 
24b6(rgyud): mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'i//rang 'byung phyag rgyas sna tshogs rnams//nam mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la gnas te//grub pa'i dbang
24b7(rgyud): phyug rab bsgrubs pas//thams cad bdag nyid 'bar bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam par sbyor ba'i//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog gi rtsa ba'i phyag rgya'i tshogs so//gtsug lag ji ltar
24b8(rgyud): rjes 'thun par||dpal tshogs phyag rgya gar dag gis//mda' bzhi yongs su 'phangs pa ni//bdud bzhi dag las rgyal bar 'gyur//tshangs pa chen po'i mgo dpal rdo rje mda' zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o/rkang pa
25a1(rgyud): //bteg cing phab pa dang//gshibs te mchongs pa'i stabs dag gis//'khor lo phyag rgyas stod nas ni//khams gsum dag la 'chongs par 'gyur//dpal rdo rje
25a2(rgyud): mthu zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//bya ba dang zlog pa dang//phrogs pa dag gi khyad par du//sna tshogs khyad par gar byed cing//khams gsum dag ni bsdu bar bya//dpal rdo rje gar gyi
25a3(rgyud): dbang phyug zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//lcug ma gang yang rung bzung nas//bdag la 'khyud pa'i phyag rgya yis//bud med thams cad rtsed na ni//dpag bsam shing dag rab tu 'grub
25a4(rgyud): dpal rdo rje 'dod pa'i lha mo zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//myang ba rtul ba ltar byas pas//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshangs pa yi//'jig rten gsum dag
25a5(rgyud): skam par byed//dpal rdo rje rta dong zhes bya bI phyag rgya'o//sna'i myang ba'i rdub pa yis//drag po dbang po nye bang bcas/nyi zla gshin rje tshangs payi//khams gsum yang ni bsdud
25a6(rgyud): par byed/dpal rdo rje phag mo zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o //mig tshungs pa lta ba yis//drag po dbang po nyi dbang bcas//nyi zla gshine rje tshangs pa yis//'jig rten gsum yang za bar byed
25a7(rgyud): dpal rdo rje spyan nyi zla me zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//thams cad bdag nyid myang ba yi//drag po dbang po nye dab bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshags par bcas//thal bar 'gyur ba 'ang 'tsho bar byed
25a8(rgyud): dpal rdo rje kun gyi bdag nyid khams gsum slar 'tsho ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//gleng bu len pa'i tshul ston cing//ji ltar babs pa'i gar byed pas//tshangs pa nye dbang drag por
25b1(rgyud): bcas//'gro ba gsum dag dbang du byed//dpal rdo rje kleng bu'i dbang ces bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//pi wang len pa'i tshul ston cing //ji ltar babs pa'i gar byed pas//las rnams 
25b2(rgyud): 'grub par rab 'byam dang //dngos grub ma lus myur du 'grub//dpal rdo rje pi wang bde bas rmongs par byed pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//cang de'u tshul ston rnal 'byor pa//ji ltar 
25b3(rgyud): babs pa'i gar byed pas/rims dang gdon dang dug zhen pa'i//sdug bsngal thams cad myur du sel//dpal rdo rje cang de'u sdug bsngal thams cad sel ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//
25b4(rgyud): rdza rnga'i tshul ston rnal 'byor pa//ji ltar babs pa'i gar bye pas//gnas gsum dag ni ma lus pa//skad cig tsam gyis 'bebs par byed//dpal rdo rje rdza rngas myos par byed 
25b5(rgyud): pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o/phyag rgya gar gyis mchod pa yis//mchod pa thams cad byas nas ni//rang 'byung gar gyi mchod pa rnams//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//
25b6(rgyud): rims dang dug dang dbyig dug byad//phra men gdon gyi gnod pa rnams//skor ba'i gar rnams sna tshogs kyis//skad cig tsam gyis zhi bar 'gyur//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla 
25b7(rgyud): gshin rje tshangs bcas pa//rgyas btab rmongs byas zos pa dang //skad cig tsam gyis 'tsho bar byed//de la sogs pa mtha' yas mchog//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas 
25b8(rgyud): klas pa'i//rang 'byung lha mo'i phyag rgya rnams//nam mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' la gnas te//dngos grub dbang phyug rab grub pas//thams cad bdag nyid 'bab par 'gyur//
26a1(rgyud): //sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog gis//rab tu grub pa'i klu rnams kyis//bla na med pa'i dngos grub thob//de nas phyag rgya'i ye shes 'di'i
26a2(rgyud): dpal ldan rdo rje mchog glu//las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub cing//srid pa shin tu bsgrub pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa' bde chen bskal bzang dpal//tshogs bdag lags sgrubs la rtogs par
26a3(rgyud): mdzad du gsol//kye' de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi bde ba mkha' 'gro sgyu ma sna tshogs bde ba'i mchog//'gro ba'i rgyud rnams rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul ba'i mchog 'grub par bdag la grub pa
26a4(rgyud): mdzad du gsol//a la la ho//de bzhin gshegs pa sbyor ba rnam pa sna tshogs gar rnams bstan du gsol//dza hU~M ba~M ho/kye' bde mchog bde bas zung rnam par sprul hU~M snang bar mdzad du gsol//
26a5(rgyud): a la la ho/de bzhin gshegs pa kun sna tshogs mkha' 'gro sgyu mas slob du ma bzung du gsol//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/kun du kun nas
26a6(rgyud): thams cad ni 'di nyid kyis ni myur du 'grub//sangs rgyas thams cad nyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro ma'i rnal 'byor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi phyag bya'o//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de
26a7(rgyud): bzhin gshegs//thar pa thams cad rnam dag phyir//sangs rgyas phyag rgya dpal du gnas//don dam rnam par dag pa'i phyir//bcom gser gyi kha dog bzhin no//chags med la sogs chags chen phyir//dkar ni sna
26a8(rgyud): tshogs gsal ba'i mdangs//chos kyi rgyal srid rab bsgrubs pa//rin chen chos las lhag pa med//de bas theg chen byang chub sems//rgyal ba rdo rje rin chen can//g.yas pa'i 'the pong gung mo gnyis//
26b1(rgyud): g.yon pa'i khur tshur bcangs pa yis//so sor kun la reg pa ni//rtsa ba'i phyag rgya yin par grags//'di na sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//shes rab pha rol phyin gsal yin//byang chub sems chen
26b2(rgyud): skye ba dang //chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba ste//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//shes rab pha rol phyinapa'i tshul//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas par//rab tu sgrag par 'gyur ba yin//byang chub
26b3(rgyud): sems ni skyed pa dang//chos kyi 'khor lo skor pa ste//sangs rgyas nyid du rig par bya//mchog tu rtag pa'i sangs rgyas so//gang gis ldan pa 'di mthong zhing //rnam pa kun du mchog byed pa//
26b4(rgyud): de yis sangs rgyas rtag pa'i mchog//mthong zhing mchod pa byas par 'gyur//sems ni bskyed ma thag tu yang//chos kyi 'khor lo legs par skor//dpal dam tshig ces bya ba'i
26b5(rgyud): phyag rgya'o//g.yas pa'i yan lag gnon pa yis//g.yon pa'i yan lag kun las rgyal//sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas na//bdud rnams thams cad rab tu 'joms//dpal rdo
26b6(rgyud): rje'i dkyil mo dkrung ngo//rdo rje khur tshur bcangs nas su//lag pa g.yon pa bkab nas ni//sangs rgyas kun blo dngos grub rnams//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//de bzhin dpal ldan he ru
26b7(rgyud): kha'i//rdo rje khu tshur chen po dag//dpa' bo thod pa'i dngos grub rnams//ma las lus pa med par 'grub //pad mo'i bud med khu tshur ni//de bzhin g.yon pas bkab nas yang//byang chub spyod pa rab
26b8(rgyud): 'grub rnams//ma lus lus ma med par 'grub//rin chen 'dzin pa'i khu tshur kyang//lag pa g.yon pa bka ba na ni//sangs rgyas nor kun dngos grub pa//ma lus lus pa med bar 'grub//sangs rgyas khu tshur
27a1(rgyud): //bcangs nas ni//lag pa g.yon pas bkab na ni//sangs rgyas kyin gyi tshe 'grub pa//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//bud med phyag rgya bcings nas su//lag pa g.yon pas bka' nas ni
27a2(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad myur 'grub pa//ma lus lus med par 'grub//rta mchog dpal gyi khu tshur kyang //lag pa g.yon pas bkang nas ni//thams cad 'gugs par rab 'grub pa//ma lus lus po med par grub
27a3(rgyud): dpal grub spyan gyi khu tshur yang //lag pa g.yon pas dkab nas ni//snang ba thams cad rab 'grub pa//ma lus las pa med par 'grub//dang po sangs rgyas kun blod pa//gnyis pa de bzhin dpa'o yin//gsum
27a4(rgyud): pa kun du bzang po'o//bzhi pa mi zad bsnod yin no//sangs rgyas yang dag kun ldan dpal//de bzhin zhal zas gzungs ma yin//sprin chen mo ni bdun pa ste//rgyad pa nam mkha' spyan yin no//gzungs thams
27a5(rgyud): cad kyi phyag rgya'o//phyag rgya shes pa 'di dag gi//sangs rgyas sprul pa'i klu mchog ni//'grub par 'gyur ba med pa'i mchog//las rnams thams cad rab sgrub pa//slong bar byed pa bstan
27a6(rgyud): sgrub pa//de bzhin sna tshogs rgyas pa dag//sha rdza'i dbyings kyi glu blangs nas//d+he ba ta'i glu blang ngo //'jig rten khams mang rdul phran drangs snyed khyab par 'phros pa yis//rgyal ba'i don rnams rab tu phra ba'i
27a7(rgyud): rdul gyi grangs snyed pa//sprul pa'i sku rnams rab tu phra ba'i rdul gyi grangs snyed ni//srid pa bde chen de bzhin gshegs kun rnam 'phrul sna tshogs kyis//rdul phran grangs snyed de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par
27a8(rgyud): sprul pa mdzod ho dpal rnam par snang mdzad sbyor ba//a la la ho/de ring grub par mdzad//glu 'di dag ni blangs pa yis//nam mkha' thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//grub par
27b1(rgyud): 'gyur ba gdon mi za//de nas bud med bzhi rnams dang//rang gi yang dag gsang ba yis//chog rgyas pa'i tshul bzhin du//me tog la sogs mchod pa bya//de bzhin gshegs pa'i phyag rgya shes
27b2(rgyud): pa'o//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//dam tshig la sdang rnam sbyang phyir//dur khrod dpal gyi phyag rgyar gnas//shin tu gdug cing gtum pa la 
27b3(rgyud): zhi bas phan par mi 'gyur te//shes rab thabs kyi rnal 'byor gyi//rnal 'byor bde mchog dam pa 'grub//sangs rgyas khros cung khro bo ni//thal bar 'jigs pa mchog yin te//sems 
27b4(rgyud): dpa' sngon po 'bar ba can//'bar ba'i dkyil 'khor dmar po snang//gzhan gyi ma yin mi 'khrogs pa//lha rdzas chu yis dag pa ste//de bzhin rang bzhin nyid dag dang//
27b5(rgyud): mthu dang ngo bo nyid kyis 'grub//gos chen po dang glubs pa che//sha chen po dang sbrang rtsi che//dur khrod kun gyi 'byung po yi//phyag rgya dpal ldan dbang phyug mchog//shin tu khro gdug
27b6(rgyud): rtum pa yi;sems can khams mang rnam sbyang phyir//'di na dpal ldan he ru ka'i//rdo rje rgya chen dbang phyug mchog/thod pa'i phreng ba dbu rgyan can//sangs rgyas gtsug gi nor bu dag//rgyal srid
27b7(rgyud): thams cad rab 'grub pa//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//rdo rje kha taM rdo rjer bcas//dril bu dkrol ba'i dgrar bcas pa//dpal he ru ka'i babs pa yin//phyag rgya sna tshogs rnam 'phrul 
27b8(rgyud): bas;;sangs rgyas rdo rje pad ma 'dzin//rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum pa//rgyas btab rmongs byas de zos nas//thal bar 'gyur ba 'ang 'tsho bar byed//dpal rdo rje he ru ka rdo rje phab pa rnam par
28a1(rgyud): @#//'phrul pa//pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//se ral kha'i tshul dag du//khwa tra~M kha la rgyab rten bcas//lag pa g.yon pa thod pa thogs//g.yas pa ba dan gsu~M
28a2(rgyud): par bya;nam mkha'i phyag rgya rnal 'byor pa//nam kha' kun du 'gro bar 'gyur//bud med la sogs rin chen mchog//dngos po thams cad mkha' las 'byin//phyag rgya 'di yis yongs btab n+sa
28a3(rgyud): bde bar lhags pa'i tshul ston pas//nam mkha' las ni kun blags te//bdag gi spyad par de spyod do//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla mkha' 'gro mi zad tshogs//mkha' las sgyu ma kun 
28a4(rgyud): blags nas//lus pa med par des za'o//nam mkha' thams cad mnyam par sbyor ba//skal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma'i yis mchog ces bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//g.yas dang
28a5(rgyud): g.yon pa'i sbyor ba yis//bde bar 'bab pa'i gar byed pas//dkyil mo dkrung ni phyed bcas pa//rkang pa rang gi rlas mnan na//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshangs bac+sa
28a6(rgyud): pa;rgyas btab rmongs byas slangs nas su//gar dag byed cing des za'o//de nyid kyis ni sbyor ba yis//de nas bde bar rla sngams pas//legs par sngams te phyir btags nas//rab tu kram te 
28a7(rgyud): btang bar bya;;sgyu ma de dag nyid kyis ni//de nas rla phrag bskyod pa yis//skal bzang lha dang mi rnams ni//bud med kun la de skyod do//bde bar reg pas mnyam sbyor ba//skal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje
28a8(rgyud): drag po gnas pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//de nas gzhan yang rab bshad pa//sangs rgyas nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor du//dpal ldan he ru ka 'bab pa'i//phyag rgya sna tshogs gnas pa'o//zhi ba 
28b1(rgyud): drag po rmongs pa'i mchog//dga' ba'i gar byed 'gyur bar byas//shi ba ldam pa'i sbyor ba yis//bde ba'i phyag rgya rab tu gnas//zhi dang drag po rmongs byed dang//gyen du dbugs
28b2(rgyud): 'byin gar byed lus//lta ba'i phyag rgya dngos grub ni//gsang ba'i sbyor ba med mchog gi//me tog gdug pa sbyor ba'i mchog//chang dang chu dang lcug ma dang//ras dang me tog tong grub be yis
28b3(rgyud): khyad par du ni rgyas 'debs so//rtse gcig mnyam par sbyor ba yis//sbyor ba'i gnyer ba bde ba sbyin//mngon par gtong zhing se gol rdob//dal bus gyen du dbugs 'byin pas//
28b4(rgyud): g.yon du lta zhing lag pas drang//'o byed 'khyud pa de bzhin te//gar byed rab tu rmongs pa dang//zos dang gsang ba'i lus rnams dang//tshangs pa chen po mgo dang ni//de bzhin 'jig rten gsuM
28b5(rgyud): las rgyal;;de bzhin las la 'dren pa dang//rang gi phyag rgya'i tshul ston dang//dur khrod dag ni gar byed dang//de bzhin rlung gi dkyil 'khor dang//rab tu 'jig pa sreg pa'i me//
28b6(rgyud): las kyi phyag rgyar rab tu gnas//dang por rgyas 'debs ma'i rgya//snying 'dren ma'i gnyis pa'o//gsum pa shin tu rmongs byed ma'i//de bzhin bzhi pa 'degs byed ma'i//lnga pa gar byed ma'i rgya//drug
28b7(rgyud): pa gsod ma'i yin par bshad//bdun pa zab mo'i rgya//rgyad pa las kyi phyag rgya yin//de nas phyag rgya 'di kun gyi//phyag rgya'i las rnams bla na med//phyag rgya'i bdag nyid kun 'joms pa//khyad
28b8(rgyud): par du ni bshad par bya//dpal ldan rdo rje pad mo slong//gar dag byed cing byas na ni//rgyas btab rmongs byas zos pa dang//thal bar 'gyur ba 'ang 'tshal bar byed//de nas phyag rgya shes pa 'di//dpal ldan
29a1(rgyud): @#//rdo rje sbyor ba'i glu//las rnams thams cad rab sgrub pa//khyad par du ni bshad par bya//gzigs pa sna tshogs 'gyur ba'i rgyas thob la//rnam pa sna tshogs 'o byed 'khyud pas rmongs 
29a2(rgyud): mdzad te;;rnam pa sna tshogs bde ba'i mchog kun ston gsol la//rnam pa sna tshogs bde mchog ngo mtshar bstan du gsol//rgyas btab rmongs byas banabs su gsol//bde mchog ngo mtshar 
29a3(rgyud): bde ba zhug;;hUM a la la la ho//snang bar mdzod;rnal 'byor bde mchog sgyu mas sprul//dam tshig bdag la 'jug du gsol//nyon mongs kun las rgyal bar mdzod//ma lus pa dag snams su gsol
