29a4(rgyud): nyon mongs thams cad gsal bar mdzod//glu 'di dag ni blangs pa yis//rdo rje thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha'agro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//gdon mi za bar 'grub par 
29a5(rgyud): 'gyur;;dpal rdo rje he ru ka'i phyag rgya shes pa'ornal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//'dod chags thams cad rnam dag phyir//pad mo'i phyag
29a6(rgyud): rgya dpal du gnas//mi 'gyur dam pa dag pa'i phyir//shel sgong shin tu dag pa 'dra//'dod chags thams cad gsal ba'i phyir//'bar ba sna tshogs 'khrigs pa'i 'od//sangs rgyas tshe'i rin chen spras
29a7(rgyud): gos dang sna tshogs rgyan gyis brgyan//sangs rgyas thams cad 'du ba'i gnas//bde ba can bzhin dam par 'gyur//gar thabs rnam pa sna tshogs kyis//yongs su sgyur zhing gar byas bas//pad ma'i 
29a8(rgyud): mtshan mchog pad mor bcas//phyag ni rgyan rnams 'khor lo skor//sangs rgyas rdo rje pad mo 'dzin//rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum po//pad mo sgyu mas rnam sbyangs nas//sangs rgyas byang chub bsgrub par mdzad/
29b1(rgyud): 'dul ba'i thabs sna tshogs skal pa bzang po dpal pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug ces bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//g.yas dang g.yon pa'i sbyor ba yis//dkyil mo dkrung ni phyed par gnas//rgyal ba'i dkyil
29b2(rgyud): mo dkrung bcas na//sdug bsngal thams cad sel bar byed//dpal pad mo rgyal po'i gnas zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//snyems pa'i lag pa g.yon pa yis//pad ma dmar po ci bder bzung//rang gi 
29b3(rgyud): snying khar 'dren pa'i tshul//pad mo 'dab ma kha 'byed pa'o//snyems pa'i lag pa g.yas pa yis//pad mo sngon po bde bar bzung//rang gi snying khar 'dren tshul gyis//bud med phyag rgya bcing bar byed//
29b4(rgyud): snyems pa'i lag pa g.yon payis//pad ma dkar po ci bde bzung//'khor lo phyag rgya bcings nas ni//chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu skor;snyems pa'i lag pa g.yonpa yis//pad ma 'thing ka
29b5(rgyud): ci bder bzung//g.yas pa rab tu dga' byedpa//dpal gyi phyag rgya ba dan gsum//sems pa'i lag pa g.yon pi yis//pad ma ser po ci bder g.yas pa rab tu gar byed p+sa
29b6(rgyud): chu srin tshul du sgreng bar byed//snyems pa'i lagpa g.yon pa yis//pad mo leb rgan mchog bzung ste//rang gi snying ka'i 'dren tshul gyis//rdo rje phyag rgya bcings par byed//rnam par gzigs pI
29b7(rgyud): rnam dag dpal sgrol ma'i byang chub mchog yin no//rgyal chen ma'i 'khor lo skor//phro gnyer ma'i phyag rgya'i lan//pad mo gnas pa'i dpal chen yin//'dod pa'i dbang mo 'gro ba gsum//dpal pad
29b8(rgyud): mo rgyal po rig pa'i lha mo chen mo rnams so//de nas phyag rgya shes pa 'di//pad mo'i rgyal po dpal gyi glu//las rnams thams cad rab sgrub pa//khyad par du ni bshad par bya//de bzhin gshegs pa 
30a1(rgyud): //kun la sogs pa'i gzungs//mchog sna tshogs 'dzin sems can rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul mchog dngos grub mchog mnga' ba//sems dpa' pad mo dag mchog sgyu ma'i dga' ba
30a2(rgyud): ston du gsol//a la la la ho kye gar dbang mthar dga' gar mdzod cig//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin pa'i bdag//tshangs pa 'jug sel dbang phyug bdag//nyi zla bdag lags a la la la ho pad
30a3(rgyud): sgyu ma dpal;;rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul mdzad dag pa'i tshul gyis gar mdzad pa//pad mo dam tshig dbang phyug sangs rgyas gar rnaMs mdzad du gsol//'dod chags rnam par spyod pa thams cad du rnam par
30a4(rgyud): sbyongs;;pad mo 'dzin pa de'i spyan sngar gar mkhan gar mdzod cig//glu 'di dag ni blangs pa yis//sna tshogs pad mo mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//gdon mi za ba
30a5(rgyud): 'grub par 'gyur//dpal pad mo dbang phyug gis//phyag rgyar shes pa'i//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//nor mchog rnam par sbyang ba'i
30a6(rgyud): phyir;;dpal ldan nyi ma phyag rgyar gnas//nam mkha' snying po thams cad phyogs re ba thams cad 'joms mdzad pa//bud med la sogs rin chen mchog//rin chen thams cad mkha' las phebs//
30a7(rgyud): shin tu gdug cing phro ba la//zhi bas phan par mi 'gyur te//shes rab thabs kyi nyi ma ni//dam pa'i don du dag par 'gyur//drag po 'dul ba'i 'jig rten rnams//drag po nyid kyis bsgrub
30a8(rgyud): par byed;nyi ma'i gzugs dang spyod lam dang //tshul dang gzi brjid chen por ston//rdo rje gzhu dang mda' mchog dag//shin tu legs par 'gyings pa dag//mdangs gsal 'dzum dang bcas pa'i spyan
30b1(rgyud): g.yon pa 'byed cing gyen du 'degs//drag po dbang po ny+re dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje bcas pa'i//'jig rten gsum po ma lus pA//drag shul nyid kyis 'dul bar mdzad//dpal khams
30b2(rgyud): gsum las rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rjes pus mo gnyis bcings te//rdo rje dkyil mo dkrung bcas nas//bdag gi stan la 'dug nas ni//bgegs rnams thams cad gshig
30b3(rgyud): par bya|dpal ldan rdo rje dkyil mo bkrung //dbang skur ba dang gter bdag dang //rgyal mtshan mchog ldan mchod pa che//ji ltar tshul dag ston pa dag//rang gi don rnams thams cad bsgrub //
30b4(rgyud): de nas phyag rgyar shes nas 'di//rgyal po sgyu ma mchog gi klu//las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub pa//khyad par du ni bshad par bya//khams gsum kun gyi rgyal srid sna tshogs mchog gi
30b5(rgyud): nor ldan pas|'jal dum g.yul 'gyed rnaM mang khyad par du sangs rgyas kyis bdu ba rnam pa sna tshogs 'jal dum rnam par rgyal ba'i rgyal mtshan can//rdo rje nyi ma rgyal po bde mchog 
30b6(rgyud): sgyu mas bstan du gsol//sems can sprin la byang chub mchog sbyin sna tshogs char 'bebs pa/bdag gi re ba kun bsgrubs bar myur bar ston//a la la la ho mi bzad gter gyi dbang skur khyad
30b7(rgyud): par bdag la stsal du gsol//khyad par rin chen 'di dag thams cad bud med rin chen rnams kyi phyir//rin chen tshogs kun nang na bud med rin chen gcig bu mchog//bud med rin chen dag pa'i
30b8(rgyud): rin du thams cad 'gro//glu 'di dag ni blangs pa yis//rgyal srid thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//gdon mi za bar 'grub par 'gyur//dpal rdo rje nyi ma'i 
31a1(rgyud): phyag rgya zhes bya'o//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//drag shul sbyor ba rnal 'byor dpal//rta'i phyag rgya rab tu gnas//thams cad
31a2(rgyud): dag par mos pa yi//sdig med stobs kyis ngo 'thon du//rab tu gnon par stsal dag gis//'jig rten thams cad rab tu 'joms//'dod chags can la g.yem par ston //gdug pa
31a3(rgyud): rnams la gdug par 'gyur//zhi ba rnams la zhi bar 'gyur//drag shul can la drag shul sbyor//zhal bzhi pa la phyag bzhi pa//zhabs bzhi pa la mig pa bzhi//rta 'grin rgyal po chen po ste
31a4(rgyud): dbang phyug dam pa 'grub par 'gyur//g.yon pa 'dzub mo'i khu tshur gyis//dpal ldan pad ma sna tshogs kyis//rdo rjer bcas shing slong ba bsgrub//g.yas pa ba dan gsum
31a5(rgyud): par bya//phyag rgya 'di yis btab nas ni//shin tu ltung bar gyur//de ma thag tu kun sla bas nas//lta ba thams cad rab tu bsgrub//thams cad dag par mnyam par sbyor ba bzang po
31a6(rgyud): dpal rdo rje slong ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//g.yas dang g.yon pa'i sbyor ba yis//rkyang skum pa'i tshul byas nas//kho bo'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas te//'jig rten thams cad rab tu gzhig

31a8(rgyud): ltag par byed//bde ba 'dod pa'i gzugs ston po//'dul ba'i dbang gis gshin rje'i stong pa'i lhag par rab tu 'jigs//zhi ba'i sku yang tshangs pa'i stong pas lhag par shin
32a1(rgyud): /du ston;;dkyil 'khor kun gyi mchog dude bzhin gshegs kun 'grub par mdzod//thams cad rnam dag mos pas ngo 'thon stobs kyis gnon pas non /hi he he he/dbang
32a2(rgyud): phyug thams cad rab shig rab tu shig/rab kyi rab rtsal gyis gnon pa'i stobs kyis ngo 'thon dbang phyug non//sbyor ce hu lu hu lu mig gsum pa la lag gsum grub par drongs//rab kyi rtsal
32a3(rgyud): gyis mnon pa'i stobs kyis//ngo 'thon 'thu chen non//mnyam sbyor ru tra ma Ti snying las dpal mo drongs shig rab drongs shig/rab kyi rtsal gyis gnon pa'i stobasakyis ngo 'thon tshangs pa non//
32a4(rgyud): sbyor chen tshangs pas grol ma drongs shig smrar ra Ta/rab kyi rtsal gyis mnon pa'i stobs ngo 'thon dga' dbang non/mnyam sbyor kun gyis bdag la bdag drongs/a la la la ho//
32a5(rgyud): gdug cing khro la rab tu gtum pa'i sems can sgral ba'i phyir/grub zhugs a la la la ho/oM suM b+ha ni suM b+ha ni hUM phaT phaT/'dza bo'i mi la bya 'am mi bya yongs rtog de dag med la sla//
32a6(rgyud): de 'dra'i 'dza' bo sgyu ma yin pas de la 'dza zhes su zhig zer//dgol ba gang la bya 'am mi bya yongs su rtog pa med kyang bla/de 'dra'i dgol ba sgyu ma yin pa de la dpa' zhes su zhig zer//glu 'di blangs pa tsam
32a7(rgyud): gyis ni;rta mchog mnyam par sbyor ba yi//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//gdon mi za bar 'gyur par 'gyur//thams cad 'byung ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//'dzub mos 'the bong mnan nas ni//rang gi
32a8(rgyud): 'the bas 'dzub mo mnan//dpal grub spyan gyi phyag rgya ste//'dzub mo mig gi 'bras bur byas//rdo rje thal mo mnyam sbyor nas//rdo rje spyan ni gnyis dag tu//khong stong du ni sbyar bar bya//dpal grub rdo rje spyan
32b1(rgyud): yin no//'dis ni sangs rgyas thams cad dang//de bzhin dpa' bo thams cad dang//rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad du//tshe 'di nyid la 'grub par 'gyur//dpal grub can ni grub pa ste//sems can thams
32b2(rgyud): cad dbang byed pa//las kyi dngos grub rab 'byam rnams//ma lus dngos grub myur du 'grub//rdo rje thal mo mnyam sbyar nas//'dzub mo gnyis kyi rtse mo dgug//mthe bo'i rtse mo mnyam par gzhag//
32b3(rgyud): khong stong mi 'gyur dam pa'o//spyi bo'i mtshams su rab gzhag nas//'di yis srung ba byas na ni//sangs rgyas dngos grub ma lus pa//lus pa med par rab tu 'grub//sems dpa'i gtsug
32b4(rgyud): tor gyi phyag rgya'o//rdo rje thal mo gyen du dbye//'dzub mo gnyis kyi rtsa mo dgug//mthe bo gnyis kyi mche ba rtsigs//'jigs pa'i rdo rje gnod sbyin ma//grub pa bdud la sogs pa za//
32b5(rgyud): de bzhin du na rmongs par byed//dbab pa thams cad rab tu 'grub//de bzhin rnam par shes pa sgrub//gtor rnams 'byin par byed pa dang//de bzhin nor bu 'phrog par byed//
32b6(rgyud): ngal so po ni dpal min te//dpal mo dag kyang phrogs nas ni//mngag zhug mar ni byed pa dang//gnod sbyin pa dag dga' bar byed//gnod sbyin dag ni sde dpon du//'di yis myur du 'grub par 'gyur
32b7(rgyud): dpal rdo rje gnod sbyin gyi phyag rgya'o//rdo rje tha mo mnyam sbyor nas//gung mos mnyam par rab tu bsgrub//mthe bo 'thu'u chung mdab ma bzhi//pad mo dri med rab tu rgyas//'dis ni sdig rnams byang bar 'gyur//
32b8(rgyud): sdig pa byang nas rab tu 'grub//sangs rgyas byang chub mngon sangs rgyas//rdo rje sems dpa' nyid du 'gyur//dpal pad mo'i rgyal po'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje thal mo mnyam sbyar la//'dzub mo mnyam par phyir
33a1(rgyud): bkug ste//mthe bo rdo rje g.yas pa ni//rdo rje dpal gyi nor bu ste//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//nam mkha' thams cad rab 'grub ste//bud med la sogs rin chen mchog/mkha' las dngos 
33a2(rgyud): po thams cad spyod//rdo rje thal mo sbyar na ni//mthe'u chung srin lag mnyam par rkyang //'dzub mo rtse snyams bkug pa ni//dpal mchog btang zhes grags pa ste//phyag rgya 'di yis gtab na ni//sangs 
33a3(rgyud): rgyas byang chub rab 'grel na//thams cad dga' bar byed pa'i dpal//lha mi skal bzangs smos mi dgos//dam tshig gi phyag rgya zhes bya'o//lus dang ngag sems rdo rje yi//mtshan ma'i phyag rgyar ldan byas te//
33a4(rgyud): thams cad 'byung ba'i sbyor ba yis//bdag gi lha ni bdag nyid bya//'dis ni sangs rgyas thams cad dpal//de bzhin dpa' bo thams cad dang //rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad du//tshe 'di nyid la 'grub 
33a5(rgyud): par 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun bdag dngos grub ni//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis mthas glas pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba yi//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma 
33a6(rgyud): bde mchog 'dis//mi 'grub 'byor par mi 'gyur ba'i//dam pa'i rgya chen gang yang med//dam tshig chen po'i phyag rgyas shes pa'o//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal 'byor ba//ji lta'i gzugs dang dbyibs su ldan 
33a7(rgyud): bde ba nyid kyis kun gyi bdag/kun gyi bdag la zhugs par byed//phyag rgya 'di yis rnal 'byor pa//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng //rgyal po sangs rgyas kun dang 'dra//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub//sangs 
33a8(rgyud): rgyas thams cad mnyam par sbyor//skal pa bzang po dpal ye shes sems dpa' zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//chags pa med cing ma chags med//dbu ma'ang dmigs su yod ma yin//bud med kun gyi sbyor ba 
33b1(rgyud): 'dis//gnyis su med pa'i theg pa'i mchog//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng //dpal ldan sgyu ma kun gyi bdag//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub//sgyu ma thams cad kyi lha mo'i
33b2(rgyud): phyag rgya shes pa'o//yod pa ma yin med pa min//dbu ma 'ang dmigs su yod ma yin//shes rab pha rol phyin sbyor ba//sangs rgyas byang chub rab sgrub pa'o//thams cad nam mkha'i mtshan nyid te//
33b3(rgyud): nam mkha' la yang mtshan nyid med//khams gsum dag ni ma lus pa//thams cad sgyu ma lta bu ste//ji ltar sgyu ma thams cad du//mthong ba dang ni reg par 'gyur//dmigs su yod pa 'ang ma yin//
33b4(rgyud): de bzhin 'gro ba kun gyi tshul//phyag rgya 'di yis rnal 'byor pa//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro'ang ldeng //bud med la sogs rin chen mchog//khams las dngos rnams thams cad byed//dngos po med
33b5(rgyud): par bdag sbyor bas//nam mkha' kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//shin tu rtag par 'grub par 'gyur//ji ltar pad ma dmar po yis//chags pI
33b6(rgyud): nyes pas mi gos pa//rnal 'byor ldan pa'ang de bzhin du//gnas kyi nyes pas mi yong gos//shin tu gdug cing drag pa la//zhi bas phan par mi 'gyur te//shes rab thabs kyis sbyor ba 'di//rnam par
33b7(rgyud): rgyal phyir rab tu drag//thams cad dag phyir mos pa yis//sdig med stobs kyis ngo 'thon du//drag shul sbyor bas mnyam sbyor bas//'jig rten thams cad gzhom par bya//sbyor ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//
33b8(rgyud): sbyor ba ji snyed yod kun dang //byed pa rnam pa sna tshogs la//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyi rgya//dngos grub thams cad rab bsgrub pa'o//byed pa'i phyag rgya 'di rnams kyis//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng //
34a1(rgyud): //chags pa chen po dbang phyug dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub//kun du sna tshogs skal pa bzang po dpal dam tshig ces bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//bya ba dang ni zlog pa dang //khyad par du ni
34a2(rgyud): 'phrog pa dang //yan lag kun du byed pa yis//de ni nor rnams kun kyang 'phrog//'o byed 'khyud pa la sogs pa'i//khams gsum po yang rgyas 'debs so//thar bar byed cing bsod par byed࿒
34a3(rgyud): shi ba'ang mod la sos par byed//sbyor ba'i longs spyod thams cad kyis//gnas gsum po ni ma lus par//rgyas btab rmongs bcas slad nas su//gar byas nas ni za bar byed//sbyor ba kun gyi bde myong
34a4(rgyud): bas;;khams gsum pa ni ma lus pa//rgyas btab rmongs byas zos pa dang //thal bar gyur ba'ang 'tsho bar byed//sbyor ba thams cad kyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//lha mo kun gyi bde ba'i mcho(ga)
34a5(rgyud): mdzes pa'i zhal ni rnam sgyur cing //bde ba myong ba rab sbyor ba//mdzes par lta zhing gar byed pas//sangs rgyas pad ma rdo rje 'dzin//rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum pa//rgyas btab rmongs
34a6(rgyud): byas zos pa dang //thal( )bar 'gyur pa'ang 'tsho bar byed//bud med khams gsum sgyu ma che//mdzes pa'i bzhin ni rnam sgyur ba//rim gro chas shing steg pa'i mchog//mdzes par lta ba'i gar byas pa//
34a7(rgyud): sangs rgyas rdo rje pad mo 'dzin//rdo rje nyi ma kham sa gsum pa//rgyas btab mongs bcas slang nas ni//zos nas slar yang 'tsho bar byed//sangs rgyas byang chub mnyam sbyor ba//rab tu dga' ba lta 
34a8(rgyud): zhing 'dzum shin du srid pa yang dag sgrub//gsang ba'i gsang bar gnas na ni//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshangs bcas pa'i//srid pa shin tu kun sgrubs nas//gnas gsum dag ni sgrub
34b1(rgyud): par byed;steg la sogs pa'i nyams dang ldan//mdzes pa'i bzhin ni rnam sgyur cing;/rim gror bcas te steg pa'i mchog//steg pa'i lta bas gar byed cing//mdzes pa'i tshul dang thabs rnams
34b2(rgyud): kyis;drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshangs bcas pa'i//'jig rten gsum dag sgrub par byed//mche ba chung zad rtsigs pa'i kha//khro zhing 'dzum dang bcas pa'i bzhin//khro la 
34b3(rgyud): 'gyur bar bcangs shing 'dzum//gsal bar lta zhing legs par gnas//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshangs bcas pa//rgyal po'i sgyu mas rnam rgyal nas//khams gsum
34b4(rgyud): dag ni sgrub par byed//'dod chags myos p+sa g.yem basbyor ba//mdzes pa'i bzhin ni rnam par 'gyur//'di ni bzhin gyi phyag rgya mchog//khams gsum dag kyang dga' bar byed//drag
34b5(rgyud): po dbang po rnon po rtsigs//gdug pa za ba 'jigs lta ba//g.yas kyi bzhin gyi phyag rgya yis//khams gsum dag kyang za bar byed//dngos grub kun gyi bde ba'i mchog//rab tu dga'
34b6(rgyud): bar lta zhing 'dzum//g.yon pa bzhin gyi phyag rgya yis/khams gsum dag ni thar byed pa//spyi bor rta 'grin gsang 'tho ba//rta rtong ljang ser kha tog gsal//so rtsigs pa ni 'chu mnan nas//'jig
34b7(rgyud): rten thams cad 'jigs par byed//bzhin gyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//lha mo kun gyi bde ba'i mchog//mdzes pa'i ma 'chu sos nan pa'i//'dzum pa'i phyag rgya mchog 'di ni//sangs rgyas thams cad dges
34b8(rgyud): par byed;'dod chags bde bas g.yen sbyo ba//steg pa'i ma 'chu sos mnan pa'i//'dzum pa'i phyag rgya mchog 'di ni/khams gsum dag kyang dag 'a bar byed//sbyor ba kun gyi bde ba'i mchog
35a1(rgyud): @#//'dzum pa'i ma 'chu g.yon mnan pa//'dzum pa'i phyag rgya mchog 'di ni//khams gsum dag kyang dga' bar byed//sha chen gyi ni ro myong zhing//ma 'chu so yis mnan pa yis 
35a2(rgyud): 'dzum pa'i phyag rgya mchog 'id ni//khams gsum dag kyad za bar byed//bde ba kun gyi ro myong zhing//ma 'churnam par mdzes byas pas//'dzum pa'i phyag rgya mtshog 'di ni//thal bar gyur pa 
35a3(rgyud): 'ang 'tsho bar byed//'dzum pa'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//bde ba myong bar rab sbyor ba//zam zim lta ba rnam 'gyur bas//rgyas btab rmongs byas zos pa yis//khams gsum dag kyang 'tsho bar
35a4(rgyud): byed/bud med mkhas pa'i sgyu ma mchog//mdzes par lta ba'i gar byas pas//rgyas btab rmongs bas slang nas ni//khams gsum dag kyang bsgrub par byed//sangs rgyas gsang
35a5(rgyud): ba'i mnyam sbyor ba//lta ba rab bzhi bde ba gnas//lta ba'i phyag rgya mchog 'di ni//bud med thams cad dbang du byed//smin 'tshams khro gter 'gyur bar bcas//lta ba rab 
35a6(rgyud): 'bar rnam par 'gyur//lta ba'i phyag rgya mchoga'adi ni//khams gsum dag kyanga'abar bar byed//khro zhing 'gyur dang bcas par 'dzum//lta bar gsal ba mdzes par gnas//lta ba'i phyag rgya
35a7(rgyud): mchog 'di ni//khros pa thams cad rab tu bsgrub//steg la sogs pa'i ro dang ldan//sdeg bcas lta ba'i gar byed pas//lta ba'i phyag rgya mtshog 'di ni//khams gsum dang ni gar byed 'gyur//
35a8(rgyud): lta ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//na ma sdu ta sarba na d+he n+h+ya/zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya la gnas na//las dang mchod pa ma lus pa//lus pa med par thams cad 'grub//rab 'byung rnams kyi mchod pa kun//
35b1(rgyud): chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis mthas klas pa'i//lan cig grub pa yis//dam pa'i chos kyi phyag rgya 'dis//gang dag byas par ma gyur cing//spel ba yang ni ma
35b2(rgyud): gyur pa'i//dam pa rgya chen de dag med//A+oM ru ru s+phu dz+wo la ni s+tha si d+ha lo tsa na sarba rwa sa d+ha ni swahA//chos kyi phyag rgya mchog 'di ni//sems can thams cad dbang byed pa//stobs kyis myur du bkug nas
35b3(rgyud): ni//bran bzhin du ni byed du 'jug//su b+ha g+ha sta ma han de ba'-i hi ri mi ri mi ti ri ni shri ye swa hA//chos kyi phyag rgya dam pa 'di//'dod pa'i bde ba kun sbyin pa'o//a ta re tud ta re tu re swa hA//
35b4(rgyud): chos kyi phyag rgya la gnas pa//sangs rgyas byang chub rab bsgrubs na//bud med gzhan lta ci zhig smos//tra ba b+ha Ta Ta tra tra tra Ta/re ba thams cad kun rdzogs pa//chos kyi phyag rgya dam

35b7(rgyud): ba'i glu kun ni//hU~M gis glu'i phyag rgya dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' rab bsgrub pa'o//lha mo kun gyi lha mo mchog//ma lus bzhin du gar byas shing//kA ka l+ya sha Ta ba la sogs//glu rnams thams cad

38b4(rgyud): pa'i gzugs ldan gsung ldan par mdzod//rdo rje sems dpa' 'grub phyir 'du ba la mkhas pa ma lus bde ba'i mchog rnams las//la lar thams cad mnyam sbyor grub par mdzad//
38b5(rgyud): ru ta ma ta a la la la ho//rnam pa mang po 'dul bar mdzad pa'i tshul la gar mkhan mchog gi gar mdzad pa//dam tshig gir gyi dbang phyug sangs rgyas gar mkhan gar mdzod//a la la la ho//
38b6(rgyud): rnam mang bde mchog rab tu gar mdzad pa yis//thar pa'i bde ba 'ang thob par mdzod//de dag glu ni blangs pa yis//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//'grub

38b8(rgyud): kun las thar par 'gyur//bsod nams thams cad 'phel bar 'gyur//de bzhin rtag par srung bar 'gyur//rims dang dug dang dbyig dug dang //mkha' 'gro gtse dang //gdon rnams dang /
39a1(rgyud): @#//bdud dang sems can dmyal ba rnams//'di yis rab tu zhi bar 'gyur//dngos grub rnams dang phun tshogs dang//dga' dang de bzhin bdag po dang//sangs rgyas dam pa thams 
39a2(rgyud): cad dang/phyag rgya mchog rnams grub par 'gyur//gar gyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//rab tu dga' ba rjes su chags pa'i dga' ba'i mda' zhu rab tu phra ba'i rgyas tab pas//rab tu dga' bar sbyor
39a3(rgyud): ba'i bde bas myos pa'i bde ba thams cad bdag la grub par mdzod//rab tu bde mchog rnam pa sna tshogs rgyal ba'i rgyal mtshan sgreng ba'i longs spyod dang//rab tu dga' ba'i dbang phyug tshe mchog
39a4(rgyud): sgrub pa de bzhin gshegs pa bzhugs ho rta mchog ho//rab tu sna tshogs bde ba'i mchog la rol cig ho//bde bas slong rnam par rol snang bar mdzod//a la la la ho//de bzhin gsheg+sa
39a5(rgyud): pa kun gyi mkha' 'gro sgyu ma sna tshogs kyi//slob ma bzung du gsol//kye'e bde ba'i mchog grub par mdzod//e ma ho bde ba bde ba stsol//dam pa rab tu bzhugs par mdzod//
39a6(rgyud): bdag la rab tu dges par 'gyur//e ma ho shi shi bzhugs shig shig/mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//yongs su skor ba kun du bzhugs//'di dag glur ni blangs pa yis//sangs rgyas thams cad
39a7(rgyud): mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i chog//grub par gyur pa gdon mi za//rdo rje sems dpa' bsdud pa dang//rdo rje rin chen bla med pa//rdo rje chos ni gsung ba dang//rdo rje las ni byed par 'gyur//
39a8(rgyud): hU~M rdo rje las kyi mda' gzhu phyag 'tshal lo//dpal rdo rje chags pa'i dam tshig la phyag 'tshal lo//rdo rje kha twi ka rab tu 'jigs pa la phyag 'tshal lo//dpal rdorje gril bu khrol ba la phyag 'tshal lo//la lar rab tu drag
39b1(rgyud): cing skye bo drag la drag pa'i bud med sku ltar snang//'gro ba 'tha' yas gdul phyir la lar rab tu rna ba'i 'khor lo phyag na snams ba'i sku//la lar rab tu des shing zhi bar mi 'gyur sin pa tshangs 
39b2(rgyud): pa'i sku yang ston par mdzad//la lar rab tu 'bar ba'i chags pa pad ma'i rgyal po dam tshig la grub par mdzod//thams cad dbang skur dbang phyug pad ma mi zad gter mchog 
39b3(rgyud): bdag la grub par mdzad du gsol//kun mchog rgyal mtshan rnam rgyal sgreng ba rdo rje rin chen shin du che ba bdag la mdzod//la lar mkhas pa'i gas kyang 'dzin//la lar dus mtha'i 
39b4(rgyud): dus ltar dag//la lar rgyal ba'i dbang po bzhin du zhi//la lar rta mchog 'dod spyod 'dod pa sgrub//rdo rje glu'i mchod pa'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//me tog dkar dang dmar
39b5(rgyud): ba dang /'thing ka ser po kun sdus nas//me tog phyag rgya mchog 'di ni//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga la spro//rgya skyeg+sa/'thing ka ljang khu'i mchog//mtshon sna bzhi yis bcos
39b6(rgyud): pa dang /dkar po lnga'i phyag rgya dag//snga ma bzhin du mnyam par sgrub//khams las byung ba thams cad dang//srog dang rtsa ba de bzhin ste//rnam par mdzes shing dri zhim pa//snga ma bzhin du
39b7(rgyud): mnyam par sgrub/tsan dan gnyis dang bla rtsi dang//ga pur na sha la ni gdug pa bu ram dang sbyar ba//las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub pa'o//gan dar dri dang gu gul dang//sa rdza ga bur
39b8(rgyud): gla rtsi dang//rtsas 'di dag las btsos pa'i mar//me ra mar byas nas snang ba skyes//tsan dan gur mgum sri ka dang//ga bur dang ni bla rtsi dag//btsos pa'i gdug pa grub pa ste//spos kyi
40a1(rgyud): @#//phyag rgya spos mchog 'gyur//ka ko la dang sug smel dang//gla sgang ga bur bla rtsi dang//'di rnams zhib mor btags pa ni//seng ldeng bu ram dag dang sbyar//shing tsa ba 
40a2(rgyud): tre lo dra dang tan le sham dang ga bur sbyar//ha sa ka ri'i shing mar gyis//sbrus pa yi ni spos yin no//dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgya sbyor ba'o//me tog dmar po sangs rgyas la//rdo rje 
40a3(rgyud): can la sna tshogs pa//'jig rten dbang la me tog dkar//ser po kha las byung ba la//tsan tan gdug pas sangs rgyas la//rdo rje can la du ru ka//spyan ras gzigs la pa ka pi ta'i//ce phog
40a4(rgyud): rdo rje nyi ma la//mar me phreng ba sangs rgyas la//rdo rje cang la mar me bdun//spyan ras gzigs la mar me brgyad//stong ni rdo rje nyi ma la//sangs rgyas rnams la tsan dan spos//
40a5(rgyud): rdo rje can la gur gum yin//spyan ras gzigs latsan dan dmar//ga pur rdo rje nyi ma la//me tog kun dang gdug pa kun//de bzhin mar me thams cad dang//spos rnams thams cad rdo rje 
40a6(rgyud): rta la dbul du rung//mchod pa thams cad kyi phyag shes pa'o//sgyeg mo dang ni na chung dang//myos dang de bzhin bdud rtsi dang//gcig pu dpa' dang 'dod chags sra//g.yon can bran mo de bzhin te//lan tshwa 
40a7(rgyud): la sogs ro kun dang//ka ra la sogs bdud rtsi bzhin//bdag nyid kun gyi ro sgrub pa//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//sha dang chang du ldan pa yi//bza' ba sna tshogs grub pa ni//dga' ba'i longs 
40a8(rgyud): spyod skal mchog ste//tshe'i dam pa rab tu 'grub//A mra'i smin dang dgu 'brum dang//pa na sa dang na le ger//a ru ka la sogs pa yi//'bras bu'i phyag rgya mchog rnams 'di rnams//dngos grub 'bras 
40b1(rgyud): bu thams cad 'thob//ka ra sbrang rtsi byug pa yis//a mra la sogs tshig gus byug//sa ha ki ri'i mar la sogs//mnyam pa tri yis skur bar bya//yung dang rgya sgyeg la sogs pa'i//
40b2(rgyud): mtshon gyis 'thun par sgyur nas su//shun pa gro ga la sogs byas//mchod pa'i tshe na dbul bar bya//'bras bu'i bcos pa'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//sangs rgyas rnams la bshos dbul ba//
40b3(rgyud): sha dang chang ni rnam par spang //glu dang sil snyan grub pa dang //gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan dang //sha dang chang bu ldan pa ni//gtor ma'i mchog ste sna tshogs pa'o//bdag gis phyugs
40b4(rgyud): chen skal bzang po'o//rdo rje las ni dpal grub pa'o//bshos dang gtor ma dkar po dang //pad ma'i me tog ldan pa ni//spyan ras gzigs dbang gtso bo//phul na dgos pa thams
40b5(rgyud): cad 'grub//rgyal po'i kha zas sna tshogs pa//sha dang chu dang ldan pa dang //glu dang sil snyanaskal bzangs dag//rdo rje nyi ma'i 'khor la dbul//kha zas kun gyi ro rnams dang //sha dang sha'i
40b6(rgyud): mchog rnams dang //sha'i sbyar dang sha gro dang //bshos dag rdo rje dbul//mchod pa dang gtor ma'i phyag rgya'o//'theng po sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung //'dzub mo gnyis kyi rtse mo dgug//gung mo gnyis ni mnyam
40b7(rgyud): par sbyar;lhag ma'i rdo rje thal mor bya//sangs rgyas rin chen phyag rgya 'di//rang 'byung rnams kyis dbang skur ba//rgyal srid thams cad la ma lus pa//lus pa med par myur du 'grub//spyi bo dang ni   
40b8(rgyud): dbral ba dang //ldan pa gnyis dang lhag par ni//sangs rgyas kun dang rin chen mchog//dbu rgyan kun du brgyan par bya//dbu rgyan bcings pas bcings nas ni//'gying zhing mtha' nas dgrol bar bya//dar gyi 
41a1(rgyud): @#//mda' ma sbyangs pa ltar//byas nas thal mo brtan par bya//'di yis bsrung bar byas nas ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal dang 'dra//khams gsum dag ni rgyal srid che//'khor 
41a2(rgyud): los sgyur bas rab tu 'grub//dpal rdo rje he ru ka pad ma'i nor bu rin po che dbu rgyan mnyam par sbyor//skal pa bzang po dpal gyi phreng ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje thal mo mnyam sbyar te/lan 
41a3(rgyud): gnyis nor bu rin chen du//ji ltar rigs par mnyam sbyar nas//'dzub mo 'the bo'i rtse mo bzhi//yongs su sgyur te bteg nas su//pral ba legs par gzhag nas ni//khams gsum bdag 
41a4(rgyud): po rgyal ba dag//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//dpal rdo rje rin chen gyi phyag rgya'o//lag pa shin tu rkyang ba yi//'dzub mo gnyis ni 'dud par bcing //gung mo 'theng bong rtse mo can//
41a5(rgyud): rdo rje phyag rgya yongs su sgyur//yongs su sgyur te btegs nas kyang //khro gnyer steng du legs gzhag nas//rgyal srid thams cad rab 'grub pa//ma lus lus pa med pa 'grub 
41a6(rgyud): 'di yis srung ba byas nas ni//rdo rje las ni dpal dpal dra//rnal 'byor dbang phyug rab 'grub pa//ma lus las pa med par 'grub//ma mo dang ni byad ma dang //mkha' 'gro yang lhag par 'grub//dur khrod dngos 
41a7(rgyud): grub thams cad dang //las rnams thams cad byed par 'gyur//dpal rdo rje he ru ka'i phyag rgya'o//'the bong dpa' bo dkyil mo dkrung //'dzub mo gnyis po'i rtse mo dgug//gung mo gnyis ni mnyam par sbyar//
41a8(rgyud): lhag ma rdo rje thal mo'o//rdo rje rin chen phyag rgya 'di//rang 'byung rnams ni dbang skur ba'o//'jig rten dbang po dam pa kun//ma lus lus par med par 'grub//'di yis bsrung ba byas nas ni//rnam par snang 
41b1(rgyud): mdzad dpal dang 'dra//sangs rgyas kun gyi tshe grub pa'o//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//tshe dang ye shes dpag tu med pa rnam par nges pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje thal mo mnyam sbyar te//
41b2(rgyud): 'dzub mo gnyis snyams te phyi rol dgug//'the bong gnyis ni rab tu rgyang//rdo rje nor bu zhes kyang bya//dpral bar myu gu gzhag pa ni//bsam pa thams cad yongs sa rdzogs shing//rgyal srid thams
41b3(rgyud): rab tu 'grub tam lus lus pa med par 'gyur//dpal nor bu rin po che dbang skur ba'i phyag rgya'o//sangs rgyas min chen pad mo dang//de bzhin rin chen rdo rje dang//yid bzhin nor bu rin chen mchog
41b4(rgyud): rta mchog rab tu 'grub par 'gyur//phyag rgyas dbang skur ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//sor mo phan tshun snol ba ni//rdo rje thal mo yin par bshad//so so thams cad sdams pa ni//
41b5(rgyud): rdo rje sdams pa yin par bshad//rdo rje sdams pa byas nas ni//'dzub mo 'the bo'i nang dag tu//g.yas pa'i 'the bong mtshan ma yin//rtse mo bcug la sdam par bya//
41b6(rgyud): dga' chen dpal gyi rdo rje ste//phyag rgya thams cad rang 'byung ba//rab 'byam las rnams dngos grub ni//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//dga' ba'i rdo rje bcings nas ni//gung mo bcings
41b7(rgyud): pa'i nang du bzhug //'the bong 'the chung rtse mo can//rdo rje phyag rgya yongs su sgyur//'di yis sangs rgyas thams cad dang//de bzhin dpa' bo thams cad dang//rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad ni
41b8(rgyud): tshe 'di nyid la 'grub par 'gyur//dpal bde ba chen po'i rdo rje'i phyag rgya'o//de nas gung mo lcags kyu ni//bdag gi snying gar sbyar bar bya//'di ni thams cad dgug pa'i rgya//de ma thag tu phyag
42a1(rgyud): @#//rgya 'dis//sangs rgyas pad mo rdo rje 'dzin//rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum pa//bkug ste dbang du byas na ni//las rnams kun du sbyor ba byed//dpal bde ba chen po'i rdo rje 
42a2(rgyud): lcags kyu zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje sdams pa byas na ni//gung mo bcings pa'i nang du gzhug//'the chung 'the bong rtse mo can//rdo rje'i phyag rgya yongs sgyur na//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' 
42a3(rgyud): yang /sbyor ba 'di yis mi dka' na//dngos grub gzhan dang las kyi mchog//rab 'byam gzhan lta ci zhig smos//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dpal rdo rje'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje'i phyag rgya bcings 
42a4(rgyud): pa la//'byor ldan thal mo khong stong sbyor/khams gsum dag ni ma lus par//de ma thag tu 'bebs par byed//dpal rdo rje dbab pa'i phyag rgya'o//de'i phyag rgya bcings pa'i 
42a5(rgyud): las//gung mo de bzhin bkug nas ni//rdo rje rdo rje zhes brjod te//gung mo nam mkha'i rdo rje mchog//phyag rgya 'di yis btab nas ni//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong ldeng //dpal ldan 'dod 
42a6(rgyud): pa kun gyi bdag//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub//dpal rdo rje snying po'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje sems sdams pa la gnas te//'the bong rtse mo de bzhin snyam//'dzub mo gnyis kyi rtse mo dgug//rdo rje phyag 
42a7(rgyud): rgya bcing bar bya//phyag rgya 'di yis rnal 'byor pa//dag pa kun la rab tu gnas//byang chub sems dpa'i mtha' yas mchog//dngos grub thams cad rab tu 'grub//dpal rdorje rab tu gnas pa'i rdo rje'i phyag 
42a8(rgyud): rgya'o//rdo rje'i pad mo byas pa las//gung mo bcings pa'i dbus na gnas//'the'u chung snyams pa'i rtse mo mde'u//rdo rje mda' ni yongs su 'phang //phyag rgya 'di yis btabs na ni//tshangs pa nye dbang dga' 
42b1(rgyud): byed byas//mthu yi myur du rgyal byas na//khams gsum dag ni sgrub par byed//dpal rdo rje mda'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje'i phyag rgya yongs bcings nas//gung mo bcings pa'i phyi rol gzhag//rdo rje sna
42b2(rgyud): tshogs dpal gyi rgya//ldang ba thams cad rab bsgrub pa'o//rdo rje phyag rgya thams cad shes pa'o//a li ka li'i mnyam sbyor bas//kha'i thad kar gdud par bya//de'i 'og tu ri mo rnams//yi ge thams
42b3(rgyud): cad bdod par bya//gug skyed dag ni lcug mar bya//yi ge ra ni myu gur bya//glad gor zla ba gam pa 'am//tshe gnyis zla ba gnyis su bya//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//phyag rgya mi 'gyur dam pa
42b4(rgyud): ste//sangs rgyas dpal gyi ri mor bshad//yi ge dag kyang mi 'gyur mchog//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyi lha//bstod dang bcas pa'i mtshan bris na//sdig pa kun las thar bar 'gyur
42b5(rgyud): ba sod nams thams cad rab tu 'grub//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' dpal la bya'o zhes//rab tu chags pa'i mtshan bris nas//sdug bsngal kun las thar bar 'gyur//bde ba thams cad rab tu
42b6(rgyud): 'grub//chags chen grol ba dpal zhes pa'i//chags pa'i mtshan dag bris nas ni//sdig pa kun las tharabar 'gyur//'gro ba kun la rab tu gnas//snying rje dri med khro dpal zhes//bstod dang bcas pa'i 'tshan
42b7(rgyud): bris na//sdig pa kun nas thar bar 'gyur//dngos grub kun gyi mchog kyang 'grub//pad mo chags pa dpal dag ces//chags pa'i mtshan dag dris nas ni//sdig pa kunalas thar par 'gyur//
42b8(rgyud): dag pa thams cad rnam par dag//rdo rje nyi ma dpal mdangs zhes//bstod dang bcas ba'i mtshan bris na//sdigs pa kun las thar par 'gyur//longs spyod kun la rab tu gnas/rta mchog pha rol
43a1(rgyud): @#//gnon pa zhes//chags pa'i mtshan dag bris nas ni//sdig pa kun las thar bar 'gyur//dbang phyug thams cad rab tu 'grub//bris nas bsod nams che ba lta//brjod pa dag kyang de 
43a2(rgyud): bzhin te;las kyi dngos grub rab 'byam dag//ma lus par ni myur du 'grub//lhag pa'i lha ni gang gang gi//slob dpon gang dang gang yin pa//de dang de'i mtshan dag ni//dpal gyi tshig dang 
43a3(rgyud): ldan par brjod//slob ma dkyil 'khor btsud nas kyang //yon gyi mchog dang blangs nas ni//rdo rje ming gis dbang skur bas//de bzhin gshegs par de lung bstan//phyag rgya'i sbyor ba
43a4(rgyud): 'di dag gis//byang chub snying po'i bar du ni//rdo rje'i ming du rab tu gnas//de nas sangs rgyas su yang 'gyur//bstodar dang bcas pa'i ming gi phyag rgya shes pa'o//spyi 'or nyug
43a5(rgyud): pa gang yin pa//phyag 'tshal bar ni bstan pa yin//pral bar reg par bya ba ni//phyag lan yin par shes par bya//smin ma sgul ba gang yin pa//sangs rgyas thams 
43a6(rgyud): cad gnas pa'i rda//g.yas pa nam mkha' 'gro ba 'grub//g.yon pa rnam par rgyal ba'i mchog//rang 'byung rnams kyi rda'i rgya//mig gnyis g.yo ba grub pa ste//g.yon pa bud med bzang mo
43a7(rgyud): yin;g.yas pa skyes pa bzang po'o//rna ba dag la reg pa'i rda//g.yas g.yon gnyis ka de bzhin te//g.yon pa legs par blangs pa'i glu//g.yas pa legs par smras pa'o//sna'i bu ga gnyis
43a8(rgyud): kyi rda;sangs rgyas thams cad gnas pa ste//g.yas pa dag la gdug pa slang //g.yon pa bdag la dri slang ngo//lce ni ldag par byed pa dag//rda'i phyag rgya'i gnas pa ste//g.yas pasha'i phyag rgya yin//
43b1(rgyud): g.yon pa chang gi phyag rgya'o//mche ba g.yas dang g.yon pa yi//bar du ma 'chu mnan pa ni//g.yon pa bud med reg na bde//g.yas pa de bzhin skyes pa'o//lkog mar reg pa'ang de bzhin du//
43b2(rgyud): brda'i phyag rgyar gnas pa ste//theg pa chen po bla med pa'i//rtog pa thams cad smra ba'i mchog//snying khar gyen du 'dren pa ni//rang 'byung phyag rgya'i rda grub pas//rdo rje thams cad 'dren pa ste//'khor ba
43b3(rgyud): shin du snyud pa'o//lte ba'i dkyil du reg pa ni//brda'i phyag rgya gnas pa ste//dbu ma'i nan tan bsgrub pa yin//chos kyi 'khor lo chen po'i dkyil//rang gi dbang po sgreng ba ni//rang 'byung rnams kyi brda'i rgya//de bas bde 
43b4(rgyud): zhing bde ba yis//sangs rgyas thams cad thob par 'gyur//'phrag pa gnyis ni kun bstod pa//rda'i phyag rgyar gnas pa ste //g.yas pa kug pa'i phyag rgya yin//g.yon pa slad du gtong ba'o//dpung pa
43b5(rgyud): g.yas g.yon gnyis ka yis//bdag la 'khyud pa'i phyag rgya ni//g.yas pa thams cad grub pa yin//g.yon pa dbang phyug thams cad 'grub//khu tshur g.yas g.yon gnyis ka yis //khu tshur bcangs
43b6(rgyud): dang grol ba dag//g.yas pa rgyas 'debs phyag rgya yin//g.yon par mi rnams thong shig pa'o//brla gnyis rab tu sngam pa ni//g.yas dang g.yon pa de bzhin te//g.yon pa 'dod la rab tu brtson//g.yas pa chos la
43b7(rgyud): brtson pa'o//pus mo'i lha nga'i bda' dag kyang //g.yas dang g.yon pa debazhin te//g.yon pa 'dud pa'i phyag rgya ste//g.yas la rab tu phyag 'tshal lo//pus mo g.yas g.yon gnyis dag ni//rda'i phyag rgya rab
43b8(rgyud): tu gnas//g.yas pa 'ching ba'i phyag rgya ste//g.yon pa thams cad rnam par grol//rkang pa gnyis kyis sa gnan pa//g.yas pa g.yon pa de bzhin te/g.yon pa thams cad gnon par byed//g.yas pa thams
44a1(rgyud): /cad rab tu 'joms//dngos grub las dang bde ba dag//'di yis mi 'grub gang yang med//rang don las rnams rab tu 'grub//ma lus lus pa med pa 'grub//rda'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//
44a2(rgyud): oM a d+hi bu zhes bya ba'i brda'//'di ni phyag 'tshal zhes ston pa'o//pra ti oM d+hi bu zhes phyag lan yin par shes par bya//sems dpa' sngon dang mtha' med pa'o//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog
44a3(rgyud): sangs rgyas brda'i phyag rgya yin//oM ma ho bde ba yin par bshad//e ma ho bde ba dpal gyi mtshan//rdo rje sems dpa' gang brjod pa//sdig pa kun las thar par 'gyur//bde ba thams cad rab tu
44a4(rgyud): 'grub//bde mchog e ma ho bde ba dang //sgrol ma de bzhin shi shi dag/rgyas 'debs ma dang rmongs byed dang //zab mo dag dang tshe 'phel ma//sangs rgyas byang chub chos 'khor lo//
44a5(rgyud): de bzhin khams gsum rnam par rgyal//'dod pa'i lcug ma thams cad kyang //sangs rgyas brda'i phyag rgya'o//rta dang rdo rje zhal dag dang //de bzhin rdo rje snang ba dang //thal bar rlog pa'i ro langs ma//
44a6(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi bde' yin no//gling bu dang ni rgyud rnams dang //de bzhin du ni rgyud gcig dang //sdug po dang ni rdza rnga dag/sangs rgyas brda'i phyag brgya'o//me tog mchod dang bdug pas mchod//de bzhin mar me'i
44a7(rgyud): mchod pa dang //spos kyi mchod pa bla rtsi dag/longs spyod phyag rgya'i rda' yin no//de la sogs pa mtha' yas mchod//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa//lha mo 
44a8(rgyud): kun gyi rda' yin no//smin ma skyod pa brda' bzhi yin//b+ha ga ling ga e ma ho bde//thod pa pad mo yin par bshad//khwa traM kha yang rgyal mtshan che//sangs rgyas phyag rgya pad mor gnas//bde ba kun gyi ra sa yin
44b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgyar shis//shi shi sangs rgyas kun du bshad//dkyil 'khor la ni 'khor lor bshad//de nyid dam tshig yin par bshad//lha rnams thams cad tshogs pas na//skye bo dam pa kun 
44b2(rgyud): tshogs la tshogs kyi 'khor lo dam pa ni//mtha' ba thams cad 'dus pa ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi brda' dag gis//de bzhin mdza ba bsdus par gsungs//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal sbyor ba//dkyil 
44b3(rgyud): 'khor thams cad yin par bshad//lha mo thams cad kun 'dus pas//thams cad sna tshogs bskal pa bzang //rdo rje sems dpa' dpa' sbyor ba//sangs rgyas kun du bdag sbyor bas//rnam pa kun du 
44b4(rgyud): mkhyen pa'i phyir//kun gyi slob dpon yin par bshad//kun gyi slob dpon rab gnas pa//lha mo thams cad 'dus pas na//sangs rgyas kun gyi bda' bdag gis//kun du sna tshogs bskal
44b5(rgyud): bzang po te de'i slob ma grub pa ni//grub pa yis ni rjes sgrub pa//sangs rgyas bda' ni gsang ba yis//rgyal tshab yin par rnam par bshad//de las gzhan pa'i slob ma kun//mchog dang 
44b6(rgyud): 'bring dang tha ma'i rnams//sangs rgyas kun gyi rda dag gis//byang chub sems dpa' yin par bshad//sangs rgyas thams cad rin chen mchog//dbu rgyan mi 'gyur dam pa ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi rgyal srid mchog
44b7(rgyud): yin par rang 'byung rnams kyis gsungs//thod pa'i phreng ba'i dbu brgyan dang //sangs rgyas gtsug gi nor bu dag//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgya yis//dpal gyi pad ma'i tshul du bshad//sangs rgyas rang 
44b8(rgyud): bzhin grub cig dang //de bzhin pad ma'i rang bzhin cig//thams cad dbang skur dbang skur che//yin par don kun grub pas gsungs//yid yid dam pa grub pa ni//nor gyi dbyig du bshad pa ste//snying yang nor
45a1(rgyud): @#//gyi dbyig yin pas//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgyar bshad//ga bur tsan dan dmar po dang //sbyar ba phrag chen yin par bshad//tshogs kyi dbus su gzhag pa dpal//thams cad ltang ba'i 
45a2(rgyud): ras yan//bag zan ni byas pas byugs//tsan dan dmar po skus pa dang //bag phye zan las byas pa'i sha//rgyal po can ces gang bshad pa'o//de nas grub pa'i gna' sgeg pa//dam tshig ces par bshad 
45a3(rgyud): bde mchog bde mchog ces gsungs pa//sangs rgyas bde mchog thams cad kyis//phyag rgya bde ba'i mchog tu gsungs//de bzhin e ma ho bde ba dang //snang ba zhes pa rab tu gnas//shi shi zhes pa'ang 
45a4(rgyud): srid pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyis kyang //'da' bar bya ba ma yin pas//sangs rgyas brda'i phyag rgya yis//phyag rgya dam tshig yin par bshad//phyag rgya kha'anga' sbyor ba yin par bshad//rdo rje 
45a5(rgyud): sems dpa' dpal 'byor ba//sbyor ba thams//cad yin par bshad//da ni sna tshogs dam tshig ste//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba che//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgya yis//sbyor ba thams cad yin par 
45a6(rgyud): bshad//de'i nye ba'i longs spyod ni//khams gsum thams cad ma lus pa'o//de'i gzugs dang sgra dang dri//ro dang reg pa bde mos pa'o//gzugs ni ri mo yin par bshad//sgra ni glu yin zhes bshad pa'o//
45a7(rgyud): dri ni rab tu tshim par gsungs//ro nor sa ya na yin//reg pa bde ba yin par bshad//rab tu reg pa dga' ba'i bde//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgyu yis//chos kyi sbyor ba yin par bshad/sbyor ba'i longs 
45a8(rgyud): spyod thams cad kyis//ji ltar bde ba rten byed cing //bdag ni lhag pa'i lhar sbyor ba//rang gi mchod pa'i dam tshig yin//rjes su sbyor bas mchod na ni//sbyor ba kun gyi bde ba mchog/sangs rgyas rda'i 
45b1(rgyud): phyag rgya yis//bde ba chen po yin par bshad//ye shes tsam las snyoms 'jog pa//rnal 'byor zhes ni bshad pa yin//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgya yis//ting 'dzin rab tu sbyor 'ang bshad//
45b2(rgyud): lha mo kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//sangs rgyas thams cad 'dus pa'o//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//sangs rgya kun gyi byin rlabs pa'o//dga' ba sbyor ba grub par bshad//rjes su sbyor 
45b3(rgyud): rjes su sgrub//skyes pa ltar byed dga' spyod pa//slar yang rjes su bsgrub par bshad//lha mo kun la bde lcags pa//sbyor ba kun la mos pa ni//kun du sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag//ra sa ya na bde bar
45b4(rgyud): bshad|thams cad ldang ba kun 'gro ba//dngos||grub dam par rab tu bshad//dngos grub chen po ldang ba che//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rkyar bshad//dngos grub chenapo kun gyi mchog//dbang 
45b5(rgyud): phyug chen po bla na med//dbang phyug kun gyi dam pa mchog//sangs rgyas kun gyi brda yis bshad//dngos grub chen pa kun gyi mchog//dbang phyug chen po bde mchog yin//sangs rgyas thams cad
45b6(rgyud): mnyam sbyor ba//sbyor ba'i dbang phyug bde chen no//khrag ni me tog yin par bshad//mdangs ni gdug pa rtags//sha chen dag ni mar me ste//thal ba dag ni yongs su bshad//bam ni sgeg mo yin par bshad
45b7(rgyud): rab tu myos pa sbrang rtsi'o//bu ram chang ni myos byed gsungs//dgung chang dag ni bdud rtsi yin//bdud rtsi lam gyi yan lag brgyad//sangs rgyas kun gyi rda yin no//dgun dang sbrang rtsi gcig tu dpal//
45b8(rgyud): tshe ni 'drod chags sra ba yin//sgyur po khu ba g.yon can yin//kam pa la ni bran mor bshad//tshur ba zla ba nya ba yin//sha ni sman gyi mchog yin no//chang ni ra sa ya nar bshad//zas ni tshes zhes bya bar
46a1(rgyud): @#//bshad/bza' ba sna tshogs grub pa ni//sha dang chang du ldan pa dag//dga' ba'i longs spyod bskal bzang po//tshe'i dam pa yin par bshad//oM mra dgung 'brum pa na sa//na le ke ri a ru 
46a2(rgyud): ka/'bras bu chen po'i khyad par dag//bdud rtsi 'bras bu yin par bshad//am mra smin ma grol bar bshad//de bzhin bde ba nyal ba dang //bde bas rab tu rmongs pa dag//sangs rgyas kun gyi rda yin no 
46a3(rgyud): 'bras bu chen po pa na sa//dgung 'bruM khu ba khu ba'i mchog//na li ki ri dngos grub che//dam chos 'bras bu a ka ru ka//glu ni tshig tu brjod par bshad//dwa der dag ni sil snyan yin//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgya 
46a4(rgyud): yis//gar ni rnam par rol par bshad//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal 'byor pa//glu dang sil snyan gar byed pa//brda'i dam pas bshad pa ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi rnam sprul pa'o//rab tu zhi ba'i sil 
46a5(rgyud): snyan glu/gar ni shin tu rnam sprul pa'o//dur khrod dang ni sel snyan dang //gar ni shin du rnam sprul pa'o//chags byas glu dang sil snyan dang //gar ni shan du rnam sprul pa'o//mi'i dbang po sil snyan 
46a6(rgyud): glu/gar ni shin du rnam pa sprul pa'o//rab tu dga' ba'i sel snyan glu//rang gi rtsal gyis gar byas pa//rta mchog rnam par sprul pa ni//rta'i dam par rab tu bshad//b+hi zhes bya ba mkha' 'gro yi//khams 
46a7(rgyud): gsum 'dir ni rnam par rtogs//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma'i phyag rgya dag//mkha' 'gro zhes ni rab tu grags//kun du sna tshogs phyag rgya ni//thams cad mkhar 'gro ba ste//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha'

46b1(rgyud): byed pa/sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro ma'i dam tshig mchog//bcom ldan rdo rje sems dpa' dpal//rdo rje a ra li zhes bshad pa'o//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyamasbyor ba//mkha'
46b2(rgyud): 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//sems dpa' sngon dang mtha' med pa'o//rdo rje sems dpa' dga' ba'i mchog//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgya yis//de ni bskal pa bzang por bshad//gang zhig rdo rje sems
46b3(rgyud): dpa'i mtshan//skal pa bzang po zhes brjod na//shin tu srid pa kun 'grub cing//de ni dbang phyug chen por bshad//Ta gi 'dod pa yin par bshad//dga' ba'i ge le ki li yin//byams pa chu sren yin par bshad
46b4(rgyud): 'dod pa'i dbang po dbang phyug mchog//rdo rje dril bu phyag rgya dag//dam tshig gsum gyi dam pa yin//oM dang b+hu ka kha kun bshad//sangs rgyas rda'i phyag rgyu yin//drangs te bcug nas bcings pa
46b5(rgyud): dang //zhugs nas dbang du gyur pa ni//dza hUM baM ho/bshad pa ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi rda yin no//e hi ya hi zhes bshad pa//tshur spyon phar bzhud zhes sgrags pa'o//hi sta ha zhes gsungs pa ni
46b6(rgyud): long la drongs shig drongs shig/pa'o//kha kha kha hi zo cig pa'o//a ba'i zhes pa dag kyang yang//da ra da ra byen zhes pa'o//ru ta ma Ta rol cig pa'o//hu lu hu lu zhugs shig pa'o//a ra ra ra ra skul
46b7(rgyud): cig pa'o//e ma ho bde mchog ngo mtshar ni//a la la la ho bshad pa'o//bde mchog rgyas 'debs phyag rgya yin//e ma ho bde ba rmongs byed ma'i//smad pa zhes pa za ba'i rgya//hU~M shi shi na ni tshe 'phel

47a1(rgyud): /mdzad angaHa a a kun bdag bshad pa//sangs rgyas rnga'i phyag rgya yis//thams cad mkhyen par gyur pa dang //de bzhin bde ba mkhyen pa ni//oM sarba byid ces bshad pa ste//

47a8(rgyud): khwa traM kha dang rgyal mtshan che//thod pa kham por pad mo'i snod//khrag chen dang sbrang rtsi che//dkar mo dgu byed rab rmongs dang //ro langs ma dang dbos byed ma//rtum mo dang ni sne ma dang //lha mo dang ni bda' yin no//

47b2(rgyud): phaT phaT/zhes bshad pa/sangs rgyas kun gyi rnga yin ste//rgyal ba chen po rdo rje 'dzin//dbang phyug chen po dam pa ni//sangs rgyas rnga yis phyag rgya yis//skal pa bzang po yin par bshad//rgyal

49a1(rgyud): @#//cad 'grub pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal sbyor ba//ma mchu so'i sa legs mnan nas//dpal ldan g.yon du 'gying dang bcas//gsal bar phyogs su 'khor lo'i tshul

49b7(rgyud): 'gying dang bcas pa mtha' nas krol//dar sbyang ba'i thabs byas nas//thal mo brdabs pas bstan par bya//'di yis srung ba byasan ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal dang 'dra//mi chod pa dang mi byed
49b8(rgyud): pa'i//rdo rje sku ni rab tu 'grub//rims dang dug dang dbyig dug nad//mkha' 'gros gtses dang gdon rnams dang//yan lag ma rung byed pa kun//'di yis bsrungs dang yod mi tshugs//dpal rdo rje
50a1(rgyud): go cha'i zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//srung ba thams cad kyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//chos sems pa'i tshul ston cing//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis dag dang//zhi bas lta bas bltas na ni//rims
50a2(rgyud): dang dug rnams thams cad sel//rdo rje khu tshur gnyisabacings nas//'the'u chung lcags kyu lta bur bcing//'dzub mo snyams pa'i rtse mo gnan//nad rnams thams cad zlogs par byed//yongs su sgyur
50a3(rgyud): te bteg nas ni//dpral bar bde bar gzhag par bya//ma lus lus pa med pa yis//sdug bsngal thams cad med par byed//dpal rdo rje smin mtshams lus kyi nad bso ba'o//rdo rje sdam pa byas pa la//'dzub
50a4(rgyud): mo phan tshun mngon phyogs la//ma lus lus pa med yis//zug rngu kun las grol bar 'gyur//bu nad lhog dgal sgran dag dang//dmu rdzing la sogs kun las thar//'di nyid grub par gyur na ni
50a5(rgyud): nad rnams kun gyi gnod pa med//zug rngu thams cad med par byed pa'o//grub pa dpal gyi spyan bcing sa te//de yis mig gnyis phyis nas ni//phyag rgya'i sbyor ba mchog 'di yis//long ba dag kyang
50a6(rgyud): mthong bar byed//bdag gi sbyor ba ldan byas pas//rdo rje khu tshur pad mo yis//grang ba'i tshul du glad nad dang//kha phyogs drang zhing btung bar bya//rims dang gdon dang dug rnams kun//rdo rje khu tshur dpal gyis gzung//
50a7(rgyud): bdag gi lhar ni sbyor ldan pas//'thungs nas thams cad med par byed//klad pa'i sman no//ta thA ga ta ga b+ha b+ha sta ma si ho//'di ni rnam par zla ba'i mchog//zug rngu med cing myur du thar//zug rngu thams cad 'byin pa'o//
50a8(rgyud): g.yas pa yi ni lag pa yis//sems dpa' gtsug tor bcings nas ni//byis pa nad med 'tsho bar 'gyur//thams cad srung bzhing bde ba 'thob//gzhu nu gso ba'o/g.yas pa yi ni lag pa yi sa//klu'i rdengs ka/
50b1(rgyud): lta bur bya//'bar ba'i tshul ston 'bebs bcas pas//dug gi las rnams sgab par bya//'bar dang 'dul dang 'ching ba dang //za ba'i dug rnams med par byed//gdug pas zin dang lu ta dang //
50b2(rgyud): shi ba 'am sos par byed pa'i mchog/gdengs ka'i phyag rgya'o//g.yas pa'i 'the bong gong mo gnyis//g.yon pa'i khu tshur bcings pa'i//gung mo la ni reg byas nas//gdon rnams kun las thar 
50b3(rgyud): bar 'gyur//dug gso ba dang dug gi cha go//ba dan gsum po gnyis bcings nas//gong mo gnyis ni 'dud par bcing //'dzub mo 'the chung rtse mo can//rdo rje phyag rgya yongs bcings la//yongs su sgyur te bteg 
50b4(rgyud): nas su//smin ma'i 'tshams su gzhag pa ni//mkha' 'gro'i las rnams ma lus pa//lus pa med par thos cad 'grub//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//gnas gsum pa ni ma lus pa 
50b5(rgyud): rgyas btab rmongs byas zos pa dang //thal bar gyur pa'ang 'tsho bar byed//dpal rdo rje he ru ka'i khro ma nyer zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya ste ma mo ste shes pa'o//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal sbyor bas//
50b6(rgyud): rta rnams thams cad zhon nas ni//rta rnams nad med sos par dang //nyam skye 'thar zhing 'gyogs par 'gyur//dpal rta mchog gi phyag rgya'o//rdo rje sems dpa' dbal 'byor bas//glang po kun la 
50b7(rgyud): zhon nas ni//glang po thams cad nad med sos//nyam skye spro ldan myos par 'gyur//shi bad dzra sad t+ya/sta ma si ho/'di ni rna bar bzla ba'i mchog/bya rnams nad med 'tsho ba dang //mi'i skad 
50b8(rgyud): du smra bar 'gyur//bya gso ba'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje sems pa'i dpal sbyor ba//sbyor ba kun la rjes chags na//shing rnams mi skam pa med cing //rtag tu me tog 'bras bur ldan//
51a1(rgyud): @#//shing rnams bskyed pa'i gtsug lag las//sbyor ba'i cho ga gang bshad pa//phyag rgya 'di yis thams cad ni//yid la bsam pa bzhin du 'grub//shing skyed pa'i phyag rgya shes pa'o 
51a2(rgyud): 'khrul 'khor gyis 'phangs ma 'phangs dang //de bzhin lag pas 'phangs pa dang //'phangs nas slad du gzung ba dang //stobs su 'gyed pa lnga 'di yin te//rang 'byung rnams kyi dgra ci spyad//phyag rgya lnga la 
51a3(rgyud): rab tu gnas;;rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyis bshad//dam pa de dag bshad par bya//dbyibs dang bde blag sbyor ba dang //kun du sbyor dang 'dren dang myur//'gyengs dang 'phen dang gsal ba'o//rkang pa 
51a4(rgyud): bshibs pas sa mnan cing //rkang pa skyed pas sa mnan tedkyil 'khor dag kyang mnan par bya//rkyang skum   dag kyang gnas gsum gnan//dbyibs kyi phyag rgya'o//mi lhod pa dang sran
51a5(rgyud): gzugs dang ;gnyen bzhis yan lag rtan pa dang //rgya cher 'gyengs par grags//lus kyi yan lag bde ba can//'di yis lus la gnod pa dang //rims dang dug dang dbyig dug dang //
51a6(rgyud): mkha' 'gro gtse dang nad rnams kyis//nams kyang tshugs par yong mi 'gyur//rgya chen 'gyengs pa'i phyag rgya'o//rang gi snying kar drang tshul du//bde blag can gyi gzhu bzung nas//g.yas pa rab tu gar 
51a7(rgyud): byed cing ;mda' 'phangs na ni 'grub par 'gyur//dpal khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba'o//lag pa g.yon pa legs sreng la//skyon med pa'i g.yas pa dgug//gzhu rgyud dal bus grangs nas ni//'gro ba
51a8(rgyud): thams cad dgug par bya//thams cad dgug pa'i phyag rgya'o//rna (dru)bar du zlum po ni//ji ltar rigs par legs bkang la//yige a tsam rang gi khar//bzung na 'gro ba 'dzin par 'gyur//'dzin pa'i phyag rgya'o//
51b1(rgyud): ji ltar rigs par drangs nas ni//cho ga bzhin du bzung nas kyang//der ni cung zad dbye bar bya //'gengs pa thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//'gengs pa'i phyag rgya'o//changs pas dben ni sgrub
51b2(rgyud): par bya|'dzum dang bcas pa'i mig gis lta//lta ba changs pa la rtad nas//myur du 'phangs nas dam pa 'grub//mda' gzhus rgyal bar bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//gru bzhi pa la rno ba dang//de bzhin kun nas
51b3(rgyud): skor bar bya//g.yas phyogs nas ni mdangs skor nas//smad te de bzhin stod pa dang//rkang pas mnan cing 'bibs pas mnan 'bibs pa bcu'i rgya mchog gis//khams gsum rgyal srid chen po dag//
51b4(rgyud): bde bar thub nas nye bar spyod//dpal khams gsum las rnam par rgyal ba'o//rab tu 'phangs nas mnyam par 'phangs//de bzhin klog ltar 'phangs pa dang//glog ltar 'khrug pa de
51b5(rgyud): bzhin te//phyag rgya thabs rnams sgrub pa'o//thabs thaMs cad kyi phyag rgya'o//nyam nga lag dang bde phyogs dang//thang ka de bzhin gyen du 'debs//'di dag bzlog pa'i phyag rgya ste/
51b6(rgyud): gnas gsum dag ni zlog par bya//nges par zlog pa'i thams cad kyi phyag rgya'o//thams cad 'debs dang nges par 'debs//kun 'debs gyen du 'debs pa'i bar//'di dag 'phros pa'i phyag rgya'o//
51b7(rgyud): gnas gsum dag kyang 'phrog par byed//'phogs pa thams cad kyi phyag rgya'o/kun du kun nas thams cad kyi//rtag tu bdag nyid rnam pa kun//gzhu gzhu spyad kyi phyag rgya mchog//mi 'gyur dam pa 'grub par
51b8(rgyud): 'gyur|dgra cha thams cad kyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//srin lag gnyis kyi nang du ni//'the bong legs par bzhag par bya//de nas khu tshur bcings na ni//'dzub mo legs par dgug par bya//rdo rje khu tshur
52a1(rgyud): @#//dpal gyi phyag rgya'o//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal la gnas//las kyi dngos grub rab 'jam rnams//ma lus pa ni myur du 'grub//dpal rdo rje khu tshur gyi phyag rgya'o//rdo rje khu tshur legs 
52a2(rgyud): bcangs la/bdag gi 'the bong nang du bzhug//zhi ba'i las rnams rab 'grub pa//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi khu tshur ro//rdo rje khu tshur khu tshur bcangs pa las 
52a3(rgyud): 'the chung gnyis kyi rab rkyang nas//mkha' 'gro'i las rnams thams cad ni//ma lus lus pa med pa 'grub//dpal rdo rje he ru ka'i khu tshur gyi phyag rgya'o/bdeg pa'i khu tshur bcangs pa las//mthe bong dag las 
52a4(rgyud): gung mo gzhar//dpal pad mo khu tshur grub pa ste//thams cad dag pa'i las la sbyar//dpal pad mo khu tshu ro//khu tshur rdo rje rin chen mchog/'bebs pa thams cad yongs rdzogs pa//
52a5(rgyud): rang don las rnams grub pa yin//ma lus lus pa med pa/'grub//dpal rdo rje rin po che'i khu tshur gyi phyag rgya'o rdo rje khu tshur dpal bcangs la//sor mo thams cad yongs su bzung //rtul phod 
52a6(rgyud): las rnams thams cad ni/ma lus lus pa med pa 'gyub//las rnams thams cad kyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//rkang pa rkyang dang mnon pa dang //skums nas 'degs pa'i stabs dag ni//g.yas dang g.yon pa'i sbyor ba yis//
52a7(rgyud): gzugs rnams thams cad gnon par bya//sdig med stobs kyis ngo 'thon du//rnam par snang mdzad dpal mnan nas//kun du byang chub yongs kug ste//bdag gi spyad pa de spyod do//sdig med stobs kyis nga 'thon du//rdo rje 
52a8(rgyud): 'dzin pa dpal mnan nas//kun tu grub pa yongs bkug ste//bdag gi spyad bar de spyod do//sdig med stabs kyi ngo 'thon du//spyan ras gzigs dbang dpal mnan nas//kun da dpal mo yongs bkug ste//bdag
52b1(rgyud): gi spyad pa de spyod do//sdig med stobs kyis ngo 'thon du//rdo rje nyi ma'i dpal mnan nas//kun du longs spyod yongs bkug ste//bdag gi spyad par de spyod do//sdig med stabs kyis ngo 'thon du
52b2(rgyud): rta mchog dam pa dpal mnan nas//thams cad dbang phyug yongs bkug ste//bdag gar spyad pa de spyod do//byang chub spyad pha rol phyin pa dang//grub dang gzungs dang sa rnams dang//rig dang phyag rgya
52b3(rgyud): rnams mnan nas//bdag gi spyad pa de spyod do//sdig med stobs kyis ngo thon du//dbang phyug dam pa mnan nas ni//mig gsum pa las gsum grub kug//bdag gi spyad pa de skyod do//sdig med stobs
52b4(rgyud): kyis ngo 'thon du//'thu bo che yang mnan nas ni//snying las pad mo yongs bkug ste//bdag gyi spyad pa de spyod do//sdi ga med stobs kyis ngo 'than du//skye dgu'i bdag po mnan nas ni//mos la sgrol ma
52b5(rgyud): yongs bkug nas//bdag gi spyad par de sbyod do//sdig med stobs kyis ngo 'thon du//rab dga'i dbang phyug mnan nas ni//bdag gi bdag la dga' ba kug//bdag gi spyad bar de spyod do//
52b6(rgyud): 'jigs byed la sogs lha mo dang//mkha' 'gro ma rnams mnan nas ni//bdag ga spyod par de spyod do//de bzhin ku be ra la sogs//rig pa grub pa spyod pa dang//mi'i rgyal po la sogs kyang//bdag gir
52b7(rgyud): spyad par de spyod do//'gro ba dang ni mi 'gro ba'i//dngos po thams cad ma lus pa 'ang//rnal 'byor bdag gis de mnan nas//bdag gi spyad pa de spyod do//dpal rdo rje las kyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//khu tshur snying kar
52b8(rgyud): bcangs pa ni//bud med phyag rgya de bshad do//bud med stor ba ma lus pa//lus pa med par thams cad 'grub//g.yon pa 'gying ba'i tshul du byas//lag g.yas dag gi snying kar ni//gyen du dang ba'i tshul byas nas//bud med
53a1(rgyud): @#//thams cad dbang du 'gyur//dpal rdo rje 'gying ba'o//dpal ldan sna tshogs pad mo 'dzin//g.yon pa bdeg pa'i khu tshur dang //g.yas pa ldeng bar grub pa dang //sangs rgyas dbang phyug ting 'dzin byang //sangs 
53a2(rgyud): rgyas thams cad 'phar ba'o//g.yon pa dril bu sgra sgrogs cing //g.yas pa yi ni lag pa yis//rdo rje gyen du gsor zhing skor//sgyu ma thams cad 'byung bar 'gyur//dpal rdo rje sgyu ma'i dbang phyug ma'o//
53a3(rgyud): g.yon pa g.yon pa'i rla la gzhag/lag pa g.yas pa'i 'dzub mo yis//rtse mo pad ma dam pa 'dzin//g.yas pa'i rla la lag ngar gzhag/mi dga' thams cad sel ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//bdag gi 
53a4(rgyud): snying kar 'dren tshul du//g.yon pa nor bu rin po che 'dzin//g.yas pa re ba kun rdzogs pa'i//bud med la sogs rin chen 'dzin//bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o//bdag 
53a5(rgyud): gi snying kar 'dren tshul du/pad mo sna tshogs dam pa 'dzin//g.yas pa rdo rje 'gying ba'is//dbang phyug dam pa 'thob par 'gyur//dngos grub kun gyi dbang phyug gi phyag rgya shes pa'o//dus 
53a6(rgyud): kun du sna tshogs kyi//dam tshig rtog pa ste dgu pa'o////de nas kun du sna tshogs kyis//phyag rgya shes pa sgrub pa ni//kun du sna tshogs sbyor ba yis//kun du sna tshogs bshad par bya//smin ma mig dang so dang 
53a7(rgyud): 'chu ha kha dang lus dang rka lag gis//cha lugs bzhin du gar byed pas//phyag rgya thams cad yongs su grol//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal 'byor ba//lha mo kun dang 'dus pa ni//kun du sna tshogasas skal bzang po//
53a8(rgyud): rab tu bde bar 'grub par 'gyur//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//sangs rgyas thams cad byin gyis rlob//legs par byin gyis rlabs na ni//sangs rgyas kun dang 'phrad par 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam 
53b1(rgyud): sbyor bas sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam par 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam ba na//sangs rgyas thams cad rab tu 'grub//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal sbyor ba'i//mthu nyid kyis ni thams cad du
53b2(rgyud): mdor na phyag rgya thams cad ni//so so dag nas rab 'grub 'gyur//lta bas mi ngoms pa'i sku//thams cad bdag nyid ston par mdzad//legs so zhes pa grub pa ni//dam pa bdag kyang rab tu stsol
53b3(rgyud): bsam pa thams cad yongs rdzogs pa'i//grub pa kun mchog mnyam dga' ba//ji ltar 'dod pa'i longs spyod du//thams cad kyi ni bdag nyid stsol//dbang po kun las byung ba'i//mchod pa'i  
53b4(rgyud): sprin chen thams cad kyi//bkod pa'i rab 'byam sna tshogs gyis//thams cad bdag nyid mchod par mdzad//dpal ldan g.yon pa 'gying dang bcas//dpal ldan lag pa g.yas pa yi//
53b5(rgyud): pad mo khu tshur dpral bar gzhag//bdag gi mig ni gyen du lnga//'di ni rdo rje sems dpa' yi//phyag rgya thams cad rab sgrub pa'o//gyen du 'phar ba'i dngos grub dang//rab 'byam las
53b6(rgyud): rnams myur du grub//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//khams gsum dag ni ma lus pa//rgyas btab rmongs byas zos pa dang//thal bar 'gyur ba'ang 'tsho bar byed//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa'as
53b7(rgyud): de skad gsungs so//kun du sna tshogs skal pa zang po dpal rdo rje pad mo gyen du 'phar ba'i spyan zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya ste//rnam pa sna tshogs thams cad kyi phyag rgya dang phyag rgya'i lan shes pa'o/
53b8(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad 'dus pa yi//rtag tu dpag med tshe dang ldan//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa'i//bden pa'i tshig ni rab tu smra//mtha' yas pa dang mu med pa'i//rtog pa kun dang 'di yi ni
55a1(rgyud): @#//mthu dang yon tan sngags pa'i mtha'//brjod pa dag tu yod mi nus//mthu dang rang bzhin dag gis grub//ngo bo nyid kyis rtag tu 'grub//so sor rjes grub bde ba ster//'di ni gsang 
55a2(rgyud): mchog mchog gi mchog/sems can don phyir las rnams sbyong ba dang//rnam par dag pa rgyal ba mi yong mi mdzad//'di la dgegs med bdud kyang yong ma yin//rtog pa mi 'grub dar bar yod mi 'gyur//mi 
55a3(rgyud): brtan pa dang bdud du nga mi 'gyur//brtan par bya ba'i phyir yang nga mi 'dogs//gang zhig rang gi bde bas nga bsgrub pa//dngos grub mchog gis nga ni rtan par mdzad//kun du sna tshogs pas ni de skad gsungs 
55a4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rtag tu dges/rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyis legs gsungs pa//mngon par ba stod cing rab tu dges par 'gyur//rnam mang rjes chags dga' 
55a5(rgyud): ba'i pad mo rnam dag mchod pa dang//rta mchog rnal 'byor bde bas gsal bar rjes su mchod na ni//rab tu bde ba'i rnal 'byor dam pa bskal bzang dga' ba'i mchog//rab mchog bde ba'i 
55a6(rgyud): rang bzhin dam tshig bde bar rjes su grub par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas de skad gsungs so//sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro ma'i sgyu ma  
55a7(rgyud): bde ba'i mchog ces bya ba'i rtog pa//rtog pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo bskor ba chen po bde ba chen po rdzogs s+h+yo//@#//rgya gar skad du/sarba kal pa sa mu dza ya/na ma sarba b+hu ta sa ma yo ga
55a8(rgyud): d+ha kwi ni 'dzi la saM b+ha ro da ra ud ra ta na tA/bod skad du/rtog pa thams cad sdus pa zhes bya ba//sangs rgyas thams cad nga mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog rgyud phyi ma//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la 
55b1(rgyud): phyag 'tshal lo//sangs rgyas thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//rnam rtog med pa'i sems la ni//de kho na nyid nges par gnas//sems dpa' sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos
55b2(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog//gsang ba mchog gi dges pa na//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu zhugs//'dod chags chen po bde chen la//bde chen rdo rje chen po pa//rdo rje 'dzin dang lhan cig pa'i bde 
55b3(rgyud): gshegs mgon po rnams kyis zhus//thams cad mchog gyur don chen gyi//dngos grub dbang phyug rab grub phyir//sangs rgyas thams cadmnyam sbyor bas//rtog pa 'dus pa bka' bstsal pa//rnam par mi rtog 
55b4(rgyud): chos dag la//rtog pa'i don 'di ji ltar gsungs//ci'i slad du rgyud phyi ma//dang po sa mchog ji ltar lags//gdod ma tha ma med nyid las//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin te//yig 'bru ji ltar gsungs
55b5(rgyud): pa lags//sngags byung ba yang ci 'dra ste//shin tu dga' dang bzang nyid dang //rdo rje chen po gtso bo ci//sems dang sems dpa'i ting 'dzin dang //de bzhin rdo rje rdo rje 'dzin//rdo rje 'dzin nyid chen po yang //byang 
55b6(rgyud): chub spyod pa rnams ji ltar//bdud dang bdud las rgyal ba yang //byang chub sangs rgyas ji ltar lags//rdo rje rtum po mtha' yas pa'i//phyag na rdo rje yang ji ltar//dngos po rigs ni ji ltar lags//rdong dang tha ma'i rnam
55b7(rgyud): pa ci//mgon po rnam gsum ji ltar 'tshal//ji ltar thams cad mkhyen nyid lags//ji ltar dang po'i snying po 'byung //nye ba'i snying po de ji ltar//bde ba chen po rdo rje'o//don yod dam tshig ji ltar lags//dkyil 'khor ji ltar 
55b8(rgyud): gsungs par 'gyur//de'i rnam smin ci 'dra ste//thig bdab de bzhin rtog pa yi//dngos grub ji ltar bshad pa lags//gsang ba'i ji ltar de nyid ni//gang lags ci 'dra de bzhin te//dkyil 'khor gyi ni gtso bo rnams//
56a1(rgyud): @#//rigs rnams kyi ji ltar lags//gsang ba dbang skur kun de nyid//de nyid phyogs kyi phyag 'tshal yang //thams cad dgug dang mchod chen dang //rang gi byin rlabs brtson 'grus pa//de dag de nyid gangs chen yang 
56a2(rgyud): don dam pa ru dam pa ni//dam pa'i yi ge grub don du//thams cad mi gsung 'os ma lags//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog las de kho na nyid thams cad nges 
56a3(rgyud): tshig gi rtog pa ste bco brgyad pa'o////de na ston pa rdo rje 'dzin//rtog pa kun gyi gtso bo chen pos//the tsom dag ni tshogs gnas nas//do dam 'dzin pa bka' bstsal to//de la ji ltar rtog pa'i don rgyud yin 
56a4(rgyud): zhe na/rnam par mi rtog chos dag la//sems can don du yongs su rtog//de phyir rtog par yang dag bshad//rtog pa yongs su dag phyir ro//de la ji ltar rgyud phyi ma yin zhe na//rgyud ni rab tu 'brel par bshad//
56a5(rgyud): phyi ma gong du byung ba yin//de nyid gsang ba'i gsang chen dag//don yang rgyud ni phyi mar 'dod//de la ji ltar dang po'i sa//nyid kyi ye shes yin zhe na//nam mkha' kun tu rab gnas pa//rdo rje 
56a6(rgyud): kun gyi dngos po'i mchog/bde ba chen po dges pa che//dam pa'i yi ge mchog de yin//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//'di la ji ltar gdod mtha' ma'i rdo rje sems dpa' 
56a7(rgyud): yin zhe na/yi ge ka sogs ra yig mtha'//nga nya spangs nas 'jug par bya//yi ge nga sogs ga shA yig mtha'//phreng ba gnyis par yang dag bri//dbus gnas par ni a yig phreng //zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la bkod 
56a8(rgyud): te//thig le zla tshe dang ldan pa//tsheg drag zla ba gnyis yin te//dbus su rdo rje dngos gnas pa'i//a yig ma 'gyur sgom par byed//dbus su rdo rje ngo bor gnas//de dbus rtag tu sgom pa ni//'dod chags chen po 
56b1(rgyud): bde ba che//'di de sangs rgyas thams cad kyis//sangs rgyas ring por grags pa yin//de la 'gro ba g.yo mi g.yo//thams cad yang dag 'byung ba ni//nam mkha' las byung mtshan ma'i phyir//gdod ma tha ma 
56b2(rgyud): med mchog yin//'jam pa chen po dngos sems ni//rdo rje sems dpa' zhes 'dod do//de la bcom ldan 'das de gang yin zhe na//dngos grub chen po kun gyi mchog//dbang phyug chen po la sogs lha//rgyal po
56b3(rgyud): rdo rje 'dzin chen po//dga' ba dam pa zad med yin//de la ji ltar yi ge rtog pa yin zhe na//yi ge a sogs gshA mtha' ni//yig 'bru yang dag bshad pa ste//mi bzad dga' ba'i rdo rje can//de nyid kun
56b4(rgyud): don mtshan nyid phyir//de la ji ltar sngan dbyung ba yin zhe na//rdo rje sems sogs 'khor lo la//gang dag sngags su cher bshad pa//de dag byung ba'i don du ni//rdo rje can gyis yang dag bka'
56b5(rgyud): bstsal pa//gur gum sogs pas byugs pa la//bri sku la sogs brgyan nas ni//bla re la sogs bres nas su//rtag tu re'u dmig bzhi rtsems te//gru bzhi gzhal yas khang bya ste//re dmig 
56b6(rgyud): gzhi ni 'bar bar bya//re'u dmig sum bcu pa la//bcu dgu dang ni ldan par bya//zla tshes stan la gnas pa la//de la lnga thig le yis//spyi bor gtsug la rtag pa'o//ka dang sha zhes bya ba ni//dbus kyi re mig la
56b7(rgyud): bri'o//de'i phyi rol gnas brgyad dag//mtshams dang phyogs kyi re mig la//dbang ldan la sogs rim pa ru//yang dang sha'i sde bkod te//me mtshams la sogs re'u mig la//ma ning yig 'bru bcu po dang/
56b8(rgyud): a yig sa bon la rten nas//sa bon bcu drug bkod par bya//dbang ldan la sogs re mig la//ka yig la sogs rim par bkod//de ltar bzhin du bkod bya ste//yig 'bru dam pa phyung bar bya//shin du
57a1(rgyud): @#//bde chen rgyal po ni//sbyor ba thams cad dbang phyug gtso//yig 'khrul mchog ni dbyung bar bya //re'u mig nyi shu rtsa bzhi la//gnas pa zla phyed gtsug phud zhal//nyi shu rtsa bzhi 'og gi drug
57a2(rgyud): bde ba rdo rje can 'byung ngo//ri mig bcu bzhi zhes pa las//gnas pa tseg drag sbyor ba bya//sum bcu rtsa brgyad sbyor ba yis//de ni bzhi pa rnam par bshad//bde ba chen po rgyal chen po//lha mo rnams
57a3(rgyud): kyi dam pa dag//re mig bcu gcig la gnas pa//thig le nga ro yang dag ldan//re'u mig brgyad pa la gnas//bcu dgu dang ni ldan pa ste//de 'og sum bcu rtsa bzhi dang//thig le nga ro rgyan pa'o//re mig
57a4(rgyud): nyi shu rtsa bzhi la//gnasapa tsheg drag sbyor ba'o//mig gnyis pa la bkod pa yi//thig le nga ro ldan pa'o//gling bu la sogs 'dus pa yi//sngags phyung ba ni mchog yin te
57a5(rgyud): re'u mig sum bcu rtsa brgyad la//gnas pa thig le nga ror ldan//dbus nas nyi shu rtsa gcig ni//dbus nas bco lnga zla ba can//re'u mig sum bcu rtsa gcig gnas//dbus nas bco
57a6(rgyud): lnga zla ba can//bcu drug pa ni nyid dang sbyar//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa gcig gnas//bcu gcig zla ba dang ldan te//lha mo gling bu la sogs kyis//sngags phyung rmad du byung ba'o//re'u mig bcu pa la gnas dang
57a7(rgyud): bcu gcig pa yang de bzhin te//bcu gnyis pa ni nyid dang sbyar//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa gsum gnas//bcu drug pa yang bzhi pa ste//me tog la sogs 'dus pa yi/'di ni sngags phyung mchog yin no
57a8(rgyud): rta 'gri na 'phang 'tho ljang gu sogs//dngos po 'byung ba'i sa bon nyid//rdo rje sems dpa' dkyil 'khor gyi//tshogs kyi yi ge 'byung ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//re dmig gnyis pa can kho na//zla tshes gtsug dang ldan
57b1(rgyud): pa'i zhal;;rnam par snang mdzad rgyal chen po//kun mchog pa'i dbang phyug yin//re'u mig bco lnga brgyad gnas pa//thig le nga ro ldan pa'o//re'u mig bcu dgu la gnas pa//thig le nga ro
57b2(rgyud): re'u mig nyi shu rtsa lngar ldan//thig le nga ros brgyan pa'o//dbus na nyi shu pa nyid la//thig le nga ror brgyan pa'o//chos gos la sogs bzhi dag gis//sa bon 'byung ba mchog 'di yin//re'u mig nyi shu
57b3(rgyud): rtsa gzhir gnas//thig le nga ros rgyan pa'o//re'u mig bdun pa de bzhin yang//dbus nas bco lnga pa dang ldan//thig le nga ro ldan pa ni//byad du sil snyan cha lang ngo//dbus na nyi shu rtsa gcig la
57b4(rgyud): gnas pa bcu dgu dang ldan pa//thig le nga ro ldan ni/rdo rje rin chen cher spyod do//dbus nas bco brgyad pa dag nyid//thig le nga ro rgyan pa ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi dkyil 'khor la//chos kyi dung gi
57b5(rgyud): phyag rgya che zhes bcom ldan 'das/dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//rnam par snang mdzad kyis chos kyi yi ge 'byang ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//nyi shu rtsa bzhi re'u mig la gnas pa nyi shu
57b6(rgyud): pa ldan ni khrag 'thung zhes bya ba'i rgyal po che//tseg drag lhag chad gyur pa'o//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa bzhi la//gnas pa 'og ni nyi shu ldan//re'u mig nyi shu gnas pa dang//sbyor bas tseg drag ldan pa'o
57b7(rgyud): re mig nyi shu rtsa bzhi la//gnas pa dbus na bcu gcig ldan//re'u mig bcu gnyis sbyor ba yi//tsheg drag ldan par 'dod pa'o//re mig bcu bzhi la gnas pa//'og gi 'dren pa nyi shu gcig//nyi shu rtsa 
57b8(rgyud): gnyis tseg drag can//gsum pa can du bshad pa'o//re mig bcu bzhil gnas pa//bcu gnyis pa dang ldan pa la//tseg drag dang ni mnyam sbyor ba//lha mo snod chen mo'i 'dod//re mig nyi shu rtsa bzhi la //gnas pa dbus
58a1(rgyud): @#//na gnyis pa can//de bzhin thig le nga ror ldan//thig le 'byung gnas gnyis pa can//dbus nas bcu pa gang yin pa//rtag tu a med par 'gyur ba//dmar chen sogs pa'i lha mo
58a2(rgyud): yi||bzhi po dang ni phyung ba'o//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa lnga la//gnas pa thig ler bcas pa'o//dbus las drug pa gang yin pa//thig le nga ror ldan pa'o//re'u mig de bzhin bcu bdun pa//thig le nga ro rgyas 
58a3(rgyud): pa'o|lha mo mda' rnon 'dzin sogs kyis//sa bon 'byung ba de ngo mtshar che//lha mo me tog ma sogs kyi//sa bon 'byung ba sngon stan yin//gang dag sgo bsrungs bstan pa yang//gzugs mi mthun pa
58a4(rgyud): kho na'o||zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpa'as de skad gsungs so//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi chos kyi yi ge 'byung ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//re'u mig
58a5(rgyud): nyi shu rtsa bzhi la//gnas pa bcu dgu bdan la gnas//tseg drag sbyor ba ni/pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug yin//re'u mig bcu bzhi la gnas pa yi//tseg drag gnyis rtsi ba de bzhin no//re'u mig nyi shu
58a6(rgyud): rtsa bzhi la//gnas pa dbus nas dgu pa ldan//rtag par thig le dang ldan na ni//gnyis pa yi ni yongs su grags//re'u mig sum bcu rtsa gcig la//gnas pa nga ro thig ler rgyan//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa bzhi
58a7(rgyud): la|gnas pa dbus gnas bcu gsum ldan//tseg drag can ni bzhi pa yi//khro gnyer ma'i rgyud las grags//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa ba zhir ldan//dbus nas dgug pa thig ler bcas//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa
58a8(rgyud): gzhir gnas||dbus nas bco lnga dang ldan no//re'u mig bcu gcig la gnas pa//tseg drag gi ni sbyor ba'o//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa bzhIr gnas//sum bcu rtsa brgyad pa dang ldan//tseg drag mnyam par sbyor ba ni//pad ma gar 
58b1(rgyud): dbang mchog yin no//lha mo gling bu ma sogs nyid//de bzhin lha mo me tog ma la sogs//lha mo lcags kyu ma sogs kyang//rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs kyi//dkyil 'khor la ni skyed zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje
58b2(rgyud): sems dpas gsungs te//pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug gi dkyil 'khor gyi chos kyi yi ge 'byung ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//dbus nas nyi shu rtsa gcig pa//bcu gcig pa dang ldan pa ste//bcu dgu
58b3(rgyud): zla ba gtsug ldan ni//rdo rje nyi ma 'byung ba yin//dbus nas bcu gsum gang yin pa//zla tshes thig le gtsug gi zhal//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa bzhi la//gnas pa bcu dgu dang ldan pa'i//re'u
58b4(rgyud): mig nyi shu gnas dang sbyar//tseg drag ldan pa gnyis pa'o//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa ba zhir gnas//dbus nas bcu gsuM pa dang ldan//tseg drag lhan cig can de ni//gsum pa yi ni grag
58b5(rgyud): pa yin|re'u mig nyi shu rtsa bzhir gnas//bcu gcig dang ni thig ler ldan//re'u mig bcu bzhi la gnas pa//tseg drag ldan pa(lan) pa('a)dod pa'o//re'u mig rtsa bzhir gnas pa//dbus(sa)
58b6(rgyud): nas dgu dang zla ba'o//re'u mig sum bcu rtsa gcig gnas//thig le nga ros rgyan pa'o//re'u mig nyi shurtsa bzhir gnas//dbus nas bachu gsum pa dang ldan//tseg dag mnyam par ldan pa ni//
58b7(rgyud): rdo rje nyi ma'i lha dag yin//g+ha mo gling bu ma sogs kun//rtsa ba'i dkyil 'khor sbyor skyes yin//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs te/rdo rje nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi chos
58b8(rgyud): kyi yi ge 'byung ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//re'u mig nyi shur+ca bzhir gnas//nyi shu rtsa bdun sbyor ba ste//zla tshes thig ler bcug can ni//yul 'khor thams cad 'dul ba'o//re'u mig bcu bzhi
59a1(rgyud): //gnas pa bcu dgu pa dang ldan pa ste//nyi shu tseg drag sbyor ba ste//sna tshogs pad ma'i brjod pa yin//re'u mig nyi shu rtsa bzhi pa//gnas pa tsheg drag dang ldan pa'o//rta'i rgyal
59a2(rgyud): po'i dkyil 'khor la//rdo rje rgya gram zhes grags so//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal bde ba chen po rdo rje sems dpas gsungs te//rta mchog gi chos kyi yi ge 'byung ba'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//sangs
59a3(rgyud): rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog la//chos kyi yi ge 'byung ba'i rtog pa ste//bcu dgu pa'o//ri mo gnyis kyi dkyil 'khor gyi phyi rol pad ma 'dab ma brgyad bri//de'i nang du
59a4(rgyud): rnal 'byor pas//a yig la sogs kyang bri bar bya//shar phyogs mdab mar bka'i sde//lho'i mdab ma tsa'i sde//nub phyogs 'dab mar ta'i sde//ta'i sde ni de bzhin byang //sde pa lnga'i dbus su
59a5(rgyud): ni/yi ge k+sha dang lhan cig 'dod//dbang ldan 'dab ma nas brtsams te//ya ra wa la rnam par gzhag//dbang ldan 'dab ma'i phyi rol du//yi ge sha ni 'jug par bya//me mtshams 'dab ma'i phyi rol du
59a6(rgyud): yi ge sha ni 'jug par bya //bden dral 'dab ma'i phyi rol du/yi ge pa ni 'jug par bya//rlung 'tshams 'dab ma'i phyi rol tu//yi ge ha ni 'jug par bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal bde ba chen po rdo rje 
59a7(rgyud): sems dpas gsungs so//dbus kyi mtha' ru gang yin pa//thig le dang ldan yi ge oM//rlung 'tshams phyi rol gnas gang //sgra yig a ni sbyor bar bya//bden gral phyi rol gnas pa la//'og tu sgra yig dbu sbyor ro
59a8(rgyud): shar gyi 'dab ma gnyis par ni dbus las dbus ma de bzhin no//lho nas de bzhin dbebs ma la//ma mtshams 'dab ma dad ldan no//bden gral phyi rol sa zung nas//de nas byang gi dang po'o//dbus nas u
59b1(rgyud): ma sbyin nas su//de tshe 'od kyi gung bur 'gyur//a yig tseg drag dang ldan pa//de'i mtha' ru de tshar 'gyur//lho nas dbu ma gang yin pa//tseg drag dang ni ldan pa'o//bden gral phyi rol nas 
59b2(rgyud): bzung nas//byang gi dang po gang yin pa//dbus la dbu ma gzungs nas su//'od kyi gong bu mtha' ru 'gyur//de nas thig le sbyin nas su//'od kyi gong ba mtha' ru 'gyur//de nas/thig le sbyin byas nas//rdo rje sems
59b3(rgyud): dpa' grub par 'gyur//zhes chags pa chen po rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//lho nas dbu ma gang yan pa//tseg drag dang nildan par bya//dbus nas dbu ma de bzhin no//lho nas dbu ma yang yin no//me
59b4(rgyud): mtshams 'dab ma dang ldan pas//rtag tu spyod par 'gyur ba yin//byang nas dbu ma bzung nas su//me mtshams dang ni shin du sbyor//sgra yig i dang ldan par ru//rtag par zhi bar 'gyur ba yin//
59b5(rgyud): me mtshams phyi rol bzung nas su//nub kyi dang po ldan par bya//sgra yig i de ldan nas su bzu//'od kyi gong bu lnga ba yin//bden gral phyi rol gnas pa la//a yig sgra dang ldan par bya//
59b6(rgyud): de bzhin dbang ldan 'dab ma la//sgra yig e ni sbyor bar bya//shar gyi dang po dad pa ni//de nas dbu ma'i mtha' ma la//nub kyi dang po bzung nas su//a med rtag tu 'gyur bar bya//de dag zlas par byas pa 
59b7(rgyud): yis//'da' gzhu rab tu 'grub pa yin//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa bzung nas//sgra yig dbu ni sbyor bar bya//thig les nga ro mnon nas su//
59b8(rgyud): de nas dbus nas dbu ma'o//lho nas dbu ma bzung nas su//me mtshams pa dang sbyor bar bya//shar nas dang po bzung nas su/yi ge e dang ldan par bya//bden gral 'dab mar gnas pa gang //yi ge e ni sbyor bar bya//
60a1(rgyud): @#//shar gyi dang po bzung nas su//sgra yig i ni sbyor ba'o//bden gral 'dab mar gnas pa la//sgra yig i ni sbyor pa'o//rlung mtshams phyi rol nas bzung nas//sgra yig dbu ni sbyor bar bya//thig
60a2(rgyud): le nga ros mnyam mnon na//skal ngan skal bzangs gyur pa yin//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//dbus nas dbu ma bzung nas su//thig le yis ni rgyan par bya//de bzhin
60a3(rgyud): dbus las dbu ma yang//lho dang dbu ma de bzhin te//me'i mtshams pa mnyam par ldan//yi ge u yis 'du bar bya//nub las tha ma bzung nas su//sgra yig ye ni sbyar bar bya//bden gral phyi rol pa bzung 
60a4(rgyud): nas//dbu ma'i tha ma dang ldan par//me mtshams lan gnyis brjod nas su//nub nas 'gyur byas nas ni//rtag tu a med brgyan pa ni//rdo rje khwa tri 'dzin mo yin//zhes bcom ldan

60a6(rgyud): shar las dang po bzung nas su//sgra yigai ni sbyor bar bya//dbus las mtha' ma bzung nas su//sgra yag e ne sbyor bar bya//dbang ldan phyi rol pa bzung nas//dbus las dbu ma ldan par bya//me 'tshaMs

60b1(rgyud): sems dpa' la sogs pa'i lha mo bzhi yis gsungs pa//oM yig thig le dang ldan pa//a yig thig le yang dag ldan//dbus las mtha' ma bzung nas su//yi ge dbu dang ldan par bya//byang nas dang po bzhi pa'o
