60b4(rgyud): yig e dang sbyar bar bya//shar las bzhi pa gang yin pa//sgra yig e dang sbyor ba'o//shar las gsum pa gang yin pa//sgra yig u dang sbyar ba'o//bden bral 'dab ma la gnas pa 
60b5(rgyud): sgra yig u ni sbyor ba ste//nan tan lan gnyis zla ba'o//dbus nas bzhi pa gang yin pa //u yig me mtshaMs pa dang ldan//thig le nga ro lan gnyis so//e yig thig le dang ldan pa//
60b6(rgyud): rlung mtshams phyi rol la gnas pa//tseg drag dang ni mnyam par ldan//dbang ldan phyi rol la gnas pa//me mtshams 'dab ma can de bzhin//bzhi pa thob pa yang min te//byang nas gsum pa gang yin pa//dbus
60b7(rgyud): la dbu ma 'gyur bar bya//lho nas dbu ma pa la yang //me mtshams 'dab ma tsan dang ldan//sgra yig i dang rtsi bar bya//a'i thig le dang ldan pa//lan gnyis su ni brjod bya ste//lha mo gsum pa kha na'o//
60b8(rgyud): sngags pa 'byung bar grags pa yin//a yig thig le dang ldan pa //zla tshes thig le gtsug gi zhal//shar nas gnyis pa gang yin pa//dbus nas dbu ma de bzhin du//lho nas dbu ma pa yang //me 'tshams 'dab ma 
61a1(rgyud): @#//tsam 'byor pa//dus thams cad du sbyor bar bya//dbang ldan phyi rol pa de bzhin//sgra yig e ni sbyor bar bya//de kho na'i phyi rol pa bzung nas//sgra yig i dang ldan byas te//me mtshams 'dab ma 
61a2(rgyud): la gnas pa'i//sgra yig i yis brgyan par bya//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa gzung nas//thig le oM yig sbyar byas te//lan gnyis su ni brjod bya'o//de ni dpag sam shing sogs lha//rdo rje snying po pad ma dang //
61a3(rgyud): rdo rje srid len gyi bu mo dang //rdo rje dbang phyug ma dang //'dod pa'i dbang mo rnams zhes pa//lha mo bzhi yin no//oM yig thig le dang ldan pa//yi ge oM yis sbyar bar bya//rlung 'tshams phyi rol la gnas pa//me 
61a4(rgyud): 'tshams 'dab ma can de bzhin//yi ge i yis mnyam ldan te//byang las dang po 'gyur byas te//dbus las dbu ma de bzhin du me 'tshams 'dab ma can dang 'byor//lho'i dbu ma pa yang ste/
61a5(rgyud): yi ge i dang mnyam ldan te//dbus las dbu ma yang 'gyur nas//bden bral 'dab ma la gnas pa//sgra yig a ni sbyor/bar bya//bden bral phyi rol la gnas pa//sgra yig aM ni sbyor byas te/
61a6(rgyud): byang nas tha ma gang yin pa//sgra yig i ni sbyor bar bya//byang nas dang po gang yin pa//mi mtshams pa'i brdan dang ldan//nub nas dang po gang yin pa//rtag par a med 'gyur bar bya//gdod ma tha ma med pa'i mchog
61a7(rgyud): lha mo rdo rje pi wang ma'i 'o//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa bzung nas//sgra yig i ni sbyor par bya//me mtshams 'dab ma gnyis pa nyid//sgra yig i dang ldan byas te//byang nas dang por 'gyur bar bya//dbus nas dbu ma 
61a8(rgyud): bzung nas su//lho nas dbu ma bzung bar bya//me mtshams 'dab pa dang sbyor nas//sdu bar bya ba yang yin te//shar nas gsum pa gang yin pa//sgra yig aM ni sbyor bar bya//byang na dang po bzung nas su//sgra yig e 
61b1(rgyud): ni sbyor byas te;nga ro bzhi pa dang ldan pa//rtag par lan gsum brjod bya'o//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa bzung nas//sgra yig e ni sbyor bar bya//me mtshams 'dab ma pa la yang//sgra yig i ni ldan byas te//
61b2(rgyud): byang nas dngos por 'gyur bar bya//dbus nas dbu ma bzung nas su//me mtshams 'dab ma can lhan cig//lho nas dbu ma 'khyud byas te//rlung 'tshams phyi rol la gnas pa//sgra yig a ni sbyor bar bya//de bzhin bden dral
61b3(rgyud): phyes la;sgra yig e ni sbyor ba'i phyir//rlung mtshams phyi rol ba de nyid//malan gnyis su ched du brjod par bya//dbus nas dbu mabazung nas su//thig le gcig pa sbyor byas te//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog la//
61b4(rgyud): dbyings kyi lha mor grags pa yin//rlung mtshams phyi rolapa bzung nas//sgra yig ai ni sbyor bar bya//me mchams 'dab mar gnas pa yang//sgra yig e dang ldan par bya//dbus las dbu ma de bzhin du
61b5(rgyud): me mtshams 'dab ma dang 'byor te//lho nas dbu ma pa dang yang//byang nas tha ma bzung bya ste//me mtshams 'dab ma ldan byas nas//sgra yig i ni sbyor bar bya//dbang ldan 'dab ma can sbyor nas//
61b6(rgyud): de tshe brjod pa yang byas te//de bzhin dbus las dbu ma yang//sgra yig e ni sbyor bar bya//dbus las dang por de bzhin du//de ni lan gnyis brjod par bya//lho nas bzhi pa bzung nas su//nga ro tshul gyis ldan par bya//
61b7(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa'i dkyil 'khor la//rdza rnga ma zhes grags pa yin//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//gling bu dang ni pi bang kun//rdo rje sems dpa'i dkyil khor la//grA yi lha mo mchog grub pa'o
61b8(rgyud): a yig dang po byang nas su//dbus nas dbu ma pa la ya//me mtshams 'dab ma can ldan pa//lho nas dbu ma 'gyur bar bya//dbus las mtha' ma de bzhin du//sgra yig u ni sbyor bar bya//me mtshams phyi rol pa bzung nas
62a1(rgyud): @#//dbus las dang yang yin ste//e yig dang ni mnyam por ldan//'dus nas me tog ma byung ngo//a yig dang po byang nas su//dbus nas dbu ma (ba)yang ste//me mtshams 'dab ma dang ldan par//
62a2(rgyud): lho las dbu ma yang yin te//byang las bzhi pa gang yin pa//sgra yig dbu dang sbyor bar bya//dbus las dang po bzung nas su//rdo rje spos me rnam grags so//oM yig dang po byang nas su //dbus las dbu ma pa
62a3(rgyud): yang ste me mtshams 'dab ma can ldan par//lho nas dbu mar gyur bar bya//yig ge a las mnyam ldan te//bden gral 'dab ma gnas pa la//sgra yig oM ni sbyor bar bya//shar gyi dang po de bzhin du//e yig ldan
62a4(rgyud): par gsum pa'o//a yig dang po byang na su//dbus las dbu ma de bzhin yang//me 'tshams 'dab ma dang ldan par//lho las dbu ma 'gyur bya ste//shar gyi gsum po de la yang//thig le dang
62a5(rgyud): ni 'byor par bya//byang las bzhi pa gang yen pa//yig ge e yig lhan cig 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//rdo rje me tog ma dang/rdo rje spos me ma dang/rdo rje mar ma dang /rdo rje
62a6(rgyud): dri chab ma zhes pa'i lha mo bzhi phyi yis gsungs so//oM yig thig le dang ldan par//dbus las dbu ma pa yang ste//me mtshams 'dab ma dang lhan cig/lho las dbu ma 'gyur bar bya//a yig thig le mnyam ldan pa
62a7(rgyud): rtag par brjod par bya ba ste//shar las dang po bzung nas su//sgra yig u ni sbyor bar bya//dbang ldan phyi rol pa bzung nas//sgra yig i ni sbyor bar bya//tsheg drag mnyam por ldan pa ni//lho nas dbu mar 'gyur
62a8(rgyud): ba'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsung so//oM yig thig le dang ldan par//dbus la dbu mar 'gyur byas te//me mtshams pa dang mnyam ldan te//lho nas dbu ma pa yang ste//dbus las
62b1(rgyud): dang po bzung nas su//sgra yig a ni sbyor bar bya//dbang ldan phyi rol pa bzung nas//sgra yig a ni sbyor ba bya//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa bzung nas/thig le tshul dang 'byor ba'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das
62b2(rgyud): dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//oM yig thig le dang ldan par//dbus las dbu ma 'gyur bya ste//me mtshams 'dab ma can ldan zhing//lho las dbu ma 'gyur bar bya//dbang ldan pha rol pa bzung nas
62b3(rgyud): thig le nga ro brgyan byas te//shar gyi dang po bzung nas su//skra yig dang ni mnyam ldan zhing//bden dral 'dab ma can du 'gyur//de bzhin dbus las dbu ma yang//dpral bar thig le brgyan pa'o//bcom ldan 'das dpal
62b4(rgyud): dpal rdo rje sems dpas bsungs so//oM yig thigs le dang ldan par//dbas las dbu ma yang yin te//me mtshams 'dab ma cang lta zhig//lho las dbu ma 'gyu bar bya//shar las bzhi pa gang yin pa
62b5(rgyud): thig le nga ros rnam par brgyan//nub las dang por bzung nas su//sgra yig i ni sbyor bar bya//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa bzung nas//tshig drag dang ni mnyam ldan zhing //oM yig lhan cig 'gyur
62b6(rgyud): ba'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal bde ba chen po dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//rta mgrin 'phang 'tho'i lcags kyu ma dang//phag 'go 'che zhags ma dang//nyi zla lcags sgrog ma dang//shi ba gso bI
62b7(rgyud): dril bu ma zhes bya ba//sgo bsrungs ma bzhi gsungs ste//dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i dkyil 'khor 'byung ba'i sngags chos kyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//oM yige thig le dang ldan par//dbus las bzhi pa gang yin pa//tshig
62b8(rgyud): drag u yig ldan pa ste//shar las gsum pa can la yang//thig le nga ro gnon pa ni//thub chen rnam mdzad kyi'o//oM yig thig le dang ldan par/dbus las dbu ma yang yin te//me mtshams 'dab ma dang lhan
63a1(rgyud): @#//cig :lho las dbu mar 'gyur byas te//bden dral phyi rol pa bzung nas//byang nas dang po yang ste//dbus las dbu ma pa ldan par//dbang ldan phyi rol la gnas pa//thig le nga ro ldan pa'o
63a2(rgyud): oM yig thig le dang ldan par//dbus las dbu ma yang yin te//me mtshams 'dab ma dang lhan cig//lho las dbu ma 'gyur bar bya//me mtshams 'dab mal gnas bzhin//byang las dang po 'gyur byas te
63a3(rgyud): byang las tha ma dang ldan par//gzhug tu me mtshams 'dab ma can//zla tshes thig le gtsug gi zhal//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungaso//oM yig thig le dang ldanapar//dbus las
63a4(rgyud): dbu ma par gyur te//me mtshams 'dab pa can lhan cig//lho las dngus ma pa yang yin//byang nas bzhi pa gang yin pa/dbus las mtha' ma pa yang ste//me mtshams pa yis mgo mnon bya//dbus las
63a5(rgyud): tha ma gang yin pa//zla tshes thig le gtsug ga zhal//zhes bco ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sesams dpas gsungs so//oM yig thig le rang ldan par//dbus las bu ma 'gyur byas te//me mtshams
63a6(rgyud): 'dab ma can dang lhan cig//lho la dbu ma 'gyur byas te//shar las dang po bzung nas su//me mtshams can gyis mnon pa ste//dbus ma tha ma pa yang 'dod//byang las gnyis pa gang yin pa//zla tshes thog le gtsug
63a7(rgyud): gi zhal//zhes bcom ldana'adas dpal rdo rje sems pas gsungs so//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa gzung ste//thig le nga ro ldan par bya//bden dral phyi rol pa bzung ste//dbus las dbu ma dang ldan par//byang las dang por 'gyur bar
63a8(rgyud): bya/dbus las dbu ma de bzhin du//me mtshams pa la e yig ldan//lho las dbu ma 'gyur byas te/lho las gnyis pa gang yin pa //thig le dang ni 'byor bar bya//dbus las dbu ma de bzhinadu//me mtshams 'dab ma
63b1(rgyud): can lhan cig;lho las dbu ma 'gyur byas shing//me mtshams 'dab mar gnas pa nyid//byang las dang por de bzhin du//byang las tha ma dang 'byor ba//sgra yig e dang sbyor ba'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das
63b2(rgyud): dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//byang las dang po bzung nas su//thig le me mtshams yang dag ldan//dbus las dbu ma de bzhin du//me mtshams pa dang ldan pa la//lho las dbu ma 'gyur bar bya
63b3(rgyud): dbus las dang po bzung nas sa//byang las dbu ma yang yin te//dbus las tha ma dang ldan pa//e yig lhan cig 'gyur ba'o//zhes bcom ldana'adas dpal rdo rje sems dpas bsungs so//byang las
63b4(rgyud): bzhi pa gang yin pa//thig le nga ro ldan par bya//dbus las dbu ma de bzhin du//sgra yig e ni sbyor byas te;dbang ldan phyi rol pa bzung nas//dbus las dbu ma pa dang ldan//de bzhin
63b5(rgyud): dbus las dbu ma yang//e yig me mtshams yang dag ldan lho las dbu ma pa 'gyur ro//zhes bcom ldan 'das bde ba chen po dpal rdo rje sems dpasagasungs so//dang po sangs rgyas dbyangs
63b6(rgyud): ma dang;de bzhin gnyis pa dpa' bo ma//kun du bzang po gsum pa ste//bzhi pa zad med snod ma ste//dpal mo sangs rgyas su ldan ma//de bzhin gzugs ma zhal mdzes pa//rin chen ma ni bdun pa ste//brgyad pa
63b7(rgyud): nam mkha'i spyan ma'o//gling bu ma sogs bsdus pa dang//sgo bsrungs ma sogs yang sdus te//me tog ma sogs bsdus pa ni//dngos po dkyil 'khor la skyed do//zhes bcom ldan 'dasa'adod chags chen po 
63b8(rgyud): dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs te/de bzhin gshegs pa dkyil 'khor 'byung ba'i sngags kyi tshogs kyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//oM yig thig le dang ldan par//yi ge i yis ldan pa ni//rlung mtshams phyi rol
64a1(rgyud): //gnas pa dang //me mtshams 'dab mar gnas pa nyid//sgra yig u dang sbyor bar bya//shar las dang po de bzhin du//dbus las dbu ma yang yin te//me mtshams 'dab ma
64a2(rgyud): can ldan pa//lho las dbu mar 'gyur byas te//bden dral phyi rol pa bzung ste//dbus las tha ma pa yang bzung //dbang ldan 'dab mar gnas pa nyid //yi ge i dang mnyam ldan te//
64a3(rgyud): rlung mtshams phyi rol gnas pa'o//tshig drag gis ni mnyam por ldan//de ni bcu par yongs grags te//bden dral phyi rolaganas pa la//me mtshams 'dab ma can dang ldan//dbus las dbu ma
64a4(rgyud): 'gyur nas su//byang nas dbu ma de bzhin ste//sgra yig u ni sbyor bar bya//me mtshams phyi rol pa de bzhin//nub las dang po pa dang ldan//bden bral phyi rol pa la yang //dbus ma'i tha ma yang yin te//
64a5(rgyud): dbang ldan 'dab mar gnasapa ni//yi ge u dang mnyam ldan te//dbus las dbu ma par gyur nas//me mtshams rngan la dang ldan pa ste//byang las dbu ma 'gyur byas te//
64a6(rgyud): 'dren pa yi ge pa dang ldan//dbus las dang po bzung nas su//me mtshams stan dang ldan par bya//dbus nas bzhi pa gang yin pa//thig le dang ni yang dag ldan//lho nas dbus ma de bzhin du//
64a7(rgyud): shar las dang po pa yang yin//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa bzung nas//thig le u yig ldan par bya//dbus las gsum pa gang yin pa//nub kyi dang po a med par//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems
64a8(rgyud): dpas gsungs so//rlung mtshams phyi rol ba bzung nas//thig le uyig dang ldan te//dbus las dngu ma de bzhin du//me mtshams stan dang lhan cig pa'i//lho las dbu ma pa 'gyur te//shar las
64b1(rgyud): gsum pa gang yin pa//sgra yig i dang sbyor bar bya//rlung mtshams phyi rol gnas pa la//sgra yig e yis rgyan pa ste//dbang ldan pad ma bdan can ni//lnga par yongs su drags pa ste//bden bral phyi rol la 
64b2(rgyud): gnas pa//sgra yig i dang spyor bar bya//byang las bzhi pa gang yin pa//sgra yi i ni sbyor ba ste//dbus las dang po bzung nas su//me mtshams 'dab ma can dang 'dra//dbus las tha ma yang yin//
64b3(rgyud): dbang ldan 'dab mar la gnas pa//sgra yig o ni sbyor bar bya//dbang ldan phyi rol gnas pa gang//dbus las dbu ma dang ldan te//me mtshams 'dab ma la gnas pa//sgra yig aM ni sbyor bar yin//
64b4(rgyud): rlung mtshams phyi rol gnasapa yi//me mtshams bdan dang ldan pa ste//tsheg drag yige i ldan pa//dang po yongs su grags pa'o//rlung mtshams phyi rol nas pa la//thig le yig ge u sbyor te//mkhas 
64b5(rgyud): pas lan gnyis brjod par bya//dbus las dbu ma yang bzung te lho las dbu ma de bzhin du//me mtshams 'dab ma cad dang ldan//lho las dang po bzung nas pa//thig le dang ni mnyam por ldan//nub nas
64b6(rgyud): dbu ma bzung nas su//yi ge e dang mnyam ldan zhing //dbang ldan phyi rol gang yin pa//dbus nas dbu ma dang ldan te/me 'tshams 'dab ma la gnas pa //sgra yig i ni sbyor bar bya//shar las gnyis pa gang
64b7(rgyud): yin pa||nub las dang po bzung nas su//dbus las dbu ma ldan par bya//thig le a yig ldan par bya//bcu par yongs su drags pa ste//shar las gsum pa gang yin pa//sgra yig i dang sbyor bar bya//dbus

65a1(rgyud): @#//shar las dang po bzung nas su//yi ge a dang ldan pa/dbus las dang po 'gyur byas te//bden bral pad mar dbus gnas pa//yi ge a yis ldan pa'o//dbus las tha ma 'gyur ba'o//bden 
65a2(rgyud): bral pad mar dbus gnas ba//sgra yig a dang sbyor ba yi//dbus las tha ma yang yin te//shar las dang po bzunganas su//sgra yig e yis sbyor bar bya//me mtshams 'dab ma la gnas pa//a

65a4(rgyud): gnyis bsags yi ge u dang ldan//rlung mtshams phyi rol gnas pa la//byang las tha ma ldan byas te//tsheg drag mnyam por ldan pa la//sgra yig i dang sbyor bar bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal

65a7(rgyud): lho'i dbu ma de bzhin du//lho las bzhi pa gang yin pa//tsheg drag yi ge i ldan pa//'dus pa gsum pa yin pas so//rlung mtshams phyir rol pa bzung nas//thi ge la u yig sbyor bar bya//dbus la dbu ma
65a8(rgyud): de bzhin yang //me mtshams pa bstan ldan pa yi//lho yi dbu ma 'gyur bar bya//dbus las ngang po bzung nas su//sgra yigamanyam por ldan pa yi//byang gi dang po yang yin te//shar las dang po bzung ba la//sgra yig
65b1(rgyud): i ni sbyor bar bya//lho las dbu ma de bzhin du//tsheg drag e yig ldan pa'o//tsheg drag ni rdo rje rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi'o//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa bzung nas//thig le u yig sbyor bya ste//dbus las dbu ma bzung ba

65b3(rgyud): nub kyi mtha' ma de bzhin du//sgra yig i dang sbyor bar bya//dbus las mtha' ma yang yin te//nub las dang po bzung nas su//e yig med par 'gyur bya'o//rlung mtshams phyi rolapa gzung nas//thig le
65b4(rgyud): u yig sbyor bar bya//dbus la dbu ma de bzhin te//me mtshams pa bstan ldan payi//lho las dbu ma 'gyar bar bya//dbang ldan phyi rol pa nyid pa//sgra yig u dang sbyor bar bya//bden
65b5(rgyud): bral dmub mar gnas pa yang //sgra yig u dang sbyor par bya shar las gsum pa gang yin pa//i yig me mtshams pa dang ldan//dbus las bzhi pa gang yin pa//thig le i yig ldan par bya//
65b6(rgyud): byang las dbu ma phul nas su//de nas dbus nas mtha' ma ste//nub kyi dang po rtag tu ni//a yig med par brjod par bya//zhes dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa bzung
65b7(rgyud): nas//thig le u yig sbyor bar bya//dbyus las dbu ma de bzhin du//me mtshams dang po gang yin pa//gnyis par ru na sbyor byas nas//thig le i yig ldanapar bya//de yang drug par 'gyur ba ste//byang las dbu
65b8(rgyud): ma de bzhin du//dbu ma'i tha ma yang yin te//nub las dang po rtag tu ni//u yig med par 'gyur ba'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpags gsungs so//rlung ma tshams phyi rol pa bzung nas//thig
66a1(rgyud): /le u yig ldan par bya//dbus las dbu ma bzung bar bya//me mtshams stan dang ldan pa yis//lho las dbu ma 'gyur byas te//yi ge a dang ldan par bya//dbus la sa tha ma bzung 
66a2(rgyud): nas su//bden bral 'dab mar gnas pa yang //dbus las dang po bzung nas su//sgra yig a ni sbyor bar bya//dpal ldan phyi rol pa nyid la//sgra yig e dang sbyor bar bya//dbus las tha ma pa bzung

66a4(rgyud): li//smra ri khrag 'thung 'dra ba bzhi//rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma dag yin//rlung mtshams phyi rol pa gzung nas//yi ge e dang ldan par bya//dbu las dbu ma de bzhin du//me mtshams 'dab ma stan ldan pa'i//
66a5(rgyud): lho las dbu ma 'gyur bar bya//me mtshams 'dab mar gnas nyid dang //sgra yig i dang ldan pa yi//byang las dang po 'gyur bar bya//dbus las dang po de bzhin du//thig le yang dag ldan pa'o//
66a6(rgyud): zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje 'dzin pas gsungs so//dbu las dbu ma de bzhIn du//thig le nga ro yang dag ldan//dbus las tha ma bzung ba dang //yi ge a dang mnyam ldan pa'i//rlung mtshams phyi rol gnas 
66a7(rgyud): par 'gyur//me mtshams 'dab mar ba nyid//byang nas dang po gang yin te//dbus las dbu ma pa yang bzung //e yig me mtshams dang ldan pa'i//lho las dbu ma 'gyur ba bya//rlung mtshams phyi rol ba bzung nas//

66b1(rgyud): dbus las dbu ma 'gyur ba'o//oM yig dang ni ldan pa ste//bden bral 'dab mar gnas pa de bzhin//lho las dang po de bzhin du//yige e yis ldan pa yi//byang nas tha ma yang yin te//dbang ldan 'dab
66b2(rgyud): mar gnas pa la//zla tshes thig le gtsug gi zhal//zhes bcom ldan 'das thogs pa med pa shin tu dga' ba'i rgyal po rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//me mtshams 'dab mar gnas pa la//zla tshe thig le

66b4(rgyud): sbyor bar bya//bzhi par yongs su grags pa yin//dbus las dbu ma pa nyid dang//me mtshams pa stan ldanapa yi//sgra yig a dang sbyar ba nas su//lho las dbu ma 'gyur bar bya//rlung mtshams phyi rol
66b5(rgyud): gnas pa yang//sgra yig i dang sbyor bya ste//tsheg drag lhan cig 'dod pa'o//de dag ni mda' rnon 'dzin ma la sogs pa//dgra'i sring mo bzhi rnams kyi'o//me tog la sogs 'dus pa dang
66b6(rgyud): sgo srungs ma sogs 'dus pa ste//rtsa ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi tshul gyis//de bzhin yang dag 'byung ba'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das thogs pa med pa shin tu dga' ba'i rdo rje sems dpas gsungs
66b7(rgyud): te;rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma'i dkyil 'khor 'byung ba'i sngags kyi tshogs kyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//oM yig thig le dang ldan par//dbus las dbu ma gzung byas te//bden bral phyi rol pa la sogs//dbus las bzhi pa gang
66b8(rgyud): yin pa;;sgra yig a dang sbyor bar bya//dbus las dbu ma bzung ba dang//dbang ldan phyi rol gnas pa la//sgra yig u dang sbyor bar bya//tsheg drag mnyam por ldan pa yi//byang nas dbu ma yang yin te//byang n+sa
67a1(rgyud): @#//bzhi pa gang yin pa//rtseg mar de bzhin rtsi bar 'dod//dbus las dang po bzung ba dang //byang nas dbu ma yang yin te//dbus las tha ma de sbyor ba//brgyad par yongs su grags pa yin//byang nas
67a2(rgyud): tha ma de bzhin du//sgra yig e dang ldan pa'i//byang nas dang po yang yin te//dbus la dbu ma dang sbyor te//dbang ldan phyi rol gnas pa yang //me mtshams 'dab ma bzung nas su//rlung mtshams phyi 
67a3(rgyud): rol gnas pa la /me mtshams bstan ldan bya ste//tsheg drag a yis sbyor ba'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//oM yig thig le dang ldan pa//bden bral phyi rol gnas

73a1(rgyud): @#//ci 'dra'i rtag pa'i bsod nams su//ji na rtag tu bri bya'o//de la ji ltar thig rdab pa'i//cho ga'i shes pa yin zhe na//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor bri bya na//mtshams kun mnyam por 'dren par bya//

73a6(rgyud): sbyor ba yin;lhag chad cho gar gyur na yang//sgrubs dang skyon du mi 'gyur ro//de la ji ltar gsang ba'i lnga'i de nyid yin zhe na//sems dpa' rdod ma tha ma med//rdo rje sems dpa' dga' ba che//kun du bzang

74a1(rgyud): @#//rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra ba ste//rang rang de bzhin nyid kyis bstan//chos kyi dbyings las byung ba yi//rgya cher dngos grub ster bar 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so/

74a3(rgyud): na ston gyur na//de la dpral bar stan par bya//mig la ston pa gang yin pa//de la lte ba ston par bya//gang gis lte ba stan pa ni//de la mchu ni rab tu bstan//gang gis ma mchu rab bstan na//de la so ni stan par bya//gang gis

74b5(rgyud): cad gsang chen gyi//gang gis lhag par mchod na ni//yid kyi mchod dang gzhan yang//dbyangs dang sil snyan//gar gyis yin//de la ji ltar rang gis rig pa'i lha//thams cad sgrub pa chen pa'i rgyud

75a1(rgyud): /med par de las 'dir //rjes su sbyor bar ldan par 'gyur//lho las dang po bzung nas su//gnyis pa las ni ldan par bya//nan tan gyis ni e yig sbyin//skad cig ma yis mchod 'gyur ro//

77b8(rgyud): rnams snang ba nam mkha' dang mnyam pa//der ni rdo rje'i mdzad pa gzugs can lta bu ste//rdo rje'i theg pa der ni gzugs sogs 'dod yon gyi//bde ba mi bzad chu tshogs zla ba dang mnyam stsol mdzad
78a1(rgyud): /pa'o//rnal 'byor gnas ni du ma'i rdo rje'i tshul 'di ni//du ma'i gzugs 'dzin der ni rdo rje dang mnyam zhing//nyid dang 'dra ba'i sems dpa' rnams ni spro mdzad pa'i//rdo rje de ni dbyings dang
78a2(rgyud): lhan cig mchog rtsol mdzad//rang bdag mkha' mnyam zhugs la skul ba ni//gang zhig rtse gcig sems kyis 'di nyan cing//brjod byas sems kyi klu 'di 'dzin pa yi//glu 'di nam mkha'
78a3(rgyud): dang ni mnyam pa ste//der ni gzugs cas rdo rje len pa'i phyir//rdo rje'i bdag nyid thugs ni sang byed cing//rang bzhin gcig pa'i phyir na//rgyud kyang len par 'gyur 'di ni//rdo rje'i tshig ste rdo rje gzhan
78a4(rgyud): rang bzhin//sngags pas thugs chud dkyil 'khor kun sgom zhing//chos kyi dkyil 'khor kun sgom zhing //dbus gnas pad mo mchog der sgom pa//dkyil 'khor dbus gnas chos ni hU~M
78a5(rgyud): gi dbye ba can//gzugs rnams thams cad sgyu ma'i ngo bo nges par rtag//thugs kyi ngo bor ci bsams rdo rje theg pa'o//sems ni mkha' mnyam sems kyi theg pa 'ang rang bzhin med//
78a6(rgyud): gnyi ga'i rang bzhin yang ni lus can ma yin te//der ni rdo rje la ni med pa'o//rdo rje sems dpa' khyod ni gcig pa'i rang bzhin ste//'jig rten pa la sgyu ma'i rang bzhin nyid du mdzad//sems can 'dul
78a7(rgyud): ba'i rang bzhin du ni sang mdzad pa//kye lags rdo rje sems dpa' khyod nigacig pa'i dngos//lha mo bzhis smras rdo rje kun du sbas pa ste//nang du gnas pa rdo rje'i rol par ldan pa dang//thugs kyi ngo bo nyid kyis der
78a8(rgyud): ni rab tu gnas//thugs kyi gsang ba'i chos ni rnam par byung ba'o//nam mkha'i rang bzhin can rdo rje spro bya zhing//spros pa'i rdo rje slar yang sdu bya ste//sems kyi rang bzhin du ni gcig phyir ro//sems ni 'gog par 'gyur ba na
78b1(rgyud): kun kyang mnyam zhing 'tshungs par mthong //yang dang yang du sngags bsdu bas//gcig pa'i rnam par byang ba'o//thugs kyi dkyil 'khor gyi le'u ste//gnyis pa'o////bram ze dang ni gdol pa'i rang bzhin du//
78b2(rgyud): chos rnams kyi ni rang bzhin sangs rgyas kyis gsungs pa'o//gcig gi rang bzhin la gnas dang po'i dngos po ste//de la rdo rje can gyis mnyam pa'i ngo bor gsungs so//bram ze dang ni khyim bdag gcig pa'i 
78b3(rgyud): dngos yin zhing //rus pa'i rgyan rnams kyang ni sgyu ma'i rang bzhin te//rdo rje'i go 'phang de la bden pa'i rang bzhin no//rnal 'byor de la mnyam pa'i ngo bor ngas mthong ste//de la mnyam pa'i rang bzhin de'i sngags sems
78b4(rgyud): can rnams la sbas//de'i rnal 'byor rang bzhin lha mos gsungs pa ni//lha mo thams cad der ni gcig pa'i rang bzhin te/gtso bo dang bcas mdzes pa dang gcig bu byung 'gyur khyod//mnyam pa'i rang bzhin
78b5(rgyud): de'i sngags ni gcig pu ste//rdo rje rnam par sad par byed pa cig pu nyid//de rin zhes sdang rang bzhin ste//yan lag phyed pa'i chos ni 'byung bar 'gyur ba yin//der ni rdo rje'i btsun mo gcig rang
78b6(rgyud): bzhin||gang tshe rdo rje la ni dbyangs kyis stod pa'i tshe//lha'i g.yon gyi phyed dang cig du gyur par lta//ltung nyid tshad mar byas nas//rdor sems phyag rgyas de bsgo bya//gsung gi dkyil 'khor gyi le'u ste gsum pa'o//
78b7(rgyud): glang po dang ni rta dang mi dang khyi dang ni//ba lang sha ste thams cad ma lus gyur pa rnams//dam tshig de rnams gcig pa'i rang bzhin nyid yin te//mkha' mnyam rang bzhin can gyi rnal 'byor bas bza'
78b8(rgyud): ba yi|dam tshig 'di ni lha mo bzhis pos gsungs pas so//rnal 'byor pa'i sgom pa'i sems dang ldan 'gyur cing //sems gcig pas ni rdo rje 'chang nyid 'grub 'gyur te/dngos po nyid dang bcas pa'i thugs ni
79a1(rgyud): @#//skye bar 'gyur//rdo rje'i dngos mdzad zhi ba ma gsungs snying po ste//'di ltar chos rnams mnyam pa nyid du rtogs bya zhing //de ltar mkha' dang mnyam par rnam par spyad par bya//lha 'di rnam snang lha mo lnga 
79a2(rgyud): rnams de'i bdag nyid/rdo rje theg pa la ni mnyam pa'i rang bzhin te//skul byed mas ni sred pa'i rang bzhin yin pa'i phyir//gcig pa'i dngos las rdo rje sems de skur mthong ba//rdo rje'i theg par 'dod rnams lha mos
79a3(rgyud): 'di skad gsungs//bden pa skyed pa de ni rnam snang lha mos gsungs//'di ni zla ba'i dbus gnas sgom pa rdo rje'i gsang sngags te//de ni sems gcig pa yis rdo rje rnam sgom pa//rab tu 'bar ba'i rdo rje rtse gsum pa nyid do

79a5(rgyud): dngos med mi rtog pa la dngos bcas lus sgom mi//lha mos bskul bar byed pa'i dngos po ste//de ltar chos las nges par rtag byar bas//sems ni dngos po nyid du bya ba'o//sems las dngos po theg
79a6(rgyud): pa med pa'i dngos por bya//dngos po'i rang bzhin la ni theg pa med pa ste//sems ni nam mkha' nyid dang mnyam pa'i rang bzhin can//snga ma ci bzhin sems ni rnam par sbyang bar bya//ji ltar de ni dngos por
79a7(rgyud): bya ba ste//dngos po'i de ni theg par 'grub par 'gyur ba ste//sems ni nam mkha'i lta bu'i mtshan nyid do//dbus su tshig ni chos la nges rtog par//sems las dngos po nyid du bya ba ste//rnam pa du ma'i tshul gyis rang bzhin
79a8(rgyud): gyi//rdo rje sems dpa' skyed par bya ba'o//ji ltar dangs zhing rnyog pa med pa'i chu//'di 'dra'i sems ni myur ba'i ngo bor sgrub//de ltar stan du med pa 'di ni nga yis bstan//rdo rje sems dpa'i sku'i rang bzhin gyis//
79b1(rgyud): zhi ba'i rnam pa'i ngo bor bya ba ste//de la rdo rje'i chu ni de lta'i rang bzhin can//thugs kyi ngo bo'i rnal 'byor pas bza' bya//chos dang dge 'dun dang ni rdo rje'i chu ste de rnams mnyam pa'i rang bzhin te//zhag dang phrag 
79b2(rgyud): sogs mchog du mdzes par rab zhugs dngos po'i lus ni sgyur bar bya//bza' ba dang bca' dang mal stan la sogs de rnams thams cad 'di rang bzhin//gang zhig ting nge 'dzin du nges par bstan pa'ang de dang 
79b3(rgyud): gcig pa'i rang bzhin no//sangs rgyas kyis ni gsum char rdo rje'i ngo bor mdzad//chos rnams thams cad dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin te//rdo rje can gyis sgyu ma'i rang bzhin nyid du gsungs//de'i rang bzhin
79b4(rgyud): mkha' mnyam mo//so sor snang bar 'gyur//sgyu ma'i ngo bor nges par bstan pa yis//rdo rje yi ni nges par 'jug par bya ba ste//sems can rang bzhin la ni theg pa med//sems ni de la gnas
79b5(rgyud): pa'i snying po'o//de lta'i ngo bos thugs kyi bdag nyid kyi//sngags khyod kyi ni 'grub par 'gyur ba ste//thugs dang spro dang sdur bya ba//gsum char yang ni gcig pa'i rang bzhin no//sems
79b6(rgyud): ni re rab hU~M dang mnyam bya ste//dag pa'i sangs rgyas su ni sems slar skyed//sems dub gyur na slar yang sdu ba dag//der ni sngags pas des rtag sgom par bya//sdus pa'i ngo bos slar yang sbyar bar bya//rdo rje
79b7(rgyud): sems dpa' des ni skus g.yo mdzad//slar yang nam mkha'i ngo bos spro ba ste//de ltar spro dang sdu ba'i rang bzhin no//de la rdo rje sems dpa' gcig 'grub pa'i phyir//de phyir spro ba yang rdo rje 'chang du
79b8(rgyud): rigs//sngags pas bstan par gyur na spro//bstan par ma gyur 'dir spros pa//lus nyon mongs kyi 'bras mi 'byin//nges par 'grub 'gyur theg pa'i dngos//thams cad bden pa 'di rnaMs stan

80b1(rgyud): mdzad cing ;bdud rtsi'i ro ni myang mdzad pa//gnyis pa tshig gis bdag phyag 'tshal//thams cad ye shes kyis sprul pa'o//nga rgyal khro ba des mi bya//thig le lam gyi sbyor ba yis//gsum

80b7(rgyud): bcu gnyis rgya chen la//de'i sbyor ba mthong nas ni//dkyil 'khor cho ga sdus pa'o//dbye ba rnam pa bco brgyad kyis//'khor bar lus ni rnam par grol//zhi dang rgyas dang dbang dang dgug//skad dang mnan dang

81a1(rgyud): @#//dul zhing rnam par mkhas pa dang //dang po re shig slob dpon gyis//sngon du snyen pa la sogs bya//yid dang 'thun pa'i sa phyogs su//'dzam pa'i stan la 'dug nas ni//lag 'thil nyi ma

81a4(rgyud): nas 'gug par byed//dug gis pad mas skul zhing skrod pa'o//g+hi hang gis ni bdug pa mnon//dri med gsuM gyis 'joms par byed//'byed pas bsod par rnam par bshad//las kun las ni sa'i 
81a5(rgyud): gdugs||ye shes kyis ni rab tu sbyar//rnam shes g.yon pa kun du sbyor//thal mo skor ba sngon du 'gro lag pa kha sbyar byas nas ni//g+hi hang dang ni rnam shes sbyar//dri med gsum ni gyen du sgreng 
81a6(rgyud): a la'i phyag rgya rnam par bshad//chu'i sa bon rgyud pa'i//me'i sa bon steng du'o//rdo rje mchog ltar 'bar ba'i 'od//sa yang dmigs la rnam par bsam//nam mkha' rna ba lnga la'o//bdud rtsi'i chu'i sa bon ni//
81a7(rgyud): lce'i dbang po'i kun du sten//sde tshan lnga pa'i tha ma ni//rtsa ba'i steng dang 'og du mtshan//sum 'gyur du ni re bas mtshan//bzhi pa rlung gis rab tu sbyar//u dang thig les rgyan pa ni//bzhi pa yi ge drug pa'o 
81a8(rgyud): rmongs pa chu yis 'grin par ro//sa gnyis nyon mongs thong du'o//snying khar g.yo ba'i rlung dang ni//me ni de bzhin lte bar phyugs//rgod bya yi ge rnam dgod nas//lha nyid sa bon du byas pa'o//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus

81b4(rgyud): zlas shing snyen pa bya//rgyal dbang grub dang gtum mo sogs//phyi rim gnas pa'i 'jig rten pa//de yang khri ru snyen pa yis//thams cad kun nas grub pa'o//me tog bdag pa dri dang ni/

83b2(rgyud): mtshon rtsi kun nas gsang bar bya//sa~M A+o~M swahA/de'i gnas su rnal 'byor pas//gnyis med gtor ma kun du dag//gnas brgyad du ni mar me dang//rgyas pa'i gtor ma rnam par sbyin//ji ltar nus pas mchod

83b4(rgyud): nas su ha thig bdab pa ni rab tu bya//yongs su bzung ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa rdzogs s+ho////rab tu mkhas pa'i slob dpon gyis//khyad par du ni thig gdab po//mtshon sna lnga yang gtso bar bya
83b5(rgyud): rang gi sa dbus thig le yi//phyogs brgyad du ni gnas brgyad du//gnas dang gnas su rnam par spyad//de yang dman pa'i mi gnas pa'o//thams cad kun ni hU~M gis ni//mkhas pa yis ni gzung
83b6(rgyud): bar bgyi||rdo rje sems dpa' sbyor ba yis//bdag nyid ni lus ni sbrul byas la//lag gnyis thal mo sbyar nas ni//thig ni dbus su bzung bar bya//mkha' 'gro lnga'i sa bon gyis//sngags dang sngags kyi gsang bar
83b7(rgyud): bya/oM a a aM a hU~M swahA//thig skud gsang ba'i sngags so//bla mas lag pas gzung nas ni//slob ma lag gis mtha' bzung ste//slob ma dbang ldan phyogs gnas la//de bzhin bla ma bden dral du//slob ma
83b8(rgyud): me'i phyogs gnas la//slob dpon chu'i phyogs nyid do//slob ma shar du gnas nas ni//slob dpon nub kyi phyogs gnas la//slob ma byang gi char gnas nas//bla ma lho ru rnam par gnas//phyogs brgyad kyi ni
84a1(rgyud): @#//thig btab na|phyi ni gru bzhir gdab par bya//kun nas thams cad yongs rdzogs nas//dbus su rdo rje dkyil 'khor yang //lnga pa yi ni gsum pa dang //re re mthar ni gnas pa'o//phyi rol 
84a2(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma gnas pa//mtha' ma brgyad sogs kyis skor ba'o//snying po'i cha ni dgu pa la//rdo rje ka bas nye bar mdzes//dbus kyi grar ni sna tshogs pa'i//rdo rje bzhi yis rab tu sbyar//cha lnga pa ni 
84a3(rgyud): kun gnas mas//sgo'i gru bzhi pa nyid ni//phyogs kyi phyed ni gru bzhi pa'o//steng du rim mo mnyam por byas//sgo bzhi'i bzhi cha nyid kyis ni//lha mo kha khyer du nyid du ni bya//sgo'i rgyang cha dag du ni//tshod rtsi'i thad 
84a4(rgyud): rab tu 'dod|snying po'i rgyu'i cha la ni//phyi mas phyogs kyi mdog du bya//rdo rje'i phyi rol gnas su ni//mtshon rtsi dmar po'i phye mas so//de'i phyi rol gnas su ni/ra ba lnga ni rab tu sbyin//de'i phyi rol 
84a5(rgyud): gnas su yang|ji ltar nye bar mdzes par dgod//lha rnams kyis ni gnas su yang //kun ni gser bsregs pa'i mdog//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs bzhi'i sa nye bar mdzes//rnam pa sna tshogs 
84a6(rgyud): rab bkod pa//dril bu g.yer ka dra ba'i phreng //dra ba dra phyed kyis skor cing //rdo rje ka bas nye bar mdzes//mtshams kyi sna tshogs rdo rje ni//zla ba'i steng du rnam par rgyan//sgo ru rgyal mtshan sgreng ba ni//gnyis gnyis bum pa 
84a7(rgyud): rten pa'o|pad ma seng ge mtshan pa ni//shar gyi phyogs su rab tu gzugs//rma bya pad ma'i mtshan pa ni//byang gi phyogs kyi ming can no//dang po pad mas mtshon pa ni//nub kyi phyogs su rab tu gzhag//chu srin gyis ni pad ma 
84a8(rgyud): ni|lho'i phyogs su gzhag pa'o//rab tu 'phyad pa'i na bzar ldan//gnas dang gnas su rnam par sbyar//'di nyid de nyid ldan dkyil 'khor//sangs rgyas kun gyi gnas yin//phyogs brgyad dag du bum pa ni//dri'i chu 
84b1(rgyud): yis rab bkang zhing//pa la sha'i rtse mos mtshan//sgo skyong gi ni phyogs nyid du//gzhan yang 'tshams ni bzhi pa ru//bum pa brgyad ni gzhag cing bkod//rgyal dang bkra shis shar du'o//grub dang rdzu 'phrul byang phyogs su'o//
84b2(rgyud): nub du rgyal byed bum pa'o//lho ru grub dang sgrub byed yin//kha cig lhag par bri ba dang//snying rje khung stong bar du'o//zhi rgyas dang ni don gnyer bas//ji ltar nub par sbyar bar bya//dkyil 'khor gyi ni shar gyi char//slob dpon gyi ni
84b3(rgyud): ngas byas pa//rang gi lus po lha nyid du//rdo rje mkha' 'gro sprul par bya//gal te slob mas 'byor gyur na//sgo bzhi ru ni slob dpon bzhi//tha ma gcig gis rtag par bya//dang po tsham 'am sbyor ba yis//bdug pa'i bgegs 
84b4(rgyud): sogs rab tu skad//g.yas kyi rdo rje gsor bya zhing //dril bu g.yon pas bsil nas ni//phyag rgya sngon 'gro lta stangs kyis//mkhyen pa gar gyis rab tu sbyor//mnyam pa'i 'gros dang mi mnyam 'gros//'gros pa
84b5(rgyud): dang ni g.yon du sgyur//'jigs pa hU~M dang hU~M sgrogs shing lha mo rdo rje bdag ces brjod//oM hU~M ha na ha na mar ya du sta na g+ha ta ya a tha a tha hU~M hri phaT/byang gi sgo ru gnas nas ni//rdo rje dril bu mngon sgrags
84b6(rgyud): nas//lag pa gnyis la hU~M rnam pa drag mor sprul cing bsams nas ni//lag gnyis mngon par sbyar byas nas//snying ga'i phyogs su bzung bar bya//g.yas rkyang ba dang g.yon rkyang ba//zlum pa dang ni sa ga'i 'gros
84b7(rgyud): oM hU~M a phaT/ro langs ma'i gzugs bsams nas//nub kyi phyogs 'phen par bya//lag pa gnyis kyis mtshan ma yir//bkyed pa'i mtshams su bzung bar bya//bus mo'i 'gros dang rus sbal 'gros//rked
84b8(rgyud): pa'i mtshams su bzung bar bar bya//g.yon pa'i lag pa rkang pa'i pus mor sbyar//sgyur zhing rab tu 'phang ba ni//seng ge mnan pa zhes su bshad//hU~M gis sgra'i dag bya zhing //oM hU~M a phaT//rtum mo'i gzugs ni
85a1(rgyud): @#//rdzogs byas nas//lho'i phyogs su 'phang bar bya//lag gnyis thal mo sbyar nas ni//sbyi bo'i phyogs su bzung bar bya//pus mo 'gros dang rus sbal 'gros//khro zhing 'jigs
85a2(rgyud): pa'i hU~M byas la/drag mo sngags zlos pa la brtson//oM hU~M a phaT/sngon gyi gnas dang gnas su ni//'phen pa dag ni yang bya'o//snying khar rdo rje lag gzhag nas//dril bu 'gul du mngon par bkod
85a3(rgyud): bdag nyid ye shes mkha' 'gro mar//hU~M gi nga rgyal byas nas ni//g.yas rkyang g.yon rkyang skums nas ni//de nas phur bu dra ba dang//ra ba gos byas lus po bsrung//ye shes mkha' 'gro'i sbyor ba yis//sems
85a4(rgyud): dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung gnas na//lag gnyis lcags sgrog ltar byas nas//steng gi phyogs su sgyur dang bcas//oM hU~M phu phaT/gra ba'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//gnyis pa khu tshur byas nas ni//
85a5(rgyud): pad ma dang ni ye shes rkyang//sa'i gdugs dang rnam shes sgreng//rnam shes dang ni ye shes spang//de bzhin sa gdugs pad mar sbyar//phyogs rgyad dag du skor byas la//oM dzaM b+ha ba ni hUM/
85a6(rgyud): dra ba chen po sngags dang phyag rgya'o//gan rgyal lag byas sa la gdab//oM dzu hUM phaT/rdo rje phur bu'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o/de nas slob dpon bde ldan pa'i//stan gnas rdo rje mkha' 'gror byas//sems can kun la
85a7(rgyud): snying rtse bas bum pa la sogs gsang gtor bya//thams cad de nyid mtshan ldan pa'i//dal bar 'gro ba'i chus grangs nas//bum pa chu'i sa kun nas gang//oM ta pa te ma ha ta pa hU~M swahA//lan bdun yongs su
85a8(rgyud): zlas byas na//dal ba'i chu ni sngags kyis bsre//sna tshogs dri dang me tog dang//bdud rtsi lnga dang yang dag ldan//myug gu sa lngas rnam par rgyan//thams cad bdan du sbyin dang ldan//gos kyis mgrin pa dkri ba
85b1(rgyud): dang /rgyan pa brgyan ni rab tu brgyan//bum par 'o ma sres pa dang//me tog phreng ba rnam par sbyin//sa'i gdugs dang rnam shes ni//sbyar la lhag par gyen du sgreng//lan bdun zlas par ldan pa yis//bum
85b2(rgyud): pa kun la reg par bya//oM hUM suM k+Sh+yaM yuM hUM phaT swahA//bum pa pa thun mong gi sngags dang phyag rgya'o//ji bzhin sngon gyi gnas su ni//de bzhin thams cad gzhag par bya//de nas slob dpon gyi bya ba la
85b3(rgyud): tshon chen po'i gos dang ni//dar la sogs pa'i bgo ba dang//lus la rgyan rnams kyis rgyan//sangs rgyas lnga'i spyod pan byas//shin tu mnyam gzhag sems bcas pas//go bar byang chub yan lag bya//de la
85b4(rgyud): go cha sbyin par bya//mkhas pas bsrung dang bcing ba dang//lag gnyis rab tu rkyang byas nas//rnam shes ye shes dkri bar bya//ngang pa'i gzhog pa'i tshul gyis ni//de bzhin go cha chen po'o//oM hUM phaM phaT
85b5(rgyud): go cha'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//sngon gyi mtshan nyid yang dag ldan//rdo rje dril bu mngon par gzung//de kho na nyid kyis gzung nas//rdo rje sems dpa' bdag ces brjod//ba dan gsum
85b6(rgyud): gyi rdo rje sgreng//de nyid du ni rdo rje ldan//yi ge brgyad dang ldan pa'i//yan lag brgyad la gtsug par bya//lce la tshun nyid dang ni//nam mkha' rna ba lnga la'o//sa gnyis dang ni dpung par sbyar//me ni de bzhin tshangs pa'i
85b7(rgyud): gnas/rmongs pa shing rta 'grin par ro//g.yo ba snying ga snying du ste//thams cad nyon mongs 'byung la'o//lta phyugs kyi ni sa bon no//yan lag brgyad la gtugs pa yis//bdag nyid lha ru shes pa yis
85b8(rgyud): me tog spos dang dri la sogs//phreng bas rab tu mchod par bya//oM badzra ka ya ho//oM badzra ba k+sha a/oM badzra hri da ya hUM/swa b+ha ba shud d+ho sarba shud d+ho sarba d+ha rma sa b+ha ba shud d+ho haM//badzra shud d+ho sarba d+ha rma badzra shud d+ho haM/
86a1(rgyud): @#//yo ga shud d+ha sarba d+ha rma yo ga shud d+ho ho//bdag nyid rdo rje rdo rje ni//byang chub rim pas kun du rtse//de nas dgug pa'i mtshan nyid bshad//ri mig dbus ma la sogs pa//
86a2(rgyud): ye shes spyan drang mchog tu brtson//seng ge rtan gyi btan la ni//nyi ma'i dkyil gyis brgyan pa//a li ka li'i dbus su ni//lha'i gzugs ni khyad par du//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis bcings nas//
86a3(rgyud): sa'i gdugs dang dkar por sbyar//g+hi haM lcags kyu 'gugs byed pa//dam tshig gi cho ga mchog/oM badzra a g+hu sha dza/spyan drang ba'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//zhal gsum phyag ni drug pa nyid//kha tog sngon
86a4(rgyud): po rab tu mdzes//rgod dang khro dang sgeg pa dang //sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung gis bzhugs//dang po mda' ni 'phyar ba dang //gnyis pa mtshan ma nu mar 'dzin//gsum pa ni ni rdo rje'o//g.yon
86a5(rgyud): gyi dang po gzhu snams shing //bdud rtsi chu yis kun kang ba//g.yon pa'i phyag mtshan gnyis pa'o//ga sum pa dril bu sgra sgrogs shing //shin tu mdzes pa'i na bza' klubs//dar gyi
86a6(rgyud): ral pa bcings mdzad cing //thod pas cod pan du bgyis shing //de yis mtshan ma rnam pa lnga//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam rgyan cing //'od zer 'bar ba mang po 'khrugs//sangs rgyas rnal
86a7(rgyud): 'byor nam mkha' dgod//kha dog dkar po rab tu mdzes//shes rab de'i gdan la yang //gnyis kyis 'grin pa 'khyud mdzad cing //tshems kyis ma 'chu tsir nas ni//rkang pas 'tshams su rnam
86a8(rgyud): dkris ta rdo rje pad mar kun du this//rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan pa'i sku//rab tu mdzes pa'i na bza' brgyan//'di yis sangs rgyas rnal 'byor ni//sems kyi dbus su bsam par bya//ye shes shar gyi re
86b1(rgyud): dmig la/rdo rje dril bu'i phyag rgya yis//rdo rje mkha' 'gro dgug par bya//klu dbang rol pa'i gdan la ni//rol pa'i 'phen pas rnam par gnas//kun da ka dang mnyam pa'i 'od//phyag gnyis sgra ni rnam
86b2(rgyud): par dag//sangs rgyas lnga yis rnam par brgyan//brgyan kun gyis ni brgyan pa'i sku//shin tu mdzes pa'i na bza' klu bas//rgyan gsum khro dang hUM mdzad cing //kha traM ka dang rnal 'byor snon//'od zer bye ba rnam mang
86b3(rgyud): po//glang chen lpags pa bsnams pa dang //de bzhin mi'i lpags pa snams//me'i dkyil 'khor gyis cing //rdo rje mkha' 'gro shar du'o//byang du drag mo ni//rdo rje lcags kyus dgug par bya//kha tog ser po
86b4(rgyud): ta rab tu mdzes//ha ha zhes ni 'jigs mdzad cing //skra ni gyen du 'bar zhing mdzes//sangs rgyas lnga'is dbu la brgyan//dkyil dkrung phyed pa'i thabs kyis ni//rma bya'i gdan ni kun tu mnan
86b5(rgyud): rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan pa'i sku//phyag gnyis rgyan//rnams kyis brgyan cing //kha traM ga dang rnal 'byor snod//glang chen lpags pa snams mdzad cing //de bzhin mi'i lpag pa g.yogs//ye
86b6(rgyud): shes byang gi cha ru ni//drag mo mkha' 'gro dgug par bya//ye shes nub kyi cha la ni//rdo rje can gyi ro langs dgug//bya khyung gdan la rnam gnas cing //mnyam por cog cog po yis gnas//kha tog dmar po
86b7(rgyud): mdzes pa'i sku//phyag gnyis mdzes pa'i hUM sgrogs shing //dbu skra 'bar zhing rnam par dkram//sangs rgyas lnga yis dbu la rgyan//kha traM ka dang lhung bzed snod//glang chen lpag pa rab tu gsol//
86b8(rgyud): rdo rje lcags kyu 'gugs nas ni//nub kya phyogs lha mo bsam par bya//ye shes lha'i cha la ni//rtum mo zhes bya 'i lha mo ni//kha tog nag mo 'jigs chen mo//ha ha bzhad pas 'jigs byed pa//phyed
87a1(rgyud): @#//dkrung byas pa'i dkyil 'khor gyis //mngag bzhug gi ni bdan la zhugs//dbu skra dmar po rnam par dges//dbu la sangs rgyas lnga'is brgyan//phyag gnyis pa la rab tu bzhad//
87a2(rgyud): me 'bar ba dang mnyam pa'i 'od//khwa twa kha dang rnal 'byor snod//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par brgyan//glang chen pags pas smad g.yogs shing//mi'i pags pa rab tu gsol//rdo rje lcags kyus

87a4(rgyud): 'od//phyag gnyis 'phros pa hUM sgrogs shing//na bza' dmar po rab tu gsol//rgyan rnams kun gyis rna ma par rgyan//g.yas pa steng du ral gri dang//g.yon pas sdig 'dzub zhags pa yis//bdud rnams 
87a5(rgyud): thams cad sreg byed pa'i//'di 'dra'i seng ge dgug par bya//rdo rje mchu yis gdan gnas par//me phyogs lha mo stag mo ni//dkar dang sngo ba'i kha tog can//zlum po 'phen pa'i bdan la ni//

87a7(rgyud): he'i gdan la mnyam gzhon la//bden dral lha mo lce spyang ni//dmar ba dang ni sngo ba'i mdog//drag sta zhags pa sdig 'dzub kyis//phyag gnyis nye bar mdzes pa yi//na bza' dmar po mngon snams nas//gdan phyed
87a8(rgyud): la ni mnyam por gnas//me'i dkyil 'khor gyis brgyan cing//'che ba cung zad rtsigs pa dang//ha ha rgod par 'jigs par byed//bden dral mnga' bdag lha mo ni//rdo rje lcags kyu 'di yis dgug//lce spyang gdan
87b1(rgyud): la mnyam gnas pa'i//rlung phyogs lha mo 'dug pa'o//dmar ba dang dkar ba'i 'dog//gdan la gcig pur gnas nas ni//drag po'i gzugs kyis 'jigs mdzad pa//phyag gnyis mnyam par sbyar ba'i//dbyug pa
87b2(rgyud): sdig 'dzub zhags pa dang//rgyan rnams kyis ni rab tu brgyan//na bza' dmar po brgyan byas zhing //me 'bar ba dang mnyam pa'i 'od//dbus kyi lha mo brgyad rnams kyi//rdo rje lcags kyus dgug par bya//
87b3(rgyud): rdo rje ra ba'i phyi rol gyi//phyi'i rnal 'byor ma gnas su//sngon mo rtum mo phro gnyer ma//shar gyi gnas gyi lha mo ni//bdud kyi chu dang yang dag ldan//gnyis pa'i phyag na mthong ba'o//drag mo khrag 'thung 
87b4(rgyud): sha za ma lcags kyu la sogs lnga rnams kyis//bkang ba'i thod pa phyag na snams//drag po'i gzugs can lha mo ni//rgyan rnams kun gyis rab tu brgyan//khro mo 'bar bye'i 'jigs byed ma//
87b5(rgyud): nub kyi phyogs kyi lha mo ni//gdon pa'i gdon sogs 'dzin pa'o//lag gnyis su ni rnam par dgod//thod phreng rdo rje'i bum pa yang//bza' dang sna tshogs pas//lag pa gnyis kyis kun
87b6(rgyud): nas snams//sgyeg mo dri tshab dbang ldan phyogs//me'u bi bang me tog bzhin//bden bral klu dang bdug pa ma//rlung du gar dang mar me'o//rgyan kun gyis ni brgyan pa'i//lha mo nyi shu rnam par sgom//mchod pa nyi shus

87b8(rgyud): 'bar ba ni byang du rgod//shi ba'i gnas pa ni/'jigs byed ma ni nub du 'o//de'i gdan la gnas de ni//ngag 'phrog ma ni lho phyogs su//gzugs dang mtshan sogs mdzes pa ni//rdo rje lcags kyu
88a1(rgyud): @#//yis ni dgug/lha dang lha mo thams cad la//rdo rje zhags pa bstan par bya//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis bcings nas//dkar po g+hi hang spel byas nas//oM badzra pa sha hUM/rdo rje zhags pa'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o

88b1(rgyud): khu tshur dam du bcings nas ni//dri ma gsum dang dud pa sgreng//gnyis pa dkar yang dag dmigs byas nas//rdo rje rtse mo brtan par bya//oM ti sta a s+tha na hU~M phaT/dam tshig gi sngags dang phyag rgya'o
88b2(rgyud): pad ma ye shes bkris byas la//rnam shes kyis ni sa gdugs mnan//gnyis ka ljags sgrog ltar bcings nas//lhag mar ba dan gsum par sgrang//dri ma gsum dang dud bar sprod//lnga pa rdo rje 'dzin
88b3(rgyud): pa'o//oM hU~M a swa hA//rnal 'byor nam mkha'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//zla ba nyi ma kha sbyar nas//pad ma'i tshul gyis dbye bar bya//oM a a tha swa hA//shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o/sa gdugs
88b4(rgyud): dang ni rnam shes dkri//lhag ma rdo rje ltar sgreng nas//oM u a swa hA//rdo rje ma'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o/'di'i phyag rgya gnyis byas nas//thal mo log par sbyar byas te//oM k+sha a swa hA//drag mo'i
88b5(rgyud): sngags dang phyag rgya'o//khu tshur gnyis ni sgreng byas nas/mngon dang 'tshams dang kha che ba//oM yum a swa hA//ro langs sngags dang phyag rgya'o/khu tshur gnyis dang dam bcings nas//dkar po
88b6(rgyud): g+hi hang sgrel nas ni//rtse mo gnyis ni 'dud byas nas//rin chen tshul du gasbyar ba ni//oM hU~M aHswa hA/rtum mo'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o/de'i phyag rgya dam bcings te//sa gdugs pad ma sgrel byas nas//oM yU yum swa hA

88b8(rgyud): snying po sngags dang phyag rgya'o/nyi ma zla ba'i lag pas kyang//'byed pa dang ni rnam shes sbyar//gnyis ka rtse mo sprang byas la//bdud rtsi chu'i sngon du'o//a ha ho hri swa hA//shar gyi rnal 'byor ma'i sngags dang phyag

89a2(rgyud): oM ku ru ku ru swahA//nub kyi rnal 'byor ma gsum gyi sdags dang phyag rgya'o//zla ba nyi ma rab rkyang nas//rgyab dang rgyab kyi sbyor ba bya//rtse mo sgyur nas sprad par bya//lho'i rnal 'byor ma gsum gyi'o//oM par d+ha li
89a3(rgyud): hUM phaT/lho'i rnal 'byor ma'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis bcings te//gnyis skyed pa'i 'tshams su gzhag//oM hUM swahA/sgyeg mo'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o/zla ba nyi ma rab rkyang ste//'byed pa dang

89a7(rgyud): nas ni/ye shes gyen du sgreng bar bya//oM k+k+yaM swahA/mar me'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o/rlung bu g.yas kyi rkang 'tshams su//zla ba'i lag pa 'phang bar bya/g.yas pa sku'i 'tshams su ni//nyi ma'i lag pa 'phang byas la//

89b1(rgyud): sngags dang phyag rgya'o//yi dgas lha mo kha bcug nas//oM pheng pheM swahA//'jigs byed ma'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//zla ba'i khu tshur dam bcings nas//rna ba'i rtse ru sbyar bar bya//oM pheM pheM ma swahA//
89b2(rgyud): ngag 'phrog ma'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//phyag rgyas kun nas so sor du//snying po'i sngags dang sbyor bar bya//lha mo kun dang rnal 'byor ma//rdo rje lcags sgrog stan par bya//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis
89b3(rgyud): bcings te//rgyab dang rgyab du rab tu sbyar//oM badzra s+pho ta oM k+sha swahA//lcags sgrog gi'o//rna bar 'da' ka snying g.yo ba//oM k+shu swahA//'da'i 'o//bdag nyid nyi ma 'khyud nas ni//lag pa gnyis kyis bzung
89b4(rgyud): bar bya//oM pri ya ta mi hUM//nu ma 'dzin pa'o//nyi ma cha ni rab brgyad nas//ye shes dang ni dkar po sbyar//de'i dud pa sgreng ba dang/rdo rje'i phyag rgya rab tu bstan//oM hUM swahA//de nyid zla ba la sbyar nas//

89b8(rgyud): dang phyag rgya'o//khu tshur gnyis sre ba nas//dkar po dang ni g+hi hang sgreng//rtse dang rtse dang sprad byas la//rin chen tshul bu rnam par sbyar//oM da na a gre hUM/swahA/ku pe ra 'o//khu tshur bcangs na dkar po bsgreng//oM kha ta ni

90a2(rgyud): oM khe sha sa swa hA//khyab 'jug gi 'o//ba dan gsum steng du 'phen//oM hU~M sa swa hA//drag po'i 'o//khu tshur gnyis pa gnas su skyod//oM a~M swa hA/tshangs pa'i 'o//zla ba'i khu tshur bstan byas nas//g+hi hang

90b1(rgyud): bor bcings byas nas//lhag par dar bzhin 'chang ba'o//oM hU~M swahA//dar phyangs bcings pa'i phyag rgya'o//nyi ma kun nas mgo bo la//gnas lngar ru ni gdug par bya//oM hU~M swahA /cod pan gyi'o/nyi ma'i khu

90b4(rgyud): ti tsa su gA na d+ha hU~M swahA dri'i 'o//dkar po mchog tu rkyang nas ni//ye shes dang ni kun du sbyar//oM shu she be ta s+phu swa hU~M swahA/me tog gi'o//rgya chen po'i bdug pa yis//gnas kun du ni rab
90b5(rgyud): tu sbyar de'i phyag rgya btan bcings te//dud pa sgreng nas/bdug par bya//oM pu la ma li ni hU~M swahA//spos kyi 'o'o//rgya chen po'i mar me yis//gnas brgyad du ni mchod par bya//

91a1(rgyud): @#//shes sbyar nas ni//de nyid kun nas rab tu bas//oM ha ha hri hU~M swAhA//lha gsum gyis ni mngon subs la//kun nas 'thor thung dbul bar bya//snod rgyang kun nas bkang ba ni//gnas brgyad pu ni sbyin

91a3(rgyud): nas ni/rab tu dbyangs snyan gyi ni glu//oM hU~M hU~M g+huM g+huM g+huM hU~M hU~M hU~M//oM pheM g+huM pheM g+huM hU~M swAhA//dges pa chen po'i sngags so//thams cad kun nas sngags brjod cing //de nas rdo rje dril bu sgrogs//rdo rje khu tshur dam

91a5(rgyud): ta swa b+ha ba b+he ni badzra swa hA//hri ta ya to sa ni hU~M hU~M hU~M swAhA//rdo rje dril bu'i sngags so/bdug pa me tog dkar po sogs//sa le zho dang zla ba dang //bu ram sbrang rtsi zas kyis ni//dbang ldan mchod
91a6(rgyud): par dbul bar ldan//spos dang me tog dkar po sogs//'o ma chu'i snod dang ni//lha bshos mchod pa sna tshogs pa//rlung gi phyogs su dbul bar bya//sha dang nya dang zan dron dang //snod ni khrag gis kun bkang ba
91a7(rgyud): til gyi khu ba'i bza' ba yis//bden dral phyogs su dbul bar bya//zho dang sbrang rtsi nas kun ldan//ske tshe khu ba'i bza' ba dang //bro dang ldan pas khyad par du//me'i phyogs su dbul bar bya/dri dang me tog rgya chen dang /
91a8(rgyud): spos dang me tog rgya chen dang//rgya chen gtor ma'i gnas su ni//thams cad mchod pa rgya che ba//slob dpon shin tu mnyam gzhag pas//kun nas sems kyis rdzogs par bya//zla bas dril bu mngon bsgrub cing //nyi mas
91b1(rgyud): rdo rje gsor nas ni//kun nas kun du mchod par bya//sgyeg dang bzhad pa'i zhal dang ni//hU~M gi rnam pa'i glu'i ni//rnal 'byor ma kun tshim par bya//phyag rgya ldan zhing ldan pa dang//gzhu dang mda' la sogs
91b2(rgyud): pa'i//gang gang phyag mtshan gyis mchod pa//de'i gzugs su bsam par bya//hU~M gi rnam pas glu ni dges//bkra shis sgra yis kun nas bgang//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis bcings nas//rkyed pa'i mtshams su
91b3(rgyud): rnam par dgod//sgyeg dang rgod dang khro ba dang//yan lag ji bzhin gzhan du sbyar//sgyeg pa'i tshul gyis sgyeg pa dang//pi wang tshul gyis bdebs pa dang//gling bu'i tshul gyis bung pa dang//dung gi tshul gyis
91b4(rgyud): 'bud pa dang//phyag 'tshal tshul gyis 'dud pa dang//sbang g.yab tshul gyis g.yob pa dang//rgyal mtshan tshul gyis sgreng ba dang//bla re'i tshul gyis bri ba dang//rgyal mtshan tshul kyis 'phyar ba

91b8(rgyud): chen po//kun du bzang po kun bdag nyid//rdo rje snyems ma bdag po'i bdag//sems dpa' chen po'i ting 'dzin mchog//rdo rje rdo rje stobs po che//bdud dang bdud ni rab 'joms pa//kun du bzang po'i
92a1(rgyud): @#//spyod pa'i mchog//rnam par snang mdzad phyag 'tshal lo//mi skyod rin chen 'byung ldan dang //snang ba mtha' yas don yod grub//phyag na pad ma byams pa dang //nam mkha' mdzod dang 
92a2(rgyud): kun du bzang //gnod sbyin bdag po 'byam pa'i dbyangs//sgrib sel sa le'i snying po dang //sgrol ma dang ni gos dkar mo//ma ma kyi dang sangs rgyas spyan//mdag mdzes dbyangs snyan yid 'ong ma//rdo rje phreng

92a4(rgyud): 'dod dang thar pa don gnyer ba//srid pa gsum po dus gsum po//'jig rten gsum po khams gsum mchog//phra dang rags pa sdus pa 'o//bdan pa mchog gi gtso bo'o//srid 
92a5(rgyud): pa 'khor ba skyod mdzad pa//g.yo ba dbang mchog bsnyes pa'o//thog ma tha ma gnan mdzad nas//thams cad sngon du gnas pa nyid//dam pa chen po sems dpa' che//rnal 'byor nam mkha'i snying po 
92a6(rgyud): brgya|rdo rje phro ba phro ba che//rab tu 'bar bas 'jigs pa'i tshogs//bdug pa chen po 'dul mdzad cing //drag cing rab drag zad par mdzad//de bzhin gshegs pa grub chen dang //chos dang chos mchog chen po'o//
92a7(rgyud): dge ba dang ni mi dge lam//byang chub sems kyis rtogs mdzad pa//shes rab thabs kyis nor chen po//thams cad dag pa pad ma che//'dod chags rnam dag ting 'dzin mchog//chags pa sna tshogs dbang 
92a8(rgyud): phyug che|nam mkha' mtha' med rtag pa'i don//'byung ba chen po kun gyi gzhi//dpal gyi 'byor pa 'byor pa'i brgyan//kun gyi bsam pa rdzogs par mdzad//phyag 'tshal 'dud cing phyag 'tshal 'dug

92b3(rgyud): na dza;u du ra ha badzra du hU~M/te na hU~M te na te te hU~M/su ra na ba ra d+ha ke tsa ra d+ha tu ku ra ma bi ni ro ma tha ro sha ka ru te badzra tu hU~M te na hU~M//bA bi n-i mud ti bi se sa g+hu ru ni/ga dza dza e e ra ma ra/ma ho badzra

92b6(rgyud): si hi a o//sa ko pa ti hU~M ki a na/du so sa ta tsa g+ho ra dan tu ka pa la pas tsa ma te sa ma na ta ya/d+hu so he ta ni/la tsi bi tsi dza la ta ro ha ro/mur ti/a na sa du ma bi ri/ha ha na/du kro te hU~M/ru tsa hU~M/
92b7(rgyud): ta kro ta d+ha yo na na ma ma hU~M//de nyid lnga'i glu dang ni/rdo rje dril bu rab tu sbyar/lnga pa'i gnas kyi klu yis//rab tu mchod pa blang bar bya//hU~M hU~M hU~M pad tsa ba nud ta/a i phan tsa na mi a sa sarba a dzo ni/tad dwa

93a1(rgyud): @#//badzra tsa li a ma a ni e ne pan tsa a ma bi na /a g+hu sha se b+ha k+Sha na/dzo du/ki li bin g+hu saM b+ha d+ha/dza u sa d+ha mi sa ru bi b+ha pi sha tsa ru//d+ha rma a mo thA+e si ha pi pa khA na ha na Na pi bu i tu h+ya/hi pu pi
93a2(rgyud): hi bu pi sa ru tu ra ban ti a man ta/la ta ru badzra g+hu sid ha/be ru ba khe a te hUM/ba ga du Sha da nyis/th+ya sa th+ya gar ni tha khu/ke ke sa ra phu bu phu ka re/ma ga bi si tsh+tsha na swAhA//a ne ma ha badzra tu hUM bi ru sid sa ru tu g+hu hUM pan tu a
93a3(rgyud): d+hu oM na me hUM/me hUM////rnal 'byor pa'i de nyid glu//kha dog lnga pa'i ni gnas//rdo rje dril bu mngon sgrags nas//sna tshogs mchod pas mchog du brtson//shri tad ta re se ra me sa ra tang twa mu li shri gu h+ye bi ra
93a4(rgyud): kan ti bu dza la sbe/shri badzra gan d+ha ra na tsa la na yo ga tud ta a su pra shri dz+nya na ba ba la me sa ra po ga pu ga ta/rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i stod nas//nye bar mchod par bya'o//rdo rje thal mo kha sbyar nas//

93a6(rgyud): chen po'i gos kyis ni//sangs rgyas lnga yis mngon par brgyan//rgyan kun gyis ni lus brgyan nas//lus gnyis rdo rje dril bu 'dzin//brgya byin lag na gdugs thogs shing //byang chub tshangs pa sgrang g.yab dang //
93a7(rgyud): me tog dkar po'i ra ba bzhin//spos dang mar me yang dag ldan//sa la'i bza' ba rgya chen po//zho dang bu ram 'bras thug dang //khyab 'jug lag na dbyug pa dang //drag po yon tan sgrogs par byed//rol pas 'phen

93b1(rgyud): sa la'i bza' ba rgya chen po//zho dang bu ram 'bras thug dang//shar gyi sgo ru byin par bya//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis bcings nas//rgyab dang rgyab du sbyar bar bya//gdugs dang ni pad ma sgrel g+hi hang dang ni dkar po 
93b2(rgyud): sgreng /dkar po kun nas sgreng ba ni//lha dang lha min grub pa'o//oM pa ti la mahA pa ti li hU~M hU~M dza swahA//shar gyi sgor spyan drang ba'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//oM b+ha hA ni mahA b+ha ha ni pra te b+ha ha ni su r+ya tra si d+hi mu khi dza ra
93b3(rgyud): dzo sa ma ra dzi u ma de bi gang ga su ru bi ti ra yang sa ga dra nag be ga ba ti/ka ra ti kar si si te po a lo u ba la yang/lo a u ra su ra dza yo bug si mahA si hU~M hU~M ho//a dkar sha ya a dkar sha ya/sa ma ya a te b+he hU~M
93b4(rgyud): hU~M phaT/swahA//spyan drangs pa'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o/dkar po g+hi hang dkris nas ni//a b+ha k+sha b+ha kA sa ma ya ti ba ti dzuM hU~M swahA//dges pa chen po'i gasngags dang phyag rgya'o//de nas byang gi sgo ru ni
93b5(rgyud): mnyam pa'i sgo ru ni//mnyam pa'i rkang pa gnan byas na//zla ba dril bu mngon sgrags shing//nyi ma de bzhin rdo rje gsor//dkar po'i maN+Dal mngon byas na//'o ma snod sogs gzhag par bya//me tog
93b6(rgyud): dkar po bdud pa dang//lha bshos bzang ba 'o ma'o//sngon gyi spyan drang phyag rgya dang//de'i sngags kyis sbyar bar bya//oM a nan b+ha su ki ti k+sha ka kar ko ta pad ma mahA pad ma sad ga pa la gu li sha pa la di bi sa do sa me so ma sing
93b7(rgyud): dan d+ha a ba la hu lon te/nan to sa ga ra pa ta pa ta maha pa ta shri kan ti su ru pa tra ho ma ho ha ra/dzi li mahA dzi li/oM a gad dza ya a gad dza ya/maha na ga a ti pa ti sa rbe bUr bu ba phuM phaT swahA//zla ba'i khu tshur byas nas ni
93b8(rgyud): rnam shes dang ni sa gdugs sgreng//gdengs ka'i tshul du rab tu sbyar//ji bzhin cho ga'i rim pa yis//byang du klu la gtor ma btang//zlum po'i tshul du 'phen pa yis//rdo rje dril bu mngon sgrags nas//nub du gnod sbyin
94a1(rgyud): @#//dbang po ni sngon gyi phyag rgyas drang ngo//dkyil 'khor dmar po mngon byas nas//metog dmar po ser po sogs//gtor ma gdug pa mar me ldan//khyad par du ni bdud rtsi lnga//oM na ma pa ta su b+ha 
94a2(rgyud): tre nan ta kyi la/pa ma si ka ka ka na Ta mahA na ta ni/tshe d+ha ni su ni d+ha ra ni na su ra pa ke lo ram ta ke la a si si ta tri ta/na ma sh+she b+ha shri ya ru san kan kan ti//rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma bka' yis//gnod sbyin  
94a3(rgyud): gnod sbyin mo 'dir shog//hU~M a pa ta ra a pa da ra/oM pa ta li pa ta li pa ta li bak+sha pra ya/rnal 'byor ma kun bza' dang btung //hU~M hU~M hU~M swAhA//lag gnyis khu tshur sgrel bcings nas//
94a4(rgyud): snying ga'i phyogs su rnam par mnan//oM d+ha na a ti pa ti li sgyu swAhA//nub phyogs su gtor ma sbyin pa'o//ji ltar mtshan nyid mtshon pa yis//rdo rje rkang pas mnan nas ni/a li ka li' sbyor bas 
94a5(rgyud): sbyor rdo rje dril bu mngon sgrags nas//yang ni mchod pa nyi shus mchod//kun nas glu blang gar bya'o//su ga ri d+ha ri i ba ya bi ya su kha saM mo ha ka ru/go ra bi ru sa bi ya sid sa ra i di man dal
94a6(rgyud): b+ha k+Sha b+ha k+Shi bi bad dza a pa ra ti ha ni a e a ka nan/bi sa ra na bu dza sa ba ri mo ba na/bkra shis cho ga rdo rjes glus//bstod pa dang ni nye bar mchod //rdo rje dril bu mngon sgragasanas//yang ni mchod pa nyi
94a7(rgyud): shus mchod Ide nas lhag ma'i gtor ma 'bul ba ni//oM ku ru ku ru pa ta li mahA pa ta li sa de de mu khe sa ma sa ni wa si ni tre ta mu khe can Ta ka ra la ru til ra pra ye sa ma ya rak+sha ti yo ka/a ma ba bi ki li ki
94a8(rgyud): li re pe bi b+ha hU na ma he bun tsa bak+sha bak+sha ha sa ga na du te du na ga ri tsan da ka ra la ka ra li ra Sha si ku ru hU~M hri h+na g+h+ri hUM/sarba du Ta na bak+sha bak+sha pho phru phe phaT swahA//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis bcing nas//dkar po g+hi hang sgreng nas ni//gnyis pa
94b1(rgyud): rtse mo kun bkug nas//kha'i 'gro du kun mngon kug nas//lhag pa'i gtor ma dud pa'i sol ba zhes bya sngags dang phyag rgya'o//bzhi mdo ri dang pha'i tshal//shing cig ri dang chu chen por//mchod pa rnam
94b2(rgyud): pa lngas mchod cing //'thor thung gsum ni ldan par dbul//de nas rdzas bcu rab ldan pas//dri zhim chu dang 'o mar sre//dung gi snod du kun bkang nas//dam tshig chu yis gsang gtor bya//oM a
94b3(rgyud): hU~M swA hA//lan bdun yongs su zlas byas pas//dam tshig chus ni rtag par bya//de nas sa phyogs re rer ni//slob ma la sogs dbang skur bya//gos kyis mig ni bkab nas su//thal mo sbyar ba byas nas
94b4(rgyud): ni/slob dpon gyi ni 'dun la sogs su//me tog dang bcas thal mo sbyar//byang chub sems bskyed smon lam btab//deng 'dir 'khor ba las rgal lo//de nas slob ma bya ba'i las//
94b5(rgyud): sa rba bA shud+ho sarba d+has badzra shud+ho haM//sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun la//rtag pa ru ni skyabs su mchi//lus can kun la skyabs su mchi//gzhan gyi nor la mi reg cing //byams dang snying rjer
94b6(rgyud): sems can la//tshul khrims sdom pa rab tu srung //oM pan ka d+ha ki ni pu tsho u pa dra na a da ma naM/nir ta ya ya mi//oM a tsar+ya u pa Sh+Ta na ni/oM sa ka la sa ta pa ra sta na bu tso pa Sh+Ta na ad ma na nir r+ya ta ya 
94b7(rgyud): mi sa ma ya s+t+wa~M//sa ma ya ho ho ho ho//a ni yig 'bru kun gyi mchog//ngag ni rdo rjer byin gyis rlabs//hU~M gi rnam pa'i ye shes kyis//snying kha'i rdo rjer byin gyis rlab//oM hi la hi li/na ga g+ha ra ta ti badzrar
94b8(rgyud): tsa le yuM yuM yuM hU~M swA hA//de 'dir ye shes 'di bzhes pas//bdud rtsi'i ro ni myang bar sbyin//oM a swA hA/dung chos kyi ni bdud rtsi lngas//sngags ni khar zlas sbyin par bya//sems ni spro ba'i rgyu yis ni//de nas
95a1(rgyud): @#//dbab par rab tu bya//snying ka rlung gis skor ba dang//dbus su rlung gi sa bon no//rkang par rlung gi dkyil 'khor bsam//snying ga'i rlung ni rab 'bar bsam//bdag nyid ye shes mkha' 'gro
95a2(rgyud): bya;'jigs pa'i hU~M ni rnam par bstan//oM li li so a//sngags bang rgyal dang ldan pas//rdo rje dril bu mngon sgrags na//rdo rje hU~M gis glu blang zhing//rab tu mi zad lus kyis gar//'dod pa'i gar gyis gar byed cing
95a3(rgyud): bdag nyid rang 'dod mnyam par bya//oM ti sta badzra sa ma ya pra ti pa la ni hU~M hU~M hU~M ho ho swa hA//gshegs su gsol ba'i sngags so//de nas me tog 'phang byas te//ji bzhin lha'i gnas su ni//sngags dang phyag rgya
95a4(rgyud): ldan par dbul//oM dz+nyA na tsa k+sha hU~M ya swahA//mig ni legs par dkrol byas la//bla ma'i zhabs kyi rtsa ba ru//g+ho btud mngon par phyag rgya'o//khyad par nyid du a rga'i snod//rang 'dod chos kyi 
95a5(rgyud): gsol ba btab//rjes su bzung zhing dka' drin mdzod//de nyid slab par bya ba ni//thal mo kha sbyar byas nas ni//oM badzra man+ha ra 'di kha ste ha~M ha~M/sa ma ya hos ma ya ho//slob ma shes pa do dam smra
95a6(rgyud): ba'i seng ge yis//rnal 'byor cho ga nyon mongs g.yos pa bcad pa'i phyir//nyon mongs skyes pa nyes pa skye ba'i sa gzhom phyir//rnal 'byor rgyal po don dam nyid du 'grub gyur cig//rtse gcig pa'i rdo rje yis//
95a7(rgyud): khyad par du ni bcings par bya//spyi bor khyung mig tu hU~M//rna ba dang sna la yum//lce la suM/mgrin par smruM/dpung par h+mU/snying khar y+mUM/lte ba dang gsang bar k+nuM/bdag nyid rgya phyag rgya yis//yan lag brgyad
95a8(rgyud): du sa bon bzung//sa ma ya ho/ma ti hU~M/'dzi ha ma ti a/dz+nya no ha;dz+nya na ha mi ni/mi dz+nya no/a ha a bar d+ha ma no haM/u oM hU~M swahA/de kho na nyid smon lam gyis//bdag nyid ye shes lus zhes brjod//gsang ba'i slab pa
95b1(rgyud): la sogs dbang skur rab tu bya//lho phyogs su slob ma'i dbang //nub phyogs su ni gnyis med do//byang du de kho na tshig go//shar gyi sgo ru bum pa yis//slob dpon gyis ni dbang skur bya//bde ba'i stan la gzhag
95b2(rgyud): nas ni//spyi bor gdugs kyis rab tu rgyan//mnyam pa'i rkang pas mnan nas ni//thal mo kha sbyar me tog bzhin//byang chub tu ni sems bskyed dang //slob dpon gyis ni sems can dkrol//bkra shis glu'i dbyangs dang ni
95b3(rgyud): rdo rje dril bu mngon bsgrags na//slob dpon nub du ltas nas ni//gnyis pa shar du yang dag lta//rdo rje sems dpa' mngon sbyar nas//zla ba nyi ma bum pa bzung //oM dz+nya no da ka kuM pa sa badzra a sha a sha a
95b4(rgyud): pe she gan du saM po de ar+ya saM pa ra u ta raM oM a phruM phaM phaT/hUM swahA//dbang bskur ba'i sngags so//dkar po g+hi hang sgral byas nas//rdo rje lcags sgrog phyag rgya yis//oM badzra ka ya hUM a swa hA//gnas
95b5(rgyud): dang gnas ni brgyad dag du//bsrung dang bcing ba'i rnam pa bya//zla ba dril bu ldan par sbyin//sangs rgyas lnga'i spyi bo ru//sngags dang phyag rgyas rnam par sbyar//rdo rje khu tshur
95b6(rgyud): dam bcings nas//dkar po dang ni g+hi haM sgreng //rtse mo sprad cing bsgrang bar bya//dud pa dri ma gsum bsgreng nas//oM hUM a swa hA//sangs rgyas lnga'i spyod pan gyi sngags dang //phyag rgya'o//de nas shar du go
95b7(rgyud): brgyad 'gro//gos bzang bzang po yang bgos nas//sangs rgyas zhing gi gnas su ni//yang ni mchod pa rnam pa bya//de nas ji ltar 'dod bzhin chos//lan gsum po ni bstan par bya//byin rlabs yang dag 
95b8(rgyud): bstan par bya//dang por slob ma la sogs//ji bzhin sems can rim dang rim//gnyis med mchod pa sna tshogs kyis//lha la rab tu mchod par bya//thams cad kun nas slob ma la//de kho na nyid rab tu bstan
96a1(rgyud): @#//rtag tu 'byor pa'i slob ma yis//slob dpon la ni yon dbul lo//rang gi bu dang chung ma dang //gnyen du dang bran du bcas pa dang //glang chen rta dang ba glang dang //yul dang 
96a2(rgyud): khang khyi zhing la sogs//gser dang dngul dang zangs dag dang //sna tshogs 'bru dang so ba dang //stan dang lha ma dang mal dang //gdugs dang longs spyod 'di rnams nyid //mtha' ru rang gi lus dang ni//slob dpon
96a3(rgyud): la ni yon du dbul//ma gus pa'i slob ma ni//de'i dngos grub ring du bstan//de nas slob dpon gyi yang mchod pa la sogs byas te//me tog gdug pa'i dri la sogs//phyag rgya'i rnam pa kun rnams 'dir//bkra
96a4(rgyud): shis klu'i rnam pa dang //rdo rje dril bu mngon sgrags nas//hU~M hU~M hU~M na tha tu hU pa ri mo tsa tu hu des yi/d+ha rma tu g+ha yi bu ru/mo k+shan tu maho k+shan tu/b+ha ba b+hi ba bu tad tra bi mo tsu ha de ba hU~M pa nam ha

96a6(rgyud): ra ma ra ma ho g+hu g+hu g+ho g+ho a sa hA//mkha' 'gro ma brgyad kyi phreng ba'i sngags so//zla ba nyi ma kha sbyar nas//me tog gis ni yang dag mchog//thams cad kun nas smon lam ni//sems dpa' chen po'i rnam
96a7(rgyud): pas byas||ma sgral ba rnams bdag gis sgral//ma grol ba rnams bdag gis grol//de ltar rjes su brtse ba'i phyir//bdag gis dkyil 'khor bya'o//sngon bzhin rgyal po'i ltar//'dir ni shis pa brjod par bya
96a8(rgyud): yon bdag gis ni kun nas g.yo//thams cad thal mo sbyar ba'i gnas//dung chos chu dang me tog gis//dbang ba'i shes pa'i brjod la sbyin//'khor ba'i sred pa dgon pa las dgrol zhing //rdo rje
96b1(rgyud): las byung bdag nyid rdo rje sems dpa' che//sangs rgyas dar sbyangs mngon bsams pas//sems can don la myur ba'i sems//zhes pas shes par brjod do//gshegs su gsol bar

96b6(rgyud): mkhas pa yis ni mchod par bya//snying po'i sngags dang phyag rgya yis//kun nas rab tu bstan par bya//rdo rje dril bu mngon sgrags nas//mchod pa nyi shus mchod par bya//oM badzra g+han d+ha sra ran te sarba bu ta pra tsa le ri

96b8(rgyud): mkha' 'gro ma ni lnga rnams dang /thams cad rnal 'byor ma la dbul//lan gnyis khu tshur bcings byas nas//ye shes rnam shes rnam par sgrel//oM a hU~M dzu~M guM suM swahA//lcags kyu lnga'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o
97a1(rgyud): @#//bdud rtsi lnga'i phyi rol du//lho dang nub dung bdug par bya//gnas dang gnas ni gzhan du yang //khrag gis rab tu gang ba'i snod//rab tu mkhas pa'i slob dpon gyis//bdud rtsi lnga ldan dbul bar

97b4(rgyud): mchog du brtson pas zla bas dbul//sngon du gsungs pa de nyid kyis//sngags brjod nas ni sbyar bar bya//gnas gsum du ni nges byas nas//phyis ni dpa'o la ni dbul//dam tshig skyod par ldan pa yis//

97b7(rgyud): dang ldan pa|ji ltar nus pa'i mchod pa'i las//las kyi rigs mchog chen po la//de bzhin nyid du so sor bzung //bla na med pa'i byang chub sems//mchog du bskyed pa byas nas ni//sems can thams cad don byed phyir
97b8(rgyud): sdom pa ma lus bzung bar bgyi//ma sgral ba rnams bdag gis sgral//ma grol ba rnams bdag gis grol//dbugs ma phyin rnams dbugs dbyung ste//sems can mya ngan 'das la dgod//lam mchog 'di ni dpal
98a1(rgyud): @#//dang ldan theg pa chen po'i 'bras bu 'byung//lam khyed lam nas gang 'gro ba'i//sangs rgyas nyid du grub par shog//sta gon sngon du ci rigs par//dam pa'i chos ni bstan par bya//bsam pa
98a2(rgyud): rdzogs pa'i slob ma la//phyi nas dkyil 'khor bstan par bya//de nas slob dpon dang gnas dang//bsam gtan la sogs rab brtson pas//rdo rje dril bu la sogs glu yis//yang na glu'i rnam pa bya//ha ra so 
98a3(rgyud): ra ma ku ta kri Ti Ni Ni Ni tu ta ra tsan su ka drad su ni pa ha bi ta ka tro ta da d+he ha da ra/dzi li ta ya ra la na dza gu tong ga la pad ma su ga so ha//oM wa badzra sa ta pa ra me tsa ra ba ra ma sa dang//lha rnams rab tu tshim byed pa//
98a4(rgyud): de nas sbyin sreg la sogs gtso//dkyil 'khor gyi ni shar phyogs dang//byang dang de bzhin nub du ni//zhi dang rgyas pa'i rim pa yis//kha khyer 'bar bas rtsam par bya//bzhi dang phyed dang bcas gnyis

98a7(rgyud): 'o ma spyan lnga sogs//'o mas byug cing sgreng bar bya//'o ma zho dang sbrang rtsi mar//sa le'i bza' bar ldan pa dang//thams cad kun nas 'bru yis ni//cho gor ldan pas sngon bzhin sreg//kun nas me'i de nyid ni//
98a8(rgyud): rab tu rtag cing spyad pa lo//me tog bdug spos byug pas ni//me 'bar ba la rab tu mchod//de bzhin dbus ma pad ma 'o//de'i dbus su rdo rje'i lha//lha rnams kun nas spyan drangs nas//de'i dbus su thim par bya//
98b1(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//gzugs kun rdzogs pa'i sems can bcas//sngags dang phyag rgya kun rig pas//sngon bzhin dngos pos mchod byas pas//sngags dang phyag rgya gong
98b2(rgyud): gsungs bzhin;;de nyid rig pas mchod par bya//sregs blugs kun nas dbul ba'i sa//lha rnams rab tu tshim par bya//oM sarba sho ti ni hU~M/phaT//gsang ba thams cad kyi sngags dang phyag rgya'o

104a1(rgyud): //'thabs gyur na//zhag gsum gyis ni 'chi bar 'gyur//'grin pa'i blo'i phyi rol gtsang //zla ba phyed las mi thub pa'o//brang gi lhe'u zhom gyur na//zla phyed snan pas gso spyad nus//sen
