104a3(rgyud): 'gram pa'i bar sha chad pa ni//drag mo'i sa bon gyis tshe bya//rna ba'i u da rnams chad na//ro langs ma'i sa bon zlas//lce'i thig 'di la ni//bdol pa mo'i sa bon bzlas//steng 'og so ni 'thams 'gyur na//
104a4(rgyud): 'grin pa'i blo'i phyi rol rtsa//stag mo'i sa bon rtag par bya//brang gi lhe'u gzhom gyur na//lce spyangs bon rtag par bya//sen mo khrag ni med pa la//'ug pa'i sa bon rtag par bya//lus kyis 'chi ba'i
104a5(rgyud): mtshan ma dag//'di ltar sa bon srung bar bya//gzhan yang srung ba bshad par bya//me sregs snod ni gnyis bzung ste//re mig dgu sogs cha dag dang //steng ma bcu gnyis cha dag la//gur gum go ra tsa
104a6(rgyud): nas gri dbus su ming ni bris nas su//sa bon gyis ni de bzhin skor//phyag 'tshal sgra ni sngon du ste//bsrung ba'i sngags ni gong du gzhag/mthar ru gzhag pa'i sa bon ni//sangs rgyas lnga yis mngon par brgyan
104a7(rgyud): sreg pa'i yig mthar mar ste//sgra yig steng stsogs mu khyud brtsa//kha phaT gnyis 'gyur sa bon dang //rdo rje ma'i lha mo'i mtshan//lnga ldan pa'i sa bon no//phyag 'tshal sreg pa mtha' ma ru//dbyig pa 'jig
104a8(rgyud): rten dbang phyug go//'gug pa phyin phyogs rdo rje phyag//lus ni 'jam pa'i dril bu mchog//de bzhin mgo ni byams pa dang //ma zhes bya ba steng 'bar ba//rnam par 'khor ba grol bar mdzad//shar gyi phyogs su
104b1(rgyud): gzhag pa ni//lha mo rdo rje nam mkha' 'gro ma//byang du drag mo zhes bya ba'i//sa bon de nyid rab tu sbyar//ro langs ma ni nub du bstan//de'i sa bon sbyar bar bya//bdol pa mo ni lho ru gnas//sa bon sngon du
104b2(rgyud): bstan pa yin//dbang ldan phyogs su de bzhin stag//bden dral phyogs su lce spyang gzhag//hU~M byed 'ug pa rlung gi phyogs//dgra bzhir gzhag pa'i sa bon ni//bzhi pa'i yi ge drug pa'o//ji ltar phyogs
104b3(rgyud): dang phyogs mtshams su//de bzhin sa bon rnam par dgod//steng du bcu gnyis gnas de la//sgra'i sa bon gzhag par bya//phyag 'tshal sreg pa mthar gzhag ste//phyogs su ma ning gzhag par bya//'bar
104b4(rgyud): byed ma dang 'jib byed ma//ngag 'phrog mkha' 'gro de bzhin te//ji bzhin gnas su gzhag par bya//phyag 'tsham sreg pa mtha' ru'o//shar du mkha' 'gro ma'i gnas//de bzhin byang du 'byar
104b5(rgyud): byed ma//nub phyogs su ni 'jib byed bstan//ngag 'phrog ma ni lho ru dgod//dbus su ming ni bri bya ste//ji ltar 'og ma bzhin du steng //gnyis ka yang na kha sbyar te//skud pa dmar pos bkri bya ste//
104b6(rgyud): kun du mnyam par gzhag par bya//tsan dan dmar pos byugs nas su//rnam pa sna tshogs bdug pa dang //dri dang brgya chen gtor ma yi//dbus su gzhag pa'i thabs 'di la//mkha' 'gro ma yis byin rlabs pa
104b7(rgyud): bdag nyid rdo rjer sbyor ba yis//rdo rje sems dpa' bdag ces brjod//rdo rje lag gi lag rtse yis//rdo rjer bsams te reg par bya//dbus su bstan pa'i sa bon 'di//brgyad dang brgya ru bzlas 
104b8(rgyud): par bya//tshe rabs sngon gyi sdig la sogs//lha nyid du ni dkri bar bya//thams cad lus la 'tshe ba yi//dus ma yin par mi 'chi ste//'tshe med nad kyis thams cad dang//nad pa'i blo bur rnal 
105a1(rgyud): @#//'byor pas;tshe zad pa'i lus rnams ni//zla ba drug du gnas par 'gyur//gnas gzhan du ni gzhag nas su//mchod pa rtag tu brtson par bya//de ltar rtag tu brtson par bya//
105a2(rgyud): de ltar bsrungs pa tsam gyis ni//rnam par shes pa mi 'gro'o//gzhan yang dbugs kyi bdag nyid ni//de'i rgyu yang yongs su rtag//dbugs dang mnyam par gnas pa yi//thams cad 'grub par bshad
105a3(rgyud): pa yin;g.yas kyi g.yas pa nyid kyis gnas//de bzhin g.yon gyi g.yon pa'o//de la gnas pa'i tshig gi rnams//thams cad 'grub par bshad pa yin//dbugs dang mi mthun pa'i tshig grub par 
105a4(rgyud): ring bar bshad pa yin//'jug dang 'byung ba'i sbyor ba yis//sbyor ba gnyis ka shes byas nas//bzhugs par thams cad bya ba yi//rgyas par yongs su grags pa ste//gal te 'byung bar byed 
105a5(rgyud): 'dod na;the tsom med par grub bar ring //dug sems kyis ni zos pa yi//de la gso dpyad bya bar bya//gong gi mtshan nyid la sogs pa'i//thams cad klu la bka' bzhin bya//dus bzhi 
105a6(rgyud): la sogs zos pa yi//gdon mi za bar 'chi bar 'gyur//gso spyad gzhan ni thams cad la//nan tan gyis ni dug bsdu'o//yi ge brgyad bkod thams cad kyis//zos pa'i lus la rnam par bkod//yi ge 
105a7(rgyud): brgyad kyi sngags dag gis//brgyad ka lus la gzhag par bya//kha tog dkar por kun bsams nas//snying pos dug ni 'phrog par bya//dkyil 'khor dbus su gzhag nas kyang //shes dang rnam shes kyis
105a8(rgyud): dgag bya;lus la bsrungs ma thag tu ni//de'i 'og du phyi las bya//snod la chu yis bkang nas su//'o ma yis kyang bsre bar bya//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyis brgyan pa'i//dbus su pad ma bsams nas ni//
105b1(rgyud): rdo rje mkha' 'gro bsam par bya//gdugs dkar la sogs brgyan pa yi//phyag gnyis sems dpa'i dkyil dkrungs bcas//sbrul gyis brgyan gyis brgyan pa yi//kha tra~M kha dang thod pa dang //shin du
105b2(rgyud): mdzes pa'i na bzas glubs//klu brgyad kyi ni 'go brgyad rnams//pad ma'i gdan la kun du gnon//shar du bsam pa nor rgyas bu//de bzhin byang du 'jog po ste//nub gyi glu dbang stobs kyi rgyu//lho phyogs su ni
105b3(rgyud): pad ma bstan pa pad ma chen po dbang ldan phyogs//me'i phyogs su dud skyong ste//rigs ldan zhes bya bden bral phyogs//rlung gi phyogs su hu len te//klu brgyad po ni mgo brgyad rnams//bdan du mnon par bsam par bya//mchod
105b4(rgyud): pa'i dngos po thams cad dang //me tog bdug spos gtor ma sbyin//bdag gi lus ni rdo rje ste//rdo rje sems dpar bsam par bya//rma bya'i ni mthongs kyis su//chu'i sbyor ba bya ba yin//kun du drangs nas rkang par ru//
105b5(rgyud): rkang pa nas ni tshur thim bya//om hi ta tan tra dan tra hU~M phaT/rdo rje sems dpa' mngon sbyor ba'i//snyen pa dag ni 'bum du bya//oM ba su ki hri ta tan da ra hU~M swA hA//oM kar ko ta hri Ta dan da ra swahA//oM tra kA ka hi tra
105b6(rgyud): dan da ra swahA//o~M pad ma hri Ta dan da ra swa hA//oM a mahA pad ma hri Ta dan da raswa hA//oM sham ka pa la hri Ta dan da ra swaha//oM ku li sha pa la hri Ta tan da ra swahA//oMhU~M lu ke hri Ta dan da ra swahA //oM hri ta dan da ra swahA//hU~M hU~M hU~M/
105b7(rgyud): phaT phaT phaT//che ge mo zhig dug med par gyis shig//hU~M swahA//zhes brjod cing /'o ma'i nang du drangs nas ni//'phral du 'o ma nag por 'gyur//yungs dkar dkar po'i me tog dang //tsan dan gyis ni sres pa'i//zos pa
105b8(rgyud): yi ni snying ga ru//bsngags brjod nas ni 'deg par bya//snying ni g.yos ma thag tu yang //rnam shes de ni sdams de gzhin//rnam shes bsdams ma thag tu ni//de bzhin dbugs ni dgod par byed//dbugs ni dgod ma thag tu yang //
106a1(rgyud): @#//rang gi dbugs kyis gtso bar bya//dbugs kyis rtses pa tsam gyis ni//rka lag thams cad g.yo bar byed//rka lag thams cad g.yos pa dang //bdag nyid dbyangs ni blangs par bya//hU~M zhes bya ba'i dbyangs 
106a2(rgyud): blangs nas//se gol gsum gyis slang bar bya//zin pa slangs ma thag tu yang //de nyid bdag gis bsrung bar bya//sdus pa glu'i gnas su ni//'o ma de ru blug par bya//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//rdo rje sems 
106a3(rgyud): dpa' mngon sprul nas//'bum phrag gsum du bzlas pa yi//bsnyen pa la sogs sngon du bya//rnal 'byor pa yis dug bsdu ba//'grub par 'di la the tsom med//gtong dang thig le dmig dang ni//spyi bo 'grin pa sna dag 
106a4(rgyud): dang //snying ga gsang ba lte ba dang //'the bo gnyis dang rkang gnyis su//sos ka de ru sos btab na//rgyu des 'chi bdag tu ni bstan//char dbab pa'i cho ga mchog//nyon cig de nyid rgyal po che//chu'i 'gram dang 
106a5(rgyud): nye ba'i //yid du 'ong ba'i sa phyogs su//bla re dag ni bre bya ste//na bza' sna tshogs dpyang bar bya//mtsho rtsi rnam pas lnga yis//rdul tshon la sogs bri bar bya//gru bzhi pa sgo bzhi pa//
106a6(rgyud): klu brgyad kyis ni rab brgyan te//mgo gnyis kyis ni bsgo byas nas//gzhug mas dgra ru dkri bar bya//rgyal ba bzang po shar gyi phyogs//byang gi phyogs su dpa' dga'o//char 'bebs mkha' 
106a7(rgyud): bsdud byang gi phyogs//mi g.yo drag po lho phyogs su//klu brgyad kyi ni ra ba ste//phyogs su rigs ni brgyad pa yi//nor rgyas bu ni shar gyi phyogs//'jog po byang gi phyogs su ste//nub du stobs kyis rgyu ba'i klu 
106a8(rgyud): de bzhin pad mo lho'i phyogs//pad ma chen po dbang ldan phyogs//me'i phyogs su dung skyong ste//bden dral phyogs su rigs skyong ngo //rlung gi phyogs su lhu min d+ha//sprin gyi dbang phyug rgyal chen po//phyogs brgyad nas 
106b1(rgyud): ni dgug par bya//ag tsar can dang nga ro can//drag po can dang dkrigs pa can//sra ba can dang rtum po can//tshar 'bebs can dang rab gang can//brgyad po rgyal po zhes bya'o//gang gis shar phyogs
106b2(rgyud): gzhag pa yi//phyogs kyi 'dog tu sgom par bya//dbus su rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma//pad ma'i bdan gyi steng na bzhugs//kha traM thod pa phyag na snams//sbrul gyi rgyan gyi brgyan pa yi//kha dog dkar po 'od 'bar ba//shin tu mdzes
106b3(rgyud): pa'i na bza' gsol//sbrul gyi pad mo skor ba yi//sbrul gyi bdan du sbyor ba'o//rgya che ba'i mchod pa rnams//lo ma snod la sogs pa brgyad//me tog bdug spos dri dag dang //mar me rnam pa sna tshogs gzhan//rnal
106b4(rgyud): 'byor ye shes kyis bsam pa//ye shes mkha' 'gro bdag nyid do//oM b+hur bu ba swA hA//nye bar spyod pa'i sngags so/oM ba su khi hU~M swA hA//oM ta k+sha ka hU~M swA hA/oM kar ko ta hU~M swA hA//o~M pad ma hU~M swA hA//
106b5(rgyud): oM ma hA pad ma hU~M/oM sham ka pa la hU~M swA hA//o ku li sha pa la hU~M swA hA //oM hu lun ta ko hU~M swA hA//klu'i rgyal ba brgyad kyi sngags so//oM g+hi li g+hi li b+hi li sha b+ha ri sha pa ya hU~M hU~M hU~M ho ho ho//
106b6(rgyud): tha tha tha swA hA//lag pa gnyis ni skor nas su//phyogs brgyad du ni bstan par bya//srin gyi rgyal po brgyad kyi sngags dang /phyag rgya'o/g.yon pa khu tshur bcang bar bya//'the bo 'the chung bsgreng bar bya//g.yas
106b7(rgyud): kyi gdengs ka'i tshul du bya//klu brgyad kyi ni phyag rgya'o//oM badzra d+ha gi na b+ha ri sha ya hU~M b+hur bu ba swA hA//rtsa ba'i sngags so//'bum phrag gsum gyi sngags dang ni//snyen pa la sogs sngon du bya//de ltar las ni
106b8(rgyud): rnal 'byor pas//char dbab pa'i mchog yin no//gzhan yang pho nya'i mtshan ma bshad par bya'o//mig yon mgo skya skur ba dang //'jig gos la sogs dbyig thogs dang //bud med dag ni zung 'ong ba//
107a1(rgyud): @#//skra grol la sogs du ba dang//bcer bu dang ni rings pa'i ngag//bzhi po 'chi ba'i dus yin no//de nas bdag nyid pho nya dang//gnyi ga yongs su 'grogs 'gyur te//gal te blo bur 'gro 'dod
107a2(rgyud): na|rtag tu sa stengs dngos grub med //rab tu byed pa zhes bya ba'i le'u gnyis pa rdzogs s+hA+o////rnal 'byor brtson pa'i bye brag nyid//gang gi gsang sngags 'tshal bgyi zhing//ji ltar sbyor ba zung bgyi
107a3(rgyud): ba|bcom ldan 'das la bdag nyan 'tshal//de bzhin lha nyid bdag gi rnas//nyon cig rdo rje de kho na//dang po stong pa bsam bya ste//lus kyi dri ma kru bar bya//gzugs stong la sogs dang po nas//ji snyed bcwa brgyad yid
107a4(rgyud): kyi bar||gzugs kyi khams nyid stong byas te//de bzhi du ni sgra yang bya//dri'i khams nyid stong pa ste//de bzhin du ni ro yang bya//rig g-i khams nyid stong pa la//de bzhin du ni chos kyang bya//
107a5(rgyud): mig gi bdag nyid stong pa la//dbus su rnam shes ji ltar gnas//rna ba'i bdag nyid stong pa la//dbus su rnam shes ji ltar gnas//sna'-i bdag nyid stong pa la //dbus su
107a6(rgyud): rnam shes ji ltar 'gyur//le'i bdag nyid stong pa la/dbus su rnam shes ji ltar gzhag//lus kyi bdag nyid stong pa la//dbus su rnam shes ji lta 'gyur//yid dang phyi rol stong pa'i//yid
107a7(rgyud): kyang rnam par brtag par bya//yid ni nam mkhar thim bsams nas//thams cad stong par bsam par bya//ri rab kling kun 'jig pa dang//khams gsum skyes pa yang 'jig par 'gyur//
107a8(rgyud): thams cad stong par stsogs dmigs nas//phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams kun dang//steng dang bar dang 'og dag gi//gzugs la sogs pa stong bsam+sa te//bdag gi dngos po stong byas nas//
107b1(rgyud): dngos po nam mkhar thim par bya//sbyor ba mnyam par kun bsams te//rnal 'byor mkhas pa de dag gis//de'i dbus su mthong ba ni//de la yongs su rtag pa'i rgyu//'bar ba'i sa ni zhi ba dang 
107b2(rgyud): tshag tshig 'khrigs pas rgyas pa ste//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor snang ba ni//chags pa'i las la sogs pa 'grub//de 'jug du ba gnag mthong na//mngon par spyod pa rab tu 'grub//rlung gi 'dog ltar long 
107b3(rgyud): long pos|skad cig gis ni dgar ba'i las//kha dog dkar po zla ba 'dra//dkar por thams cad mthong gyur na//sbyor ba'i dngos grub rnam shes te//blo ldan go 'phang dbyibs rtogs pa'o//
107b4(rgyud): phyi na sbyor ba 'byung ba'i//myu gu la sogs pa'i sems//gang gis dngos por stong shes na//myur du sems ni grol bar 'gyur//ye shes kyis ni shes bya rtag//shes byas 'gro bar rtag byas te//pha dang ma ni
107b5(rgyud): gsad pa dang /rgyal po rtsang sgra bram ze dang //yul phyogs rgyu ba gsad pa ni//mi de dag pa'i ming zhes bya//rab tu byed pa zhes bya ba stong par sbyor ba'i le'u gsum pa rdzogs s+ho////nyon cig de nyid
107b6(rgyud): ji ltar lam|gnyis dang gnyis med rab spangs pa//'khor ba'i rgya mtsho mi zad pa'i//chu'o dgra las grol ba '+si//mtshan bya mtshon pa'i mtshan nyid rnams//rnam shes ye shes kyis bsam bya//dkyil
107b7(rgyud): du bying ba'i lus can rnams//grol mtshan pa zhes bya ba'o//ye shes kyis ni shes pa rtag//shes byas 'gro ba mthong ba'i sems kyis ni//rang 'dod lam ni rtogs par 'gyur//'khor bar 'gyur ba'i zhags
107b8(rgyud): pa yis||glang chen gnad du rgyab pa bzhin//tshe rabs snga ma rtogs chub pa//rnal 'byor pa ni stong la 'ga'//gong du mtshan nyid stan pa ltar//mnyam nyid stong pa kun du bsam//stong pa'i zhing la sogs pa lus la//blo
108a1(rgyud): //dang ldan pas sa bon btab//dper na gdol pa'i khang pa mes bsregs nas//lha la sogs pa'i ched du gnas byas la//dge dang rigs can mkhas pa kun du gnas//de ltar ting 'dzin
108a2(rgyud): sems kyis 'joms pa'o//dgod bya yi ge rnam bkod pa//spyi bor me'i sa rtag bya ste//sa gnyis dag gi sa bon gyis//dmig du de'i sbyar ba bya//stong pa rlung gi sa bon gyis//sna dang rna ba dag la bas
108a3(rgyud): bdud rtsi chu'i sa bon gyis//lce'i dbang po dbang phyug rtso//gang dag brtsegs pa'i rims bzung ste//gnas bzhi dag du sbyar bar bya//'grin par rmongs pa'i sa bon gyis//de bzhin nyon mongs dpung
108a4(rgyud): pa gnyis||snying gi phyogs su g.yo ba ste//lte ba'i drung par phyugs dag ni//yan lag brgyad la bkod pa ni//sa bon brgyad ni bzung bar bya//sa bon dang ldan lus kyi ni//bdag nyid ye shes mkha' 'gro
108a5(rgyud): ma|nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyis brgyan pa'i//pad ma'i bdan la bzhugs pa la//gdugs dkar po'i mngon g.yogs pa//btso ma gser gyi 'bar ba 'dra//zhal gsum phyag ni 
108a6(rgyud): drug pa ste||sems dpa' dkyil mo dkrung gis bzhugs//dbu brgyan steng du rdo rje brgyan//dar gyis dkris shing rnam par dbyangs//spyan gsum khro la bzhad pa ste//shin tu mdzes pa'i na 
108a7(rgyud): bzas blubs||ral gri dgra sta 'khor lo bsnams//de bzhin g.yon pas sdig 'dzub dang //rtse gcig kha T+wAM yang bsgreng ba//rtags kyi phyug rgya mngon par 'dzin//sems can don byed brtson pa ni//'od zer 'bar
108a8(rgyud): ba mang bo'i||sangs rgyas srin ltar spro bar bya//dbugs mi g.yo bar bsgoms bya zhing //yid kyi shes pa rnam par shes//shes bzhin du ni bsam par bya//a li ka li rab sbyor ba//de'i sa bon 'byung ba yis//
108b1(rgyud): smin par thigs pa'i gnas dag tu//ye shes can gyi sa bon sgo ma//bzlas pa'i sa bon ye shes ni//sangs rgyas lnga 'is mngon par brgyan//me dang rlung gi bdun den la//rdo rje sa bon gyis skul zhing//
108b2(rgyud): thigs dang 'dren par yang dag ldan//bdud rtsi char rgyun bshad pa yin//gong du bla ma'i yi ge dang//bsreg pa'i yi ge mthar gzhag ste//ting nge 'dzin du ldan pa ru//'bum khrag gsum du bzlas par bya//de ltar 
108b3(rgyud): bzlos pa'i rnal 'byor pa//de'i bsam pa 'grub par 'gyur//las brgyad la sogs sngon du ni//sngon du snyen pa byas nas su//de 'og las shig rtsams nas ni//'grub 'gyur 'di la the tsom med//rmongs par
108b4(rgyud): bya ba'i bye brag ni//nyon cig rdo rje de kho na//dang po rdo rje dang dag dang ni//sde tshan gong gi yi ge dang//ji ltar sde tshan bzhi pa'i dang po'i yi ge bzung bya ste//nyis 'gyur ra yig steng du dang//i zhes pa dang ra yig
108b5(rgyud): sbyor /ji ltar sde tshan gnyis pa'i//yi ge gnyis bzung thigs pa la//lcags kyu yis ni rtse byas nas//bzhi pa yi ni gsum pa'i//gnyi ga gnyis bun brjod par bya//lnga pa dag ni sbyor ba dang//gang du
108b6(rgyud): phyag 'tshal yi ge dang//yi ge drag po mtha' mar bzhag//de'i sa bon zlas brtson pas//dngos po thams cad brtson par 'gyur//lag pa gnyi ga sngam bya zhing//de nyid kha la sbyar byas te//khro bo dbu ma rkyang bya zhing
108b7(rgyud): gnyi ga reg cing reg par bya//chan pa'i phyag rgya 'di yis ni//'bar ba'i 'phreng ba lang long 'od//dgra'i 'grin pa la sogs yid//dngos po thams cad spyod par 'gyur//de nas char spyod bshad par bya//bya ba thaMs
108b8(rgyud): cad chu la sogs nya ji ltar sde tshan tha ma'i//yi ge gsum pa bzung bar bya//ji ltar sde gsum pa'i//de bzhin yi ge gsum pa la//gong gi sa bon gsum pa'i cha//yi ge e ni ldan par bya//lnga pa ngag gi dbang 
109a1(rgyud): @#//pho la;lcags kyu yis ni rtse byas te//gsum pa chu'i sa bon dang//tsheg drag po'i bar dag du//rdo rje sa bon tha ma yi//ra yig lcags kyu zhags pa dang//'joms pa'i yige mthar gzhag ste
109a2(rgyud): phyag 'tshal gong du gzhag par bya//rmongs byed sa bon rab tu grags//lag gnyis thal mo sbyar byas te//srin lag gzhan dang gzhan du bya//khro bo yis ni kun mnan te//srin lag gnyis dang dbu ma yi//
109a3(rgyud): de nyid du ni 'the chung sbyar//rta dgod ma'i phyag rgya'o//rta dgod ma'i phyag rgya'is//'bar ba'i 'phreng ba mnyam pa'i 'od//phyogs bcu'i gnas su 'bar byed ma//srin la sogs pa bsreg par byed//char 
109a4(rgyud): spyod pa'i dus dag tu//phyag rgya 'di yis sbyar bar bya//snyen pa sngon du byas nas su//rnal 'byor pa yis 'bum du zlas//rab tu byed pa la sogs bdag gi snyen pa la sogs pa'o//bdag 
109a5(rgyud): gi rdan zhes bya ba rdzogs s+ho////gzugs brnyan dag ni ci 'dra dang//zhi ba'i sbyin sreg bgyi ba dang//ji ltar gtor ma mchod pa rnams//bcom ldan bdag ni gnyan par 'tshal//nyon 
109a6(rgyud): cig de kho na;gzugs brnyan la sogs 'dun bsam pa//sngags dang phyag rgya sbyin sreg dang//zlas pa bsam gtan stom pa rnams//me'i las swogs kyi sngon//blo dang ldan pas shes par zlas//
109a7(rgyud): sa ni sbyangs ma thag tu yang//mu khyud mtshan nyid mchog rtson pas//sor brgyad lhag ni yan chad du//khru gang tsam nas rtsams nasu//ji snyed khru stong bar du'o//brgyad pa dgra ni 'joms pa ste//de bzhin
109a8(rgyud): bcu pa rgyas pa dang//bcu gnyis pa dang dbang du ldan//de bzhin bcu bzhi zhi ba ste//sor mo bcu drug tshad dag gis//mngon par rigs ni 'phel bar byed//bco brgyad dag gi gnas su yang//ba lang   
109b1(rgyud): rigs la sogs pa'i bskyed//sor mo nyi shu'i mu khyud rnams//kha khyer yul 'khor zhi ba'o//gang gi cha ni gsum las ni//cha gnyis su ni rko bar bya//rgos pa las kyang brgyad kyi cha//khung gi kha ran du yang bya//kha ran gyi
109b2(rgyud): phyed kyi char phyi rol du yang mu khyud bya//mu khyud rgyad du ldan byas pa'i//pad ma 'dab ma brgyad pa ste//pad ma dag ni phyi rol du//so sor gcig pa'i ra ba bya//khung gi dbus su rdo rje ni//pad ma'i steng du gzhag par bya//
109b3(rgyud): mu khyud gsum ni mngon spyod pa'i//zhi ba la ni gru bzhi pa//yongs su gang ba dbang dang ldan//rgyas pa la yang de bzhin no//zla gam dag ni dkar po dang//zur brgyad dgug dang rmongs pa'o//go rims rim par bstan pa 'di
109b4(rgyud): zhi ba rgyas pa dbang las sogs//sangs rgyas lnga'i rtags dag gis//ji bzhin phyogs dang phyogs su dgod//gnas bzhi dag ni kha ran du//sangs rgyas rtags kyis mngon par brgyan//zla gam rdo rje rab mdzes rnams//
109b5(rgyud): gra dang grwa ru gzhag par bya//ci rigs stan gyi gnas dag du//'the//bo bcu gnyis tshad dag gis//skyes bu che dang ldan pa dang//ti se gangs ri rtsegs dge ba//'dab ma brgyad la mngon ldan pa//rims bzhin lha ni
109b6(rgyud): bzhugs su gsol//rnal 'byor pa yis de'i tshad//khyad par du ni sbyang bar bya//oM hi li hi li ka li pe ta le hU~M phaT//'di ni nye bar spyod pa'i sngags so//bdag gis lus kyang sprul byas nas//ye shes mkha' 'gro sgom
109b7(rgyud): bya zhing /yan lag brgyad du bkod pa yi//thams cad bdag gi gnas su'o//lce la chu 'di ji bzhin du//nam mkha' rna ba sna gnyis dang//mig las 'di sbyar bar bya//de bzhin me'i tshangs pa dang//rmongs pa ded dpon
109b8(rgyud): 'grin pa ste/g.yo ba snying la gnas pa dang//thams cad nyon mongs thong ga dang//phyugs bdag lte ba'i drung bar te//'di'i lha'i nyid shes byas nas//yan lag brgyad du bkod pa'i sa//ba dan gsum gyi phyag rgya yis//
110a1(rgyud): /bdag nyid yan lag brgyad du gtugs//bdag nyid ye shes mkha' 'gro ru//bdag gi gzugs ni sprul byas nas//kha dog dkar stsogs kun 'da' zla//thams cad rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i//
110a2(rgyud): phyag gnyis sems dpa'i dkyil dkrung bcas//shin tu mdzes pa'i na bza' gsol//kha traM rdo rje phyag na snams//'bar ba'i 'phreng ba 'dra ba'i 'od//bdag gi gzugs ni mnyam gzhag nas//dbugs 
110a3(rgyud): mi g.yo bar sgom par bya//de nas sa ni sbyang bya ste//oM ha na ha na kro d+he hU~M phaT//lag pa g.yas pa rkyang bya ste//rdo rjer bsgoms nas rdeg par bya'o//sa sbyang ba'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//lag gnyis khu tshur 
110a4(rgyud): bcangs nas su//bar ma gnyis ka rkyang ba ni//phyag rgya 'di yis kun dag 'gyur//oM sri swahA//mar sbyang ba'i sngags so//oM oM swahA//rjes gsang ba'i sngags so//oMdze swahA//'bru sbyang ba'i sngagaso//
110a5(rgyud): oM ku ru ku ru swahA//zan dang rdzas la sogs pa sbyang ba'i sngags so//o a swahA//yam shing sbyang ba'i sngags so//me'i dbus pad ma bsams nas ni//me'i lha ni dgug par bya//g.yon pa khu 
110a6(rgyud): tshur bcangs nas su//dbu ma khro bo sgreng ba'o//dbu ma'i tshigs ni gsum par ru//khro bo skums te bzhag par bya//oM a gang tsha a gang tsha//ag ni dza dza swahA//pad ma'i bdan la bzhugs pa ni//ral pa ser 
110a7(rgyud): dang ldan pa//bgrang phreng 'gun d+he bu ram 'dog//shin tu mdzes pa'i gos ldan pa//me 'bar ba dang mnyam pa'i 'od//me'i dbus su bsgom par bya//dngos po lnga'i mchod pa yis//de la ji ltar 'os par ru//
110a8(rgyud): me'i me'i lce la ni//thams cad lan gsum sbyin sreg bya//lag gnyis thal mo sbyar byas te//pad ma'i tshul gyis dbye bar bya//kun nas dbus su bzhugs pa ni//me'i snying po phyag rgya'o//oM ho dzu ho dzu swahA//
110b1(rgyud): me'i snying po'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//nan tan se gol gsum rtogs te//phyi nas 'bar ba gsum rtogs te thim par bya//rjes su sbyang ba rtsam pa ni//me'i dbus su kun rig pas//oM hU~M ta ti hU~M phaT//
110b2(rgyud): lag na rdo rje dang ldan pas//chu dang nyungs dkar me tog gis//phyogs bcu nas ni gsang rtor bya//me'i dbus su gtor bar bya//me sbyang ba'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o/oM badzra pra ka ra hU~M phaT//
110b3(rgyud): lag pa g.yon pa brgyab bstan te//lag pa g.yas pa 'dun du sbyar nas//gnyi ka dbang ldan phyogasu bskor//ra ba'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o //lag pa gnyi ka rkyang bya zhing //rtse mo phan tshun bre bar bya//oM dzum
110b4(rgyud): swa hA /rdo rje dra ba'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//g.yon pa khu tshur bcangs nas su//'the bo 'og tu gzugs par bya//oM bu~M swa hA/phur bu'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyis brgyan
110b5(rgyud): pa'i /me dbus pad ma bsam pa ni//gdugs dkar po yis bkab pa yis//dbus su rdo rje bsam par bya//rtse lnga pa'i rdo rje ni//kha tog dmar po bsgom par bya//'od zer 'bar ba mang
110b6(rgyud): po las//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus dag tu//rdo rje gzugs ni bskyed par bya//bdag gi dbugs las phyung ba yi//las kyi rim pas kha dog gzugs//phyag gnyis sbyor ba'i gnas dag la//kha tra~M
110b7(rgyud): rdo rje phyag na bsnams//thams cad rgyan gyis brgyan pa yis//gang dag bdag gi lha nyid la//ye shes mkha' 'gro thim byas nas//cho ga bzhin du me dbusu//rnam pa sna tshogs dngos pos mchod//oM tha tha tha
110b8(rgyud): swa hA//sbyin sreg gi rtsa ba'i sngags so//g.yas kyi phyag du be con snaMs//thod pa g.yon du bsam par bya//oM hU~M swAhA/rtsa ba'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//oM ti ga sna gan d+ha hU~M swAhA//dri ba'i sngags so//
111a1(rgyud): @#//oM pus pa ma le hU~M swAhA/metog gi sngags so//oM tig sna du be hU~M swAhA//mar me'i sngags so//oM du pe sha ri swahA//spos me'i sngags so//oM ku ru ku ru mahA b+ha le hU~M swAhA//gtor ma'i
111a2(rgyud): sngags so||lag pa g.yas pa rkyang ba ni dri'i phyag rgya'o//lag pa de nyid rkang pa las//'the bo 'dzub mo sbyar ba ni//me tog gi phyag rgya'o//me tog gi phyag rgya las//bar ma sgreng ba ni/bdug pa'i phyag rgya'o
111a3(rgyud): lag pa gnyis rkyang nas dbu ma dang //'the bo skyod pa ni//gtor ma'i phyag rgya'o//sbyin sreg zhal gyi lcags la ni//lan cig zlas shing lan cig sbyin//bdun las lhag par bzung nas ni//dbus su ran pa'i 
111a4(rgyud): kham sbyin no|gong ma'i tshul gyis gnas nas ni//bdun las lhag pa kun bsdus nas//sbyin sreg byas pa'i lhag ma rnams//mchog tu lhag pa rgyu lhag sreg//sreg pa byas nas lhag ma rnams
111a5(rgyud): bdag gi lus la bsdu bar bya//sbyin sreg byas pas kun rdzogs pa//rdzogs pa'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o snod la mar gyis bkang byas te//de'i dbus su dbul bar bya//dbugs kyi 
111a6(rgyud): dngos por drangs nas su//rang gi lus la thim par bya//de nas bdag gi lus nyid la//mkhas pa yis ni sgom par bya//me'i lha'i dbus su ni//mkhas pa yis ni bsam par bya//lhag ma lan gsum sreg bya
111a7(rgyud): mchod pa gtor ma sbyin byas shing //bkra shis pa'i dbyangs blangs shing //phyag rgya de nyid sbyar bar bya//gzhan yang bsreg las bshad par bya//sa lu 'bras dang 'bru la sogs//til dkar po dang yungs dkar dang
111a8(rgyud): nas dang ras sbal 'bras bu dang //mar dang 'o mas sres pa dang //sbrang rtsi sres pa'i 'o thug dang //sbyin sreg pa'i khyad par dang //'o ma can gyi shing lnga yi//sor mo bcu'i tshad dag gis//zhi ba'i dus su ldan pa yi
111b1(rgyud): sbyin sreg gi ni cho ga mchog//sran ma la sogs nag po'i til//nas dang dmar po'i so ba dang//dri dang gur gum 'o ma dang//mar dang sbrang rtsir sbyar ba dang//mar dang bsres pa'i 'o thug dang//sbyin
111b2(rgyud): sreg dag gi khyad par gyis//'o ma can gyi shing lnga ni//de bzhin tshad ni bcu pa ste//gal te rgyas par 'dod pa la//rnal 'byor pa'i bye brag go//mar po til dang dang byas pa'i//na ga ge sar pri yang
111b3(rgyud): ku : /ske tshe de bzhin 'u su dang//thams cad sbrang rtsir sbyar ba dang/thang shing b+ha ta ji bzhin 'bar//b+ha sa ka dang klu shing dang//bcu gnyis dag gi tshad kyis ni//chags pa'i las kyis mchog yin no/
111b4(rgyud): nag po til dang sran la sogs//b+ha la ta'i 'bras bu dang//yu mar khu bsros pa dang//khrag dag gis ni sres pa yi//lung dang shing la gug pa dang//de bzhin b+ha la ti'i shing//sor mo
111b5(rgyud): brgyad kyi tshad dag gis//mngon par spyod par rab tu grags//sren sde'u yungs dkar sran ljang dang//lam gyi bdul gyis sres pa dang//khrag dang dug dang yungs dmar gyis//
111b6(rgyud): de dag kun dang sres pa yi//gser dag gi ni shing dag dang//bya rog gis ni sbyor ba'i shing//dgar bar yongs su grags pa yin//yang dag las kyi sbyin sreg bya//sha dang nya la sogs pa'i rdzas
111b7(rgyud): thams cad 'bru dang bag phye dang//khrag dang sbrang rtsir sres pa dang//bya rog sgras ni sbyar byas la//bya rog tshang ba'i shing dag dang//so shing dag gi lhag ma yis//de dag las su byas nas ni//bya ba thaMs
111b8(rgyud): cad rmongs par 'gyur//de nas slob ma bzung ba yi//mtshan nyid du ni bshad par bya//yan lag thams cad rdzogs pa'o//shin du dul ba'i spyod pa dang//chos gsum la ni mngon dga' zhing//
112a1(rgyud): @#//theg pa che la dga' ba'i blo//sngags dang rgyud la sbyor ba dang//thams cad gzo stsogs rtogs 'dod cing//sbyor ba'i bsam gtan mngon dga' ba'i//nyan dang klog stsogs brtson pa dang/
112a2(rgyud): rtag tu bla ma la gus dang//sbyin pa la sogs dag pa la dga'//de bzhin 'dod la mi dga' ba'i//longs spyod la ni mi gnas pa'i//nga rgyal 'khro ba med pa dang//tshul khrims 'chal dang tshig rtsub yin
112a3(rgyud): slob dpon dang ni bla ma dang//sems can kun la smod pa dang//ngan pa'i bdag nyid gdug sems dang//rtag tu sdig pa'i blo can dang//tshig kyal dag rtsub smra ba rnams//'di dag 'dra ba spang bar bya//
112a4(rgyud): gzhan gyi bdan zhes bya ba le'u dang po rdzogs s+ho////chu'i sbyin stsogs ji ltar lags//chu'i sbyin stsogs de la yang//mtshan nyid ji ltar 'chal bar bgyi//ngo 'tshar lha la bdag nyan
112a5(rgyud): 'tshal|chu'i mtshan nyid yang dag pa//nyon cig rdo rje de kho na//bla re dag ni bre ba dang//na bza' sna tshogs rab rgyan te//shin du rgya che gru gzhi pa//dri yis kun du byugs nas ni//dbus su
112a6(rgyud): chu'i snod la sogs//dkar po'i rdzas kyis sku bar bya//chu dang 'o ma sres pa yis//yongs su mdzes pa'i chus bkang nas//bum pa dag ni brgyad rnams kyis//phyogs brgyad dag du gzhag par bya//dri'i chu
112a7(rgyud): yis kun bkang nas//shing lo sngon pos rgyan nas su//bdag nyid ye shes mkha' 'gro ru//yud tsam gyis ni skyed nas su//me tog bdug spos dri la sogs//bdag nyid dngos pos mchod byas te//
112a8(rgyud): de nas phyi rol gnas pa yi//chu'i las la sogs pa yi//lag pa gnyi ga sbyar nas su//pad ma'i chu yis dbye bar bya//oM ka ma la be swahA//pad ma'i sngags dang phyag rgya'o//zla ba'i dkyil
112b1(rgyud): 'khor gyis brgyan pa'i//chu'i dbus su pad ma dgod//gdugs dkar mo yis g.yogs pa yi//sa bon dbus su sgom par bya//sa bon gyi ni gzugs bskyed la//bdag gi dbugs las phyung nas su
112b2(rgyud): phyag gnyis sems dpa'i dkyil dkrung bcas//sbrul gyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa la//phyag gnyis rdo rje dbyug pa snams//kha tog dkar po rab mdzes pa//'khro zhing 'dzum la ldem bag can//
112b3(rgyud): shin du mdzes pa'i na bza' mnams//dam tshig sngags dang mngon sbyor ba'i//dam tshig phyag rgya la sogs bstan//lag gnyis phan tshun khu tshur bcangs//'dzub mo gnyis ni skeng bar bya//rtse mo kug
112b4(rgyud): nas gzhag ba bya//dam tshig phyag rgya zhes ni stan//oM sa ma ya ti sta hU~M phaT//dam tshig gi sngags dang phyag rgya'o//shar du rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma//byang du drag mo zhes bya ba//nub phyog
112b5(rgyud): su ni ro langs ma//rdol pa mo ni lho ru gnas//seng ge zhes bya dbang ldan phyogs//me'i phyogasu de bzhin stan/lce spyang bdag ni bden dral phyogs//rlung phyogs 'ug pa'i hU~M sgrogs ma//
112b6(rgyud): so sor rtags ni stan bya ba//dam tshig gi ni phyag rgya bzhin//rang gi gzugs kyi ska mdog ldan//sngags dang phyag rgya bstan pa'o//me tog gdug spos dri la sogs//mkhas pa yis ni mchod par bya//
112b7(rgyud): rdo rje khu tshur gnyis bcings te//g.yon gyis snying khar gzhag nas su//g.yas pa steng du gzhag bya ba//rtsa ba'i phyag rgyar rab tu grags//oM dzuM hUM swahA//'di ni rtsa ba'i sngags so//kun d+ha me tog dkar
112b8(rgyud): la sogs;;dur pa yis ni btsugs nas su//lha mo'i steng du dar ba ni//rdo rje spyod pan la ni dbul//lan cig sngags ni brjod bya ste//lan cig sbyor bar dbul bar bya//lan ni khri ru byas pa yis//tshe ni
113a1(rgyud): @#//kun nas 'phel bar 'gyur //ka ra bi ra'i me tog dmar//dur ba dang ni ldan pa yis//lan ni khri ru byas 'gyur na//khyim ni zhi bar 'gyur ba yin//yam shing klu'i me tog la//'o ma yis
113a2(rgyud): ni sres nas su//lan grangs khri ru phul byas na//de bzhin nyid du drod zhi ba'o//pad ma'i yaM shing gis//lan grangs khri ru phul ba yis//yul phyogs zhi bar 'gyur ba yin/thams cad la swogs
113a3(rgyud): sbyar bya 'i//khre dang bil ba ji bzhin bar//de bzhin ud pal ku mu ta//rgyal po'i las la sogs sbyar te//gzhan gyi yul phyogs 'joms par byed//sngags pas rtag tu chu gtor na//khyad par du
113a4(rgyud): ni rgyas par 'gyur//gzhan gyi bdan zhes bya ba rab tu byed pa/le'u gnyis pa rdzogs s+ho////'khor ba las rgal mtshan nyid dag//nyon cig rgyal po rdo rje che//dngos po
113a5(rgyud): dngos nyid dang bral ba//dag dang mi dag spang bya ba//dngos po gnyis ka spong ba ni//gnyis su med pI chos shes bya//gzugs stong pa'i bar du yang //dbus su mnyam par gan
113a6(rgyud): ba'i rtsang dang mi rtsang dbus su ni//ma 'dres pa de mnyam por lta//rten dang rten can dkyil du yang //mnyam pa'i ye shes yang dag 'byung //lus ni mnyam par gnas pa dang //ngag ni mnyam par gnas pa yi//mnyam
113a7(rgyud): par lta ba mthong ba rnams//ye shes mnyam par 'byung ba yin//ji ltar mnyam par lta ba dang //gnyis su med pa'i chos dag ni//bdag gis ji ltar 'tshal bar bgyi//bcom ldan 'das la bdag nyan 'tshal
113a8(rgyud): gnyis med ye shes mtshan nyid dag//nyon cag rdo rje de kho na//ye shes can gyis gnyis med chos//dngos po gnyis med spang bar bya//sbyor ba kun gyi sngon du ni//dag pa rnam gsum brjod par bya//
113b1(rgyud): sra ba wa shud d+ho sarba d+ha rma sa wa shud d+ha hU~M/badzra shud+d+ha sarba da ra ma badzra shud+d+ha ha~M//yo ga shud+ho sarba d+ha rma yo ga shud+d+ha ha~M//'di gsum brjod nas thal sbyar te//bla na med pa nyid du bya//yid du 'ong ba'i sa phyogs su//de'i 
113b2(rgyud): gnas su 'dug bya ste//bla re bre ba ji bzhin bar//na bza' sna tshogs rab rgyan cing //ba dan rgyal mtshan sgred pa dang //sham dang 'dra bas klubs pa'o//kun nas phyogs bcu'i gnas dag du//de bzhin dri'i dkyil 'khor 
113b3(rgyud): bya//yud tsam gyis ni sbyor ba yis//bdag gi lus ni bsam bya ba//ye shes mkha' 'gror sgyur nas su//sngon du bdag nyid mchod nas su//lag pa gan rgyal gzhag nas ni//de nyid rdo rje rtags nas kyang //gnas lnga ru ni 
113b4(rgyud): gdab pa yis//sa 'og bdun ni sbyangs par 'gyur//hU~M zhes bya ba la sbyor bas//phyag 'tshal 'joms pa 'thar gzhag te//lhun po'i steng du kun bsam pa//phyogs dag gi ni kha dog bzhi//gser gyi gzhal 
113b5(rgyud): yas 'byung ba ni//rin chen bdun gyis sras pa la//dril bu g.yer ka gra 'chang gi//kun du dkar dang rgya che ba'i//phyag rgya kun gyi thog ma ru//dam tshig phyag rgya bcing bar bya//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis bcings 
113b6(rgyud): te//g.yon pa snying khar gzhag nas su//g.yas pa 'chan khung bcug pa ni//dam tshig phyag rgyar shes par bya//ji ltar sa bon rdo rje dngos//phyag 'tshal sreg pa mthar gzhag pa'i//'di dag phyag rgya la sogs sngags//
113b7(rgyud): de bzhin dam tshig bstan pa yin//seng ge'i ba dan ni lnga dag gis//gnas lnga ru ni bsam par bya//gdugs dkar po yis brgyan pa'i//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor bsam par bya//bdag gi dbugs las phyung nas su//dbus 
113b8(rgyud): su ye shes mkha' 'gro ma//zhal gsum phyag ni drug pa la//sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung gis bzhugs//dbu skra sil bur bkram pa dang //sangs rgyas lnga yis brgyan pa yi//drag po chen po 'thing ka'i mdog/sbrul 
114a1(rgyud): @#//gyis rgyan gyis brgyan pa'//zhal gsum po la spyan gsum pa//khro ba dang ni sgreng ba'i tshul//'che ba can la rtsigs gzhad pa//na bza' dmar pos shin tu mdzes//ji bzhin steng du kha tra~M kha//de bzhin
114a2(rgyud): gnyis pa dgra sta dang//gsum pa la ni rdo rje ste//g.yon pa dril bu sgra sgrogs shing//gnyis pa la ni thod pa dang//de bzhin gsum par ral gri 'dzin//'od zer 'bar ba mang po ru//rtogs pas dbus su sgom par bya//
114a3(rgyud): ye shes mkha' 'gro'i shar phyogs su//bdag gi chu las byung bya ba//kha dog dkar po rab mdzes pa//dbu skra gram pas rnam par brgyan//phyag gnyis sems dpa'i dkyil dkrung bcas//sbrul gyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa
114a4(rgyud): pa yi;kha tra~M thod pa phyag na bsnams//shin tu mdzes pa'i na bza' gsol//'od zer 'bar ba mad po las//rdo rje mkha' 'gro sgom par bya//ye shes byang gi gnas dag tu//bdag gi me las byung ba'i
114a5(rgyud): kha dog ser po rab mdzes pa//sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung gis bzhugs//dbu skra gram pas 'bar bas brgyan//sbrul gyi rgyan gyis brgyad pa yi//na bza' shin tu mdzes pa'i sku//phyag
114a6(rgyud): gnyis kyis ni rnam par brgyan//kha tra~M kha dang thod pa snams//mkha' 'gro drag mo zhes bya ba//ye shes nub kyi phyogs su ni//bdag gi rlung las byung ba yi//phyag gnyis sems dpa'idakyil dkrung bcas;
114a7(rgyud): btso ma gser gyi 'od 'bar ba//kha tra~M kha dang thod padang//dbu skra gram pas rnam par rgyan//sbrul gyis yan lag kun rgyan cing//shin tu mdzes pa'i na bza' snams//'od zer 'bar ba mang po can//
114a8(rgyud): mkha' 'gro ro langs ma zhes bya//ye shes lto'i phyogs su yang//rang gi sa las byung ba yi//phyag gnyis sems dpa'i dkyil dkrung bcas//rab tu mdzes pa'i 'thing ka'i 'dog//kha tra~M thod pa phyag sna~Ms shing
114b1(rgyud): sbrul gyis rgyan gyis brgyan pa yi//dbu skra gram pas rnam par rgyan//'jigs byed skad sgrogs gzhad byed pa//'od zer 'bar ba mang po can//mkha' 'gro gdol pa ma zhes bya//bdag las byung ba lnga po dag
114b2(rgyud): lnga car mkha' 'gro ma zhes bya//yang dag rdzogs sku 'od ldan pa//lnga'i lnga de sgom par bya//dbang ldan phyogs kyi cha ru ni//seng ge'i zhal ni sgom par bya//dkar ser dag ni kha tog can//glang chen 
114b3(rgyud): dbang po rdan du mnon//rdo rje lcags kyu sdig 'dzub dang//rab tu mdzes pa'i na bza' gsol//'od zer 'bar ba mngapo'i//lus ni 'bar ba sgom par bya//me phyogs stag mo zhes bya ba//rin chen bdun gyi
114b4(rgyud): bdan la bzhugs//phyag gnyis pa la dkar dang sngo//rab tu mdzes pa'i ni bza' gsol//dri med lcags kyu phyag na bsnams//g.yon pa sdig 'dzub zhags pa thogs//'od zer 'bar ba mngapa ru

121a7(rgyud): gnyis 'gyur thigs pa dang//rtsa ba 'dren pa sbyar bar bya//de'i yi ge sa bon dag//lha mo nyid ni rigs kyi yum//zla ba'i dbusu legs bsams pa//bdud rtsi sa bon bsam par bya//dngos po dngos

169a5(rgyud): khyad par du ni sreg par ba//'o ma can gyi shing lnga bas//yam shing sor bcu'i tshad dang bcas//gal te rgyas pa'i las 'dod na//bsgrub pos khyad par sreg par bya//til ni dmar dang nag pa dang/

169a8(rgyud): yam shing sor ni brgyad pa'i tshad//mngon spyod las la rab tu grags//yungs dkar mon sran gre'u dang sna'u//lam gyi rdul dang bsres pa dang//khrag dang tsha ba'i mar nag gis//der ni bsgo ba bab
169b1(rgyud): du bya;gse shing dang ni ba sa ka'i//yam shing der ni bzung nas ni//de nyid las ni sreg byas la//skrad pa dag la rab tu grags//nya dang sha la sogs pa'i rdzas//bag phye 'bru rnams thams
169b2(rgyud): cad dag;khrag dang sbrang dang rtsis sgos dang//bya rog gi ni bsgro dang bcas//bya rog tshang gi shing bu dang//so shing 'chos nas bor ba ste//'di dag bcings pa'i las rnams la//brtson pa ldan

169b5(rgyud): lo nga'i yam shing me la ni//brgya phrag lnga ru mngon sregs nas//shing du myur bar dpal ldan 'gyur//bya ba gang dang gang bsams pa//de de thams cad 'dir 'grub 'gyur//de nas yongs bzung

202b5(rgyud): gyi gnas yin te//gzugs sogs dbang po yon tan ldan//gzugs la sogs pa snang bas ni//sa'i tshad ma gnas yin ni//mdzes ma zhes byar rab tu grags//de ni lha mo gtum ste/

202b7(rgyud): sogs pa nye spyod kyi//rnal 'byor tshad ma mkhas pa yis//lus la ye shes sten par bya//sems la chu ni 'byung bas na//rnaM shes chu ru 'gyur ba ste//gal te chu ni gzhan yin na//ci phyir shi na chu mi 'byung //
202b8(rgyud): lus la nges par drod yodas pas//rnam shes me ru gyur pa ste//ye shes 'bral ba'i lus la ni//yan lag dro bar mi dmigaso//'byung 'jug dbugs ni ngu ba 'dra//rnaM shes rlung gi lus su 'gyur//ye shes
203a1(rgyud): @#//'bral ba'i lus la ni /sems kyi dbugs ni rgyu ba med//lus ni lci bar snang ba'i phyir//sems ni sa ru gyur pa ste//ye shes 'phos pa'i lus po ni//chu'i nang du byang bar 'gyur//lus 
203a2(rgyud): tshad ma lnga yod pas//lnga pa gcig gi bdag nyid 'gyur//mtshan nyid gcig tu shes byas nas//rnal 'byor pa'i 'gro ba rtag//de'i mtshan nyid shes bya'i phyir//dge ba'i gshes mnyen sten par bya//de nyid rnal 'byor grogs
203a3(rgyud): kyis ni//'khor ba'i pha rol rgal bar 'gyur//yul dang dbang po ye shes ni//gzir dang bcas pas sdom par 'gyur//sgong skyes khyim gyi gnas su ni//dbus su gnas pa bcing bar bya//'byung dang 'byung min sos kyis ni
203a4(rgyud): g.yo ba'i dngos po skor bar bya//bcing ba'i gnas dang //stan dag ni//rnam shes pad ma'i stan la'o//rnal 'byor pa smi de'i phyir//rnam shes rang bzhin mtshon par bya//rnal 'byor bsgrub par bya ba'i phyir
203a5(rgyud): rang bzhin zhes bya'i dngos por 'gyur//lte bar chu shing me tog ni//snying khar yang ni sgreng ste gnas//rtag tu stong pa'i sgo yin te//mkhas pas rtag tu zlog par bya//go sar dang ni ldan pa yi//rtag tu 'dab ma brgyad
203a6(rgyud): du 'gyur//de dbus rtag tu gsungs pa ni//ye shes rnam shes sbyor ba ste//sbyor ba yi ni shar phyogs char//de dang 'thun pa'i sar 'dug pa'o//mdud pa yi ni nub phyogs char//'og tu sor ni lnga pa'o//zhing de la
203a7(rgyud): ni sa bon dang //'od zer tshogs kyi myu gur 'gyur//sa bon de'i 'od zer gyis//chu shing lta bur skor bar bya//sangs rgyas lnga'i sa bon la//lto'i 'og nas kas par bya//pad mo mdzes pa bkas pa yis/
203a8(rgyud): 'bad ma brgyad kyis rnam par brgyan//pad mo de'i stan la ni//sgong skyes ye shes dag gnas la//dbus su shel ltar dag pa yi//ye shes rnam shes dbu ma nyid//mkha' 'gro ma bzhi'i sa bon rnams//phyogs kyi 
203b1(rgyud): pad mar gnas pa ste//dbyangs yig dang po sogs sa bon//mtshan nyid ji ltar bstan pa bkod//'tshams su sems ma la sogs pa'i//ma ning yi ge lnga bstan bzhin//snying khar dngos po dngos po yis
203b2(rgyud): snying khar yi ge lta bar bya//ba lang rmig pa'i steng cha ni//sgo dgu ru ni bdud rtsi yis//rnal 'byor can gyis rtag tu bkru//yi ge dkar po brgyad po rnams//bdud rtsi'i 'od zer dkar po yis//'od 'bar ba
203b3(rgyud): spu'i khung nas spro//rnal 'byor can gyis thams cad du//'gro dang 'chag dang 'dug pa dang//nyal ba la sogs dus kun du//ye shes rnam shes sa bon bzla//shin du mnyam gzhag sems kyis ni//
203b4(rgyud): rnal 'byor can gyis sgom zlas na//ye shes las 'byung rnal 'byor la//'khor ba'i pha rol rgal bar 'gyur//g.yo ba med cing 'tshe med par//dngos po dngos po sgom par bya//gong du pho ba'i sbyor mtshan nyid
203b5(rgyud): ji bzhin rdo rje mnyam par gyis//lam bzang po'i yis rnal 'byor pa//'gro ba mdzes pa'i blta bar bya//sgo dgu ba ni ji lta bu//ye shes sgo'i bye brag gis//ye shes dbang la nyan par
203b6(rgyud): 'tshal|'chi ba'i dus kyi tshad ma rnams//yang dag mnyam sbyor mnyam par gyis//lam bzung po'i mdzes pa'i gnas//lam ngan srid pa'i skyon gyis gos//thig le lte ba spyi rtsug dang//mig dang sna dang rna ba dag//
203b7(rgyud): chu dang chu min sgo yin te//sgo dgu yi ni mtshan nyid yin//lte ba 'dod can mtho ris te//thig les lus kyi gzugs can no//steng nas steng gi gnas yin te//mi gdung la sogs de ru 'gro//sna nas gnod sbyin gnas
203b8(rgyud): su ste|rna ba dag gis grub pa'i lha//gal te mig las ye shes 'gro//mi rnams kyi'i bdag por 'gyur//srid pa'i sgo'i yi dgas te//de bzhin gcig pas dud 'gro'o//chu min rnam shes myur bar du//dmyal ba brgyad du
204a1(rgyud): @#//shes par bya//sgo rnams kyi ni bye brag las//'khor ba'i bye brag 'byung ba yin//de bas sgo'i bye brag las//bsgrub pos lam ni bzang po ste//'chi ba'i mtshan ma ston pa dang//'chi ba'i dus la bab
204a2(rgyud): pa na/rims la sogs pa ma phog par//gong du 'pho ba sbyor ba'i mchog//sngon du bum pa rtsams pa yis//sgo rnams thams cad bcing bar bya//rgyal ba lnga'i mchod rten gyis//sgo ni dgag pa sgom bya ste//de la 'og gi sgo
204a3(rgyud): ru ni//chu'i sa bon dkar po bsam//chu dang chu min sgo la ni//same'i sa bon 'bar ba ste//de la shin du mnyam gzhag pas//dbugs ni bum pa can du sgom//lus ni rlung gi mdog du ste//sems ni rlung gi dkyil 
204a4(rgyud): 'khor la//rtsa ba'i rlung ni sa bon no//rlung gi mtha' ni rtsa ba can//thig le sgra dang ldan pa yi//rtsa ba'i sa bon dra bar bya//rdo rje sa bon drag mo yis//lcags kyu la sogs sbyar bar bya//nyi shu rtsa gcig dbugs rnaMs
204a5(rgyud): kyis//gnas nas drag du drang bar bya//gnas dang gnas nas steng du dbye//tshigs dgu la ni steng du bya//steng du smin pa'i sa bon dang//yi ge phye dang gnyis kyis sbyar//drag mo'i nga ro brjod bya ste//brgyad kyi
204a6(rgyud): mtha'i sa bon la//sde tshan dang po'i dang po ni//yi ge phyed du sbyar bar bya//'og tu rlung gi dkyil 'khor gyis//sgra yi dbye bas skul bas bya//rlung gi sa bon ldan pa yi//rlung gi dkyil 'khor bsams nas ni//
204a7(rgyud): nyi shu rtsa gcig gnas rnams nas//gong nas de bzhin steng gzhan du//rnal 'byor pa'i sa smin pa la//steng gi rtsa bar bza' bar bya//tshigs dgu las ni steng bzhin gyis//mod las 'pho bar shes bya ste//nyin
204a8(rgyud): re bram ze bsod pa dang//'tshams med lnga po byed pa dang//rku 'phrog 'dod la dpyod pa dang//lam 'di yis ni dag 'gyur te//sdig pa gos par mi 'gyur zhing //srid pa'i skyon las ring du 'gro//ji ltar 'dam pa
204b1(rgyud): nas skyes pa yis//pad ma dri med yid 'ong ba//de bzhin lus kyi 'dab las ni//ye shes sku ni 'dod bzhin 'gro//dus la bab na 'pho bya ste//dus min lha rnams bsod par 'gyur//de bas lus la mtshan 
204b2(rgyud): ma rnams|mthong nas mkhas pas sbyor ba rtsam//rnal 'byor bsgrub pa'i khyad par gyi//de nyid ji bzhin rdo rje nyon//sngar gyi mtshan nyid kun rig pas//mnyam nyid sems ni sgom par bya//snying kha'i dkyil
204b3(rgyud): 'khor dbus gnas par//sangs rgyas lnga'i sa bon bsam//de 'od nyi ma 'bar ba yis//gzugs rnams zad par bsams nas ni//sngon bzhin mtshan nyid ldan pa yi//sngar stan stong nyid bsam par bya//
204b4(rgyud): gzugs sogs thaMs cad bcom nas ni//hUM zhes bya ba gnas par bsam//sa bon gyis ni skyed par bya//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par/pad ma'i stan la bzhugs pa yi//ye shes
204b5(rgyud): mkha'agro'i bdag nyid ni//zhal gsuM phyag ni drug pa ste//spyan gsum gcod pan gyis brgyan cing//gzhad dang 'khro dang sgeg pa dang//rgyan rnams kun gyis klubs pa'o//kun da zla ba ltar
204b6(rgyud): dkar zhing|shin du mdzes pa'i na bza' gnams//sems dpa'i dkyil dkrung gnas nas ni//sangs rgyas sprin rnams spro bar mdzad//g.yas kyi dang po mda' yin te//gnyis pa yi ni lcags kyu
204b7(rgyud): snams|gsum pa na ni rdo rje nyid//g.yon pa khro bo sdig 'dzub ste//gnyis pa 'jigs pa'i zhags pa thogs//gong ma'i bzhu ni steng na snams//'od zer sna tshogs 'ab ra ba ru//dbus mi g.yo bar
204b8(rgyud): sgom par bya|yan lag brgyad du rab 'bad pas//sa bon brgyad kyi yige bkod//a li ka li rab sbyor bas//sa bon ji bzhin btu par bya//'od zer ldan pa'i sa bon rnaMs//kun du kha dog dkar por bya//
205a1(rgyud): chu shing mdog pad mo la//de'i snying dkar sgong skyes gzhag par bya//de yi dbus su rnam shes dang //ye shes lhan cig ldan par ni//mi g.yo 'tshe ba med par ru//dngos po dngos po sgom bya la//
205a2(rgyud): de nas phyi rol sbyor ba ni//mkhas pa yis ni bzung bar bya//gnas gnyis dag ni bsams nas su//rlung gi dkyil 'khor sgom par bya//de'i dbus su me yin te//me'i dbus su nyi ma la//a li ka lir
205a3(rgyud): ldan pa yi//de'i sa bon dmar por bkod//rnal 'byor pa yis khyad par du//'byung dang 'jug pas zlas par bya//'byung ba ye shes sa bon gyis//dbus su bsnun dang ldan par bya//'jug pas snying gi
205a4(rgyud): rnam shes la//me tog lta bur bsnun par bya//'byung ba 'byung min bzlas byas shing /'jug pa 'jug pa min la zla//rnal 'byor pa yis 'byung 'jug dag//bdag nyid dang ni phyi rol bya
205a5(rgyud): 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid mtshon pa dang //'jug pa'i mtshan nyid mtshon byas la//rnal 'byor pa'i 'byung 'jug dag de kho na nyid mnyam par sgom//mnyam gzhag dngos po sgoms nas ni//
205a6(rgyud): 'grub 'gyur 'di la the tsom med//de nas phyi rol lus la ni//bsgrub pa po'i rnam par sprul//pha rol sems dang rjes 'thun par//mkhas pa yis ni shes par bya//sgom dang bzlas pa'i khyad par ni//shin
205a7(rgyud): du mnyam par gzhag te bsam//gang gi lam nas bsams nas ni//'grub 'gyur 'di ni the tsom med//gsang ba'i bdan gyi rim par phye ba las//rim par phye ba gsum pa'o////gsang ba'i gdan gyi gsang ba nyid//
205a8(rgyud): sems dang ye shes 'dres pa yis//rdzogs pa'i byang chub rim ci 'dra//ye shes dbang las nyan par 'tshal//gsang ba'i gsang ba chos kyi don//byang chub rim pa rdo rje can//'dod chags la sogs ring spangs nas//mnyam
205b1(rgyud): nyid sems ni bskyed par bya//yid du 'ong ba'i sa phyogs su//bde ba'i stan la gnas nas ni//khams dang sems ni bde ba yi//snying rje phan par bsam bya ste//yang dag stan chos de nyid thos//slob dpon lung gi rjes
205b2(rgyud): 'gro zhing /gsang chen de nyid don sgom pa//gcig nas gcig rgyud de nyid can//thams cad sngon bzhin mtshon byas nas//thams cad stong pa nyid du bya//dri ma bkrus pa'i lus la ni//byang chub rim par brtso bar bya//
205b3(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//ye shes mkha' 'gro bsam bya ste//zla ba'i 'od zer 'phro ba yi//kun d+ha zla ba ltar dkar mdog//zhabs gnyis sems dpa'i dkyil dkrung bcas//pad ma yi ni stan la bzhugs
205b4(rgyud): rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan pa'i sku//sangs rgyas lnga yis brgyan pa ste//phyag nyis phyag rgya che bcings nas//nyid kyi thugs kar btsir bar mdzad//thugs mdzes shin tu rdzogs pa ste//ye shes sems
205b5(rgyud): dpa' bdag ces brjod//chu shing me tog lte ba nas snying gi bar du sgreng ste gnas//sngar gyi mtshan nyid kyis bzlog nas//sngon bzhin pad ma rgyas par bya//ge sar dang ni lte bar byas//'dab
205b6(rgyud): rgyas shin tu mdzes pa la//dbus kyi sgong skyes la gnas par//yi ge dkod pas dgod par bya//kha dog dkar po dag cing gsal//dbus su sgom bya zhu ba'i gtso//de'i dbus su rnam shes dang//ye shes dag ni
205b7(rgyud): sbyar bar bya/shar du byams par ldan pa yi//mi skyod pa'i sa bon bsam//lho'i sgo ru snying rje ste//rin chen sa bon de nyid do//nub kyi sgo'i 'dab ma la//'od dpag med pa'i sa bon bkod//byang gi cha'i 'dab
205b8(rgyud): ma la/don yod rtso bo bsam par bya//dbyangs yig thog ma'i sa bon ni//thig le dbyig par ldan rims bzhin//'od zer dkar pos brgyan pa yi//yi ge bzhi ni bkod par bya//phyogs 'tshams dag ni bzhi
206a1(rgyud): @#//po ru ;sngon du bstan pa'i ma ning bkod//de'i dbus su de'i sems//yi ge ru ni mtshon par bya//stong pa mtshan nyid med pa las//ji ltar yi ge sbyar bar bya kyi//sems ni ya mtshan skyes par 'gyur
206a2(rgyud): bcom ldan bdag la bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa//stong pa'i mtshan nyid mtshon pa las//chu shing nyi ma'i bdud rtsi bzhin//snying po med pa'i snying por gyur//de nyid ji bzhin rdo
206a3(rgyud): rjes nyon ;dbus na gnas pa'i ye shes te//ye shes rang bzhin gzugs med cing //mtshan nyid med pa'i mtshan nyid can//dri ma med pa'i ye shes sku//dngos dang dngos med rnam par grol//yi ge'i 
206a4(rgyud): gzugs ni yi ge med//gzugs dang stong pa'i dbus su ni//rnal 'byor can gyis de nyid bzung//gang gi mtshan nyid shes byas nas//mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes dag//de yis rdzogs pa'i byang 
206a5(rgyud): chub lam;myur ba nyid du mthong bar 'gyur//bla ma nyid kyi lam gyis ni//ye shes rnam shes rang bzhin nyid//bstan chos las ni mtshan nyid bstan//de kho na nyid dpa' bos bshad//
206a6(rgyud): bod zer dkar pos brgyan pa ru//'di ni kun du sgom par bya//rgyal ba lnga'i mchod rten te//bdud rtsi'i sa bon bsams nas ni//'od zer dkar po gsal ba'i mdangs//bdud rtsi'i rgyun ni 'dzag par bkru//
206a7(rgyud): thig pa'i dbus su sa bon ni//mthe bo tsam gyi bzlum por bya//de'i dbus kyi sa bon ni//a li ka li ngag las btu//kha dog dkar po shin tu mdzes//zla ba'i 'od zer kun du 'phro//de'i dbus su sa
206a8(rgyud): bon na;thig le bsam par rab tu grags//skra'i rtse mo rgya'i cha//rdul phran gzugs su kun du bsam//rnam par shes pa'i dngos po yi//mtshan nyid mtshon pa'i ye shes can//sems kyi mtshan nyid mi shes pas//
206b1(rgyud): thig le gnas kyi go 'phang lta//snying dang tshangs pa'i sgo dang ni//bstan chos dag ni brnyed sla ste//de'i sbyor ba rnyed par dka'//bla ma'i snyen bkur la gus pa'i//thabs dang ldan pa'i bsgrub pa pos//
206b2(rgyud): bla ma'i lung gi lam gyis ni//mkhas pa yis ni lta bar bya//mi g.yo mtsho ba med par ru//mnyam par gzhag pas dngos po sgom//chos kyi sngon 'gro yid yin te//yid kyis 'jug cing yid kyis bdag/
206b3(rgyud): de bas thams cad yid rang bzhin//blo ldan yid las byung zhes bya//gal te zas 'dod rnam 'byor pa//snying gi sbom pa bsam par bya//sa bon gyi ni rgyu gyur pa//de kho na rang gi gzugs kyi min//rgyu dang 'bras
206b4(rgyud): bu'i sa bon ni//de kho na nyid mthong ba min//gzugs sogs srid pa'i longs spyod la//sa bon dmigs shing zlas par bya//kun nas kun du 'jug pa dang //'og dang 'og min yang dag gnas//rtsa ba
206b5(rgyud): rtag tu bzer bar bya//ye shes rnam shes sa bon ni//thig le sgra dang yang dag ldan//srid pa'i longaspyod 'dod pa yis pha rol dag ni spangs nas su//yid kyi bzlas dang ldan par bya//rtag par chos
206b6(rgyud): kyi dbyings nyid kyis//mi g.yo go 'phang rgyun du gnas//rtag tu 'phra zhing gos med pas//ye shes la ni rnam shes thim//rnam shes ye shes nyid du 'gyur na//byang chub rim par 'thob par 'gyur//ma me 
206b7(rgyud): lta bur thams cad du//snang 'gyur mtshan ma dang po ste//srin bu me khyer lta bu'i rdul//mthong ba mtshan ma gnyis pa yin//gsum pa las ni rnal 'byor pas//nyon mongs 'joms pa'i mtshan ma ste//
206b8(rgyud): rdul phran dkar po'i tshul gyis ni//de bzhin phyogs bcur mthong bar 'gyur//'dod pa lnga'i longaspyod rnams//sems kyis mthong ba bzhi pa ste//mtho ris gzugs kyi lha rnaMs ni//seMs kyis mthong ba lnga pa yi
207a1(rgyud): @#//gzugs med pa'i longaspyod ni//'dud pa chos kyi dbyings yin no//sangs rgyas 'bras bu brgyad pa ste//rnal 'byor pa ni rang dgar spyod//mchod rten gzugs kyi khyad par las//dbyibs kyi 
207a2(rgyud): gzugs ni ji lta bu//lha'i rnam dag sgom pa rnams//bcom ldan 'das la nyan par 'tshal//lus can rnams la yang dag lta//yang dag de nyid shes nas su//de nyid sbyor ba rtsam par bya//rnal 'byor rdo rjes nyan par
207a3(rgyud): gyis;snying khar zla ba bsams nas su//dbus su hU~M ni sgom par bya//me ltar 'bar ba'i 'od zer gsal//spyi rtsug dkar po yang dag ldan//me lce 'bar ba bcom nas ni//thams cad kun nas zad par bya//sngon gyi
207a4(rgyud): mtshan nyid de kho nas/de nyid stong pa nyid du bya//de nas zla ba'i sa bon ni//mkhas pa yis ni bsams nas su//mchod rten pho brang gnas nyid ni//de las sems ni bskyed par byed//gya gyus zhes brjod 
207a5(rgyud): pa ste;phyogs brgyad dag ni khams yin no//dril bu nam mkha'i dbus gnas pa//lhun po lha'i gnas nyid do//'dod na spyod pa'i lus can rnams//de ru nges par rtag par bya//'og dang steng dang 
207a6(rgyud): dbus dag gi//dbus su gnas pa bdag med chos//gzugs khams zhes bya'i mtho ris rnams//'khor lo'i phreng bas rnam par brgyan//gzugs bcu dang brgyad po ni//go rims ji bzhin rnam par gnas

207a8(rgyud): sems dang ye shes 'dres pas na//gnyis med mnyam pa'i khyad par gyis//gzugs dang stong pa'i mtha' dang ni//de bzhin dngos dang dngos med nyid//bzung dang 'dzin pa la sogs gzhan//legs byas
207b1(rgyud): de ni log pa rnams//mtshan bzhi'i mtshan nyid ston pa po//de'i dbus su sgom par bya//sbyin dang tshul khrims bzod lasogs//rdzas 'di yis ni mchod nas su//mi g.yo 'tshe ba med par ru//dngos pos
207b2(rgyud): dngos po sgom par bya//mnyam gzhag dngos po sgoms nas ni//'grub 'gyur 'dir ni the tsom med//chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin gyi//de nyid bstan chos lung lasogs//rnal 'byor dmigs pa goms
207b3(rgyud): byas pa'i|slab pa chen po'i lus can rnams//gdan bzhi pa'i de kho na//bla ma'i man ngag ldan pa yis//rims kyis rang dgar longs spyod de//bdag med lta zhing myang bar bya//bdud rtsi mang ba'i rnal 'byor
207b4(rgyud): pas|ye shes sku ni myur du thob//ye shes sku'i rnal 'byor phas//bdag dang gzhan don mchog tu 'gyur//shin du rnaM par dag pa yi//chos kyi dbyings kyi rnal 'byor ro//gsang chen sbyor ba'i mtshan
207b5(rgyud): nyid kyis|de nyid mchog de nyid//gang gis byang chub lam gyur pa//bcom ldan 'das la nyan par 'tshal//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa//dang po re zhig rnal 'byor pas//bdag nyid
207b6(rgyud): rang dgar byed pa yi//rta rgod lta bu'i sems de nyid//mkhas pa yis ni sdams par bya//ji ltar bung ba metog snyung ba'o//chang gis myos shing sems ni rtan 'gyur te//long spyod zhugs shing sdug bsngal
207b7(rgyud): rmongs dang bral//de bzhin sgom pa'i las kyis yid bcing bya//yid sdams pas na 'grub 'gyur bas//dang por yid ni sdam par bya//yid kyis bsgrub pa'i bye brag gis//byang chub ye shes mtshon par bya
207b8(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par/ye shes mkha'agro sbrul byas te//phyag rgya chen po snying khar bcing //sngon gyi mtshan nyid kun rig pas//yi ge brgyad kyi sa bon rnaMs/yi ge'i gnas su bkod par bya//rgyas pa
208a1(rgyud): @#//lnga'i mchod rten gyi;sgo ni dgag pas bsam bya ste//bdud rtsi sngags kyi yi ge yis//rgyas pa'i sgo ni bsrung bar bya//mi'i yi ger 'bar ba yis//chu dang chu min de bzhin bsrung //chu shing me tog pad ma ni
208a2(rgyud): mdzes shing 'khyil pas rgyas par dbye//g.yo med skyod pa'i blo dag gis//'khyil pa ring du dbye bar bya//de'i dbus kyi sgong rar ni//dung dang zla ba ltar dkar ba//phyogs phyogs gnas su sa bon te//phyogs bzhir
208a3(rgyud): khams ni bzhi po 'o//phyogs 'tshams sa bon rtsegs pa bzhi//shin du mnyam par gzhag pas sgom//'od zer kun nas dkar zhing dag//lha mo'i yi ge sgom par bya//ye shes rnam shes ngo bo yi//de nyid dbusu gzhag
208a4(rgyud): nas ni;sems ni rang dgar sgom pa yis//phyi nas gzugas ni gzhom par bya//phyogs dang phyogs kyi 'tshams dag tu//phyogs phyogs gnas su gzhag nas kyang //rang bzhin thams cad bcom pa yi//
208a5(rgyud): lhag ma rnam shes ye shes nyid//shar gyi phyogs su chu yin te//kha dog dkar po kun du 'od//me ni ser po 'od 'bar bar//rtag tu byang du bsam par bya//rlung gi sa bon dmar po ni//nub kyi phyogs su
208a6(rgyud): rtag par bya;sa'i sa bon ljang gu ni//lho'i phyogasu bsam par bya//phyogs 'tshams 'dres pa'i kha dog gis//dgra bzhi ru ni sbyar bar bya//lhag ma dbus kyi ye shes te//sgong skyes bcas pa gzhol bar bya//
208a7(rgyud): de'i mtshon nyid mtshon bya ba//mtshon bya mtshan nyid dngos po med//sems kyi mtshan nyid dngos po stong pa bas//rang bzhin gnas ni zad par bya//rnam rtog stong pa'i dbus dag du//rdul phran tsa na lta bu'i gzugs//
208a8(rgyud): a li ki li'i sa bon ni//rdul phran zhes bya dbusu ste//rnam pa med cing brjod du med//thams cad rdul zhes bya ba la//stong nyid du yang mi dmigs te//gzugs lasogs par mi lta'o//rdul ni sa bon ma yin la//
208b1(rgyud): rang bzhin med cing mtshan nyid med//rlung dang namakha' ma yin te//mnyam nyid dbu mar mtshon par bya//gzugs dang stong pa de bzhin dbus//de bzhin rnal 'byor can gyis bzung //bla ma'i lung gis rtogs byas te//
208b2(rgyud): lung gi dam pa dag dang ni//bstan chos mtshan nyid dpyad nas su//lung dang bcas pas 'grub par 'gyur//mi g.yo 'tshe ba med par ru//dngos po dngos po sgom par bya//mnyam gzhag dngos po dpyad nas su
208b3(rgyud): byang chub rim pa 'grub par 'gyur//ye shes thim pa'i rnal 'byor pas//sems can don med mtshog tu rtson//kun rtog lus kyi sems dang ni//gang tshe yul 'khor bsrung 'dod na//ye shes dbang po'i longaspyod can
208b4(rgyud): rang 'dod lus kyi dngos grub kyis//rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras 'dod cing //khams gsuM gyis ni lta ba dang //rang 'dod yid kyi gnas dang ni//namakha' rgyu ba'i lus can dang //sems can kun gyi gzhan don gnyer//
208b5(rgyud): mchog ni myur du ster bar 'gyur//lus dang dbang po don gnyer bar//bdag med pa'i rang dgar bya//chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin gyis//sbyor bas 'khor ba sgrol bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas 'dra ba rnaMs
208b6(rgyud): kyis yang /sbyor ba gzhan gyi don med 'gyur//bsgrub pos 'du 'dzi med pa dang //sgra sogs skye bos dben pa yi//tshal dang ri sul brag phug tu//gnas nas de ru sgom par bya//na ga mu la pa la sha//ru rta cha ni gsum po dag
208b7(rgyud): lag da ni srang gcig ste//cham nyam phye mar byas nas ni//ba'i 'o mar yang dag bcas//phye ma bzhi cha bsres nas su//'di ni nyin re rnal 'byor pa//mkhas pa yis ni bza' bar bya//rnal 'byor can gyis lo brgya ru//
208b8(rgyud): brag phug stong stogs gnas nas ni//nges par shin du smin pa yin//grang dang bkres skom la sogs grol//sman 'di dang ni ldan pa ru//brag phug stong sogs gnas na ni//'dul ba bsrung ba'i rnal 'byor pa//lus ni rtag tu
209a1(rgyud): @#//bsrung bar 'gyur//ji ltar bud med byis brtam rjes yid rang//gnyis sprod rtam du smra zhing ston pa ste//de ltar lam bshad 'ching ba yod ma yin//rnal 'byor lam ngan de ltar min//rig med chu dang
209a2(rgyud): me tog dur ba rnams//bram ze spyi bor gzhag pas sangs rgyas pas bzung min//dbang skur sngags sogs nyams kyis 'jigs pas na//gnas ngan lha sogs rigs la phyag mi bya//de nas rnal
209a3(rgyud): 'byor pha dang rnal 'byor ma//mkha'agro dang mkha'agro ma dang//byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun bye ba phrag brgya bcu//du ma'i 'khor rnams dga' zhing sems tshim nas//thams cad kyis ye shes thob par
209a4(rgyud): 'gyur te/phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang//sems can thams cad kyis thal mo sbyar te glu blangs pa//rang dgar sems can sgrol ba yi//rgyu med dngos la 'dud par bgyi//
209a5(rgyud): te na hUM te te hUM/ston ka'i zla ba ltar zhal mdzes shrI dung ltar dkar ba'i sku 'dzin la//te na hUM te na te te hUM/rdo rje dril bu'i phyag rgya can//gnyis 'khyud sbyor ba 'dzin pa'o//te na hUM te na ta te hUM
209a6(rgyud): sgyu ma'i lus su thim pa yi//'gro ba mdzes pa'i rgyu med dngos//te na hUM te na ti te hUM/srid pa gsum 'bar sku ni rjes chags 'dul bar ldan//'od zer sngon po nyi ma stong dang ldan//rnam mang sku dang cha byad
209a7(rgyud): rjes chags sems dpar ldan//rdo rje sems dpa' dbang phyug mchog//rol pas gar mdzad pa zhes bya ba yi//bkra shis sgra yis bkang nas su//thams cad kun nas glu blangs so//ji bzhin lam ni rdo rjes
209a8(rgyud): nyon/bstan chos shin tu mnyam gzhag pas//gang zhig su yang rung ba las//yi ge 'am tshig ni nyan byed na//mtho ris longs spyod thob 'gyur na//yang na 'khor lo sgyur bar ro//'dri 'am 'drir 'jug klog pa dang
209b1(rgyud): shin du dad ldan sngags bzlos pa'i//rnal 'byor pa rnams 'pho ba'i tshe//srid pa'i gzugs rnams bsam bzhin 'grub//rnal 'byor bsam rtan byed pa rnams//myur du byang chub rim par thob//ces 
209b2(rgyud): bdag nyid lha mo la bka' bstsal pas//phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rnal 'byor pa dang/rnal 'byor ma 'dus pa de dag dang/phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'
209b3(rgyud): chen po thams cad dang/lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang/dri za dang lha ma yin la sogs pa thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor gyis/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do//
209b4(rgyud): dpal bdan bzhi zhes bya ba//rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pas 'bum phrag bco brgyad las snying po lta bur gsungs pa rdzogs s+h+yo////
209b5(rgyud): bdan bzhi po'i rgyud kyi ni/gsum pa yi ni dang po'o de'i sbyor ba rnyed nas ni//sngags ni de nyid khyad par nyid//snying po yi ni snying po chos//rnal 'byor gsang zhing gsang ba yi//de nyid de nyid
209b6(rgyud): de las mchog snying po de'i de nyid gsungs pa'o//bla ma'i ming ni gnyis bzung la//rtsa bar skor zhing mnan par bya//spyi bor 'od dang zla ba'i 'od//sa bon skyed pa'i rim pa bzhi//zla ba dkar dang kun d+ha'i 
209b7(rgyud): mchog rtso bo sngon du sbyar bar bya//sngags kyi bzlas pa brjod pa rdzogs bya zhing//dbyangs yig mngon par mtho ba ni//las rnams gya sum gyi sngon du ni//bla ma'i yi ge sngon du'o//dkar dang ser dang dmar po'i
209b8(rgyud): bzhi pa dag pa ru ni spyod//myu gu'i gnas ni rtso bo nyid//srid pa mchog tu sgrol ba'o//dbyangs yig dbyangs kyi sgom pa ni//rnal 'byor pa yis mtshon par bya//dngos grub 'dod pas zlas pa yis//ba du ka'i me
210a1(rgyud): @#//me tog 'dra;rdo rje'i dbyug pa la rtag tu//de nyid du ni snang ba thim//dkar po zla bas mnan pa'o//gnyi gas sgoms pas bkru bar bya//pad ma rdo rje zla ba dang//bdud rtsi yis bkru bar 
210a2(rgyud): ldan;dang po'i sa bon rtso bo yis//dbyangs yig kun la khyab pa'o//dkar zhing dag la mnyam pa'i 'od//gsum pa las kyi sa bon ni//bzhi par nam yang ma yin no//ji ltar dang po'i mtha' gnas
210a3(rgyud): pa'i;khyad par dbyangs yig sa bon nyid//mtha' ma sngon du sa bon nyid//de bzhin sngon du gnyis 'gyur ro//bzhi po mnyam pa'i chu ru ni//mnyam pa'i gnas su sbyar bar bya//bzhi pa gsum pa'i 
210a4(rgyud): sa bon ni;mgo smos mgo bo'i lus nyid do//yi gegsum gyi lus nyid ni//rang bzhin gyi ni dbyangs yig dngos lus dang lus sogs yi ge ni//lcags kyu yis ni mnan par bya//dmar
210a5(rgyud): dang nag sogs sa bon ni/rnal 'byor pa'i khyad par du lnga pa drug pa'i sa bon ni//thams cad sgra'i yi ge'o//dkar dang ser la lci bar bshad//dmar dang sngon po de bzhin yang//zhi rgyas dbang la sog+sa
210a6(rgyud): pa ni;kun kyang sgra'i dbye ba'o//thams cad la yang sgra yis ni//kha dog bzhin du rab tu grags//bdun pa brgyad pa'i sa bon gyis//skyes bus rang 'dod pa'i las//nges par lus ni sdom pa dang//
210a7(rgyud): gnyis pa'i sa bon gyis mnan pa yi//yangs pas dmar dang nag la sbyar//rnal 'byor pa 'is khyad par du//'tshams ni bcad cing mnan pa'i las//gnyis pa lcags kyu'i yi ge ni//rang bzhin yi ge ming
210a8(rgyud): gis ni;de nyid las rnams dgug pa dang//sa bon 'di yi byed pa'o//kha dog dmar po mchog gi 'od//thams cad 'dren pa'i sa bon ni//dbyangs yig skyed pa'i sa bon ni//las rnams thams cad byed pa'o//
210b1(rgyud): ma ning gis bskyed sa bon ni//btan zhing rtag pa'i sa bon ni//bud med dam ni skyes pa 'am//'di nyid kun nas rab tu sbyar//che sde tshan bzhi pa'i bzhi pa ni//tha bzhi dag ni rnam par spangs//dkar
210b2(rgyud): dang kun d+ha zla ba'i mdog//nyi ma'i ye shes rnam par shes//tha ma bzhi dang lnga pa dang //sde tshan drug par yang dag ldan//kun nas mtshan mo'i stong par ni//khyad par du ni shes par bya//bdud rtsi yi 
210b3(rgyud): ni sa bon ni//dkar zhing shel ltar mnyam pa'i 'od//de nyid kun dang de nyid dang //kun nas bdud rtsi grub pa'o//lha min grub pa 'gugs pa dang //lha dang mi dang gnod sbyin dang //thos cad las rnaMs
210b4(rgyud): grub pa ni//sa bon 'di ni khyad par nyid//sngon po ma ni gnyis bkris la//lcags sgrogs bzhin du kha sprad la;rtsa bar spangs pa gnyis 'gyur ro//spyi bor bdud rtsi rnam pa'o//steng du 
210b5(rgyud): thig le 'bar ba'i mchog//sangs rgyas lnga'i rang bzhin skus//sa bon lnga'i ngo bo nyid//rnam bzhir phye zhing phye ba yi//lce rtsa lcer gsungs pa ni//kha dog dkar po mnyam pa'i 'od//
210b6(rgyud): shing dang chu'i sbyin sreg la//hUM gi rnam pa rkan la sbyar//tsab sgra dang 'od bral ba//bar yi ni dbyangasu gsungs//gal te 'dod na lha'i lus//khyad par du ni zhi ba'i las//snying gi rtsa bar gsungs pa ni//kha dogaser
210b7(rgyud): po 'od mnyam pa//sbom po'i rtsa dang thig le dang /rgyas pa dang ni me'i las//lte bar sgra dang 'joms pa ni//kha dog dmar po 'od mnyam pa//sbom pa'i sgra dang ldan pa'i 'od//dbang gi las la rab tu sbyar//
210b8(rgyud): sna rtser sbrid pa gsungs pa ni//kha dog sngon po mnyam pa'i 'od//yangs pa'i sgra'i 'od du ni//ldan par blo dang ldan par skul//bskrad dang bsad dang bcing ba dang //mnan dang dug sogs bskrad pa dang //gtor 
211a1(rgyud): @#//ma la sogs nag pa'i las//hU~M gi rnam pa sbrid par bya//de nyid de nyid bzhi pa ni//hU~M gi rnam pa nye bar mtshon//sa bon thams cad 'byin mdzad pa//hU~M gi rnam pa byang chub mchog//thams cad 'byin pa'i 
211a2(rgyud): ngo bo bas|hU~M gi yi ge mchog gi ha//stong nyid yi ge hU~M 'gyur bas//hU~M gi rnam pa phyag 'tshal bstod//stong pa'i gzugs dang rab ldan pa//dngos dang dngos med 'byin mdzad pa//btso ma gser dang 'dra 
211a3(rgyud): ba'i mdog|sgra dang thig ler yang dag ldan//ri rab rgya mtsho ri dang ni//btan pa dang ni g.yo bar ldan//lha dang lha min grub pa dang //mi dang gnod sbyin rnal 'byor pa//lha rnams kun nas g.yo ba ni//gsungs  
211a4(rgyud): pa'i sa bon gyis 'gugs par byed//sde tshan dang po'i sa bon ni//tsa bar re phas mnan pa dang //lcags kyu mgor ni rnam par dgod//steng du zla ba'i 'od dang bcas//kha dog dmar po mnyam pa'i 'od//
211a5(rgyud): lcags kyu yis ni snying po bsgrub//lcags kyu yi ge sa bon bzhin//lcags kyu yis ni kun 'gugs pa'o//stong par rlung gi sa bon ni//sde tshan brgyad kyi dang po la//rtsa bar sgra yis bkri bar bya//spyi bor zla tshes 
211a6(rgyud): thig le dang |rlung gi kha dog drag po yis//lus can kun ni bskal par bya//skul ba'i las kyi sa bon gyis//sa bon de nyid kun du sbyar//lnga pa'i sde tshan gnyis pa yi//gsum pa'i yi ge gzung byas la//yi ge 
211a7(rgyud): gsum pas mnan par bya//re bas rtsa bar sbyar ba dang //steng du bdud rtsi'i thigs pa'o//kha dog dkar po mnyam pa'i 'od//dges pa la sogs sa bon gyis//lha tshogs kun nas mnyes par byed
211a8(rgyud): sde tshan dang po'i gsum pa ni//gsang ba'i sgra ni rtsa ba ru//steng du bdud rtsi'i thig le dang //dkar zhing yid 'ong shin du 'bar//mchod pa'i sngags kyis dus dang ni//rtor ma sbyin sreg phyag rgya sogs
211b1(rgyud): rgyas pa'i las ni thams cad la//rgyas pa'i sa bon du ni grags//dang po'i sde tshan bzhi pa la//rtsa bar re bar sgra dang ni//spyi bo'i steng du 'bar ba yi//kha dog dmar po mnyam pa'i 'od//bzhi pa la ni
211b2(rgyud): rnam spangs pa//lus rnams kun gyi sngon du yang//rmongs shing lus kun rmongs byed pas//rmongs pa'i sa bon du ni grags //sde tshan lnga pa'i gsum pa dang//gnyis pa'i sa bon blang byas pas//
211b3(rgyud): mtha' ru stong pa gnyis so//kha dog dkar po mnyam pa'i 'od//lha dang lha min gsang ba sogs//rang gi 'dod pa'i gnas su gzhag//gzhag pa'i las la rnal 'byor pas//blo ldan yi ge 'di yis
211b4(rgyud): gzhag|stong pa'i sde tshan la spyad pa//de'i bzhi pa bzung bar bya//rtsa bar ma ni sbom po dang//steng du bdud rtsi'i thig le'o//'jigs pa bsrung ba'i las nyid la//kha dog dkar po mnyamapa'i 'od//
211b5(rgyud): rnal 'byor de nyid 'dod pa yis//sa bon de nyid kyis bsrung ngo//sreg pa'i yi ge'i sngags nyid ni//dbye ba rnam pa gsum du bshad//dkar dang ser dang dmar sogs kyis//las gsum gyi ni mtha' ru'o//
211b6(rgyud): bdun pa yi ni mtha' bzung la//brgyad pa'i gnyis pa yi ni bdan//bdud rtsi yis ni bdan du chu//kha dog dkar po mnyams pa'i 'od//ma ni sde tshan gyis skyed pa//me ltar 'bar ba mnyam pa'i 'od//logs su dbyangs
211b7(rgyud): yig dbyung ba'o//zhi pa la ni sreg pa'i mthar//bdud rtsi'i sa bon kha dog ser//chu'i sa bon dkar po'i bdan//logs su dbyangs yig dbyung ba'o//brgyad kyi mtha' mar kun nas 'bar//rdo rje 'od bzhin 'bar ba'i
211b8(rgyud): me|sa bon 'od bzhin 'bar bcas pa//dbyangs kyi dbyug pa'i sreg pa'o//rgyas pa'i sreg pa'i mtha' mar ru//de'i bdud rtsi dmar po ni//brgyad mtha'i logs su dbyug pa'o//kun nas dmar dang dmar ba yi
212a1(rgyud): @#//mthar ni dbyug pa lcags kyu'o//dbu mar drangs pa'i sems kyis ni//dbyug pa'i sbubs kyis zla bar bya//chags pa'i las la rnal 'byor pa//sreg pa'i yi ge rnam pa gsum//rnam bzhi
212a2(rgyud): las ni don gnyer bas//'joms pa'i yi ge gnyisu shes//gsang dang gsang ba dbye ba dang//mnan dang nye bar zlog pa las//stong pa'i sde tshan gnyis pa ni//dbyug pa blo ru rnam par sbyang//nyin mo'i
212a3(rgyud): sde tshan gsum pa las//dang po gnyis pa'i blo ru'o//kha dog nag po 'jigs chen po//'od zer me bzhin kun nas ldan//rdo rje sa bon mnyam 'bar las//lus kyi dbus su dbyug par bya//bsad
212a4(rgyud): dang bdug par srad par bya//drag po las ni thams cad la dngos grub 'dod pa'i rnal 'byor pas//'joms pa'i yige mtha' mar ru//de'i sa bon ldan pas ni//sngon gyi dbyug sogs rnam
212a5(rgyud): par spangs;dbang po dbu ma sde rnams su//kha dog sngon po rab rno bas//gsad dang dbye ba bdug pa ni//khro bo'i lha yis gsad par bya//'joms pa'i yi ge mtha' mar ni//sa bon 'di yis
212a6(rgyud): sbyar bar bya;dkar po'i sde dang sde tshan gyis//dang po'i dang po blang byas la//kun nas rdo rje'i sa bon gyis//sa bon yi ge phyed dang gnyis//'pho ba sbyor ba'i sngon dus su//rnam shes gong du skul ba'o
212a7(rgyud): lte bar rtsa'i phyogs nas ni//yi ge phyed dang gnyis kyis drang//brgyad pa'i mtha' ni sngon du'o//ba li ta'i sbyong dus su//'pho ba sbyor ba sngon du'o//'pho ba'i dus kyi sngon du ni//rnal 'byor pa yis rab tu dpyad//ud
212a8(rgyud): ba la bu li ta sa bon//sbyang ba'i yi ge phyed dang gnyis//dngos dang dngos med sa bon la//sgra dang thig le rnam par sbyar//rlung gi rtsa ba'i gnas su ni//sgra dang sgra yis bstan pa'o//chu shing me tog lte bar gnas//
212b1(rgyud): sa bon 'di yis sgyur bar bya//sngon du sbyor ba'i bsam gtan dang//mnyam par gzhag pa'i sems kyis bcing//bdun pa'i mtha' yis sa bon ni//bdud rtsi'i sa bon sa bon gyis//zlog byas rgyas pa'i gan+sa
212b2(rgyud): su ni;chu shing me tog lta bur gyis//ji ltar sde tshan gsum pa yi//yi ge gsum la bzung byas la//dbyug pa phyi ma'i sa bon dang//'od dpag gis ni mngon bkris pa//de nyid de nyid kun dang ni//sa bon sa bon
212b3(rgyud): mtshon gyi rtso//theg pa rnam gsum dag byed pa//a li sa bon rab tu grags//sde tshan brgyad pa gnyis pa la//lcags kyu yis ni spyi bor mnan//spyi bor zla tshes thig le dang//kha dog dmar po mchog gi 'od//
212b4(rgyud): sems can kun la rmongs byed pa//yid dbye ba la kun nas sbyar//kun du rmongs ming sa bon ni//rmongs pa'i las la rab tu sbyar//brgyad pa'i mtha'i sa bon ni//spyi bor lcags kyu 'dren pa dang//rtsa bar re ba'i
212b5(rgyud): sa bon ni;kha dog sngon po 'bar ba'i//dri dang me tog ro sogs ming//las kyis kun nas 'phrogs par byed//'phrogs byed ming gi sa bon gyis//'phrog pa'i las la kun du sbyar//
212b6(rgyud): 'dun pa dkar po'i sa bon ni//dung dang kun d+ha'i mnyam pa'i 'od//bdud rtsi chu'i sa bon ni//chu'i yi ge ming can no//ka shar ldan pa'i sa bon ni//kha dog ser po'i 'od mnyam pa//de bzhin 'bar zhing 
212b7(rgyud): 'bar ba yi;;me'i yi ge rnam par grags//sde tshan brgyad kyi dang po ni//de nyid stong pa'i dngos po dang//rlung gi sa bon dmar phe ni//rlung gi sa bon du ni grags//brgyad pa'i mtha'i sa boni ni//kha dog sngon po'i
212b8(rgyud): 'od mnyam pa;snying khar sa'i sa bon ni//de nyid sa'i sa bon no//'di rnams sa bon sa bon la//thig le'i yi ge rtsa ba ru//rtsa bar gnyis 'gyur re ba dang//rlung gi sa bon gsum pa ru//rtsa bar sgra ni bzhi pa'o
213a1(rgyud): //steng du thig le sbyar ba dang//de nyid bzhi pa'i sa bon ni//g.yo ba rlung gi sa bon ni//sa ni nyon mongs par 'gyur ro//phyugs ni me la kun du sbyar//yi ge brgyad pa'i sa bon ni//rnal 'byor ma
213a2(rgyud): brgyad sems dang bcas//bdag nyid de nyid rtso bo yis//'khor ba thams cad spyod mdzad pa//thams cad kun nas spyi bo dang//yi ge kun nas gnas pa'o//rtag pa'i rtsa bar mnan pa dang//sgra dang thig ler sbyar
213a3(rgyud): bar bya;dkar po kun d+ha zla ba'i mdog//ye shes rnam shes sa bon nyid//rgyu'i gzugs kyi sa bon ni//sa bon sa bon de bas mchog//ye shes rnam shes zhes bya'i ming//las kun la ni rab sbyar bya//byang chub ses
213a4(rgyud): dpa' zhi ba ni//zhi ba'i gzugs can rdzu 'phrul che//mthar ni swahA ring por ldan//sngon du bsnyen pa'i sngags pa yis//gnyi ka'i las la sgoms nas ni//'di yis lha mo sbyar bar bya//sngags pa
213a5(rgyud): bsregs pa'i sngags la ni//sngon du byugs pa med pa'o//lha 'am yang na lha mo la//khro bo'i gzugs 'joms lasog+sani//'joms pa'i yi ge mthar gzhag ste//bzlas shing bsnyen pa las
213a6(rgyud): sogs bya//gzugs can dngos po yis//khro bo khro mo khyad par nyid//bsregs dang 'joms pa'i sa bon ni//khyad par dang ni khyad par can//de nyid de nyid kun gyis ni//rab tu spyad cing lta bar bya//de nyid med pa'i
213a7(rgyud): sngags pa ni//dngos grub ring du bstan pa yin//bsgrub pa'i dus kyi lha nyid kyi//sa bon mtha' ru zlas par bya//mchog tu zhi ba de nyid la//dang po dbyangs yig mtha' ru'o//khro bo khro mo'i lha la ni//rdo rje'i sa bon mtha' ma ru
213a8(rgyud): lha mo kun gyi de nyid la//bdud rtsi'i yi ge mtha' ma ru//dang po'i sngags kyi sa bon ni//phyag mtshan gcig ni rnam spangs nas//bdag nyid ming gi sa bon ni//gnyi ka yongasu rtags pa'i rgyu//stong dang stong pa'i sa bon ni
213b1(rgyud): bar ma sgrub pa'i lha nyid do//nyin mo'i yi ge dang po ru//bsgrub pa bsgrub bya'i ming nyid ni//de'i bsgrub pa'i las rnams ni//rnal 'byor ma'i rnam par mtshon//sngags pas sngon gyi dus su ni//rab tu lta zhing spyad par bya//
213b2(rgyud): nyin mo stong pa'i bsgrub pa ni//'chi bar yang ni the tsom med//nyin mo nyin mo nyin mor bsgrub//stong pa stong pa bar ma'o//go zlog pa'i sa bon ni//lhag ma'i yi ge btsi bar bya//nyin bar bsgrub bya lhag ma ni//bsgrub pa
213b3(rgyud): po la bu lon ni//bsgrub bya stong pa lhag pa'o//dgra nyid pas na 'bras bu med//rab tu lta zhing spyad pa yis//sngags kyi bsgrub par bshad pa yin//sngon du ma ning sa bon dang //de bzhin bsgrub bya'i ma ning nyid//dbus
213b4(rgyud): su ma ning yi ge ni//dngos grub stong par bshad pa yin//de nyid 'di yi sa bon gyis//rang 'dod lha nyid bsgrub par bya//thog ma tha ma khyad par du//dbus su bsgrub bya'i ming nyid do//mtho dang dma' dang bar
213b5(rgyud): ma dang /khyad nyid dang ni khyad par ro//zhi dang rgyas pa la sogs la//khyad par sa bon ldan pa nyid//stong pa'i sde tshan gnyis pa la rtsa bar re ba sgra nyid dang/steng du zla tshes thig le ni//kha dog ser po kun 'od la
213b6(rgyud): lha mo rnal 'byor ma kun gyis//snying po yi ni snying po mchog//gong du phyag 'tshal yi ge dang //'dod bzhin ming ni dbus su'o//mtha' nyid mtha' ni sreg sogs nyid//thams cad du ni kun nas ldan//stong pa'i sde
213b7(rgyud): tshan gnyis pa la//spyi bor e'i rnam pas mtshan//rtsa bar ra dang thig ler bcas//kha dog dkar po mnyam pa'i 'od//dga' byed las kyi sa bon gyis//rnal 'byor ma kun dga' bar byed//de nyid kun gyi
213b8(rgyud): sa bon ni/rnal 'byor ma kun gyis rab sbyar ro//de nas sman sogs rim pa ni//khyad par nyid du sbyar bar bya//bla re bla re nyid dang ni//spos dang bdug pa dri la sogs//sngags dang phyag rgya'i sbyor ba yis//
214a1(rgyud): /mkha'agro ma'i gtor ma byin gyis rlabs la//sngon du gnyis med rtor mar ldan//dri'i mnAl gru bzhi la//'bras bu rnal 'byor pa yis bris la//gur gum shin du dri zhim ldan//re dmig
214a2(rgyud): nyi shu rtsa lnga pa//blo dang ldan pas gru bzhir bri//dbang po la sogs re mig gnas//blo dang ldan pas yig bkod//dngos dang dngos med ldan pa yi//de'i sa bon dbus su'o//nyin mo'i yi ge thams cad
214a3(rgyud): la/ji bzhin sa la dgod par bya//lhag mar sde tshan brgyad pa yi//sa bon mnyam par gzhag pa bkod//sgra dang thig ler yang dag ldan//kha dog dmar po'i 'od mnyam pa'o//gu gul dbang po
214a4(rgyud): lag pa dang/rmongs byed rengs byed de bzhin sra//sad ta si la tsam pa ka//kun du ru dang ki li ki//son tsi ka dang ba la dang//gu gul ru rta dur ba ka//me dang dri dang gur gum dang//tsan dan g+hi
214a5(rgyud): wang yang dag ldan//sha ka ra dang li shi dang//kri yang ku dang ge sar dang//chu srin sder mo nub phyogs cha//re mig kun dang gnas kun dung//dbang po la sogs sman ris bzhin
214a6(rgyud): rdzas ni re re re re dang//cha la sa bon zlas par bya//phyag mtshan dang po sreg pa mtha'//brgyad brgya ru ni bzlas pa bya//cha dang ldan sogs sa bon ni//sman dang ldan na de bas mchog//bdud rtsi lnga dang yang dag

214b2(rgyud): dang cha ru bya//thig le ru ni sbyin byas nas//rgyal po'i rigs la 'gro ba dang//tha snyad las ni rgyam par rgyal//'gro ba yang dag rmongs par byed//dang po sha ka ra la sogs pa dang//tsan dan du ni rnam par sbyar//sde tshan
214b3(rgyud): cig gi cha dang ni//tsan dan gyi ni cha nyid dang//byams pa'i gnas su rnal 'byor pa//yan lag nying lag byugs byas nas//dri ni sna la snams tsam gyis//nges par dbang du byed pa yi//mtshan dang mtshan gyi sha ka ra/
214b4(rgyud): sbrang rtsi'i cha dang sbyar byas nas//mchog tu sgom zhing bdug pa yis//kun nas nges par dbang du byed//'tshams kyi na kyi ya bzung nas//sha ka ra yi cha dang bsres//bdug pas bdug cing bdug byas nas
214b5(rgyud): mi ni kun nas rmongs par byed//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//ye shes mkha' la sbrul nas ni//sa bon so so'i sa bon nyid//kun nas brgya phrag gcig du'o//de bzhin du ni dmar po'i las
214b6(rgyud): de'i rjes ni kun bzung nas//bdag nyid ye shes lha ru'o//nyi ma'i lag pa'i rdzas rnams ni//kha dog dmar po mnyam pa 'od//de nyid sa bon bzlas pa yis//g.yo ba dang ni ltung bar byed//snying ka'i dkyil 'khor dbus gan+sa
214b7(rgyud): par;nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor bsams nas ni//de'i dbus su sa bon ni//skyed par re ba thig le can//khri phrag gcig tu bzlas pa yis//gnyis kyis ltung ba 'am g.yo bar byed//phyin nas rtor ma'i las kyis ni//g.yo
214b8(rgyud): dang mi g.yo 'grub par byed//gu gul la sogs srin bu lngas//rtsa ba brgyad dang sbyar byas nas//'phro ba'i sgong ba tsam zos pas//mod la myur du tshim par 'gyur//sha ta la sogs srin bu ni//'o ma bdun pa sbyar byas nas

215a3(rgyud): dbyangs dang bral bar 'gyur//'tshams kyi ma ra kun bzung nas//sbrang rtsi mar gyi cha dang sbyar//de'i phyi ma bza' byas nas//de'i mod la bzir bas 'dzin//'tshams kyi na kyi yang dag bzung //sbrang rtsi lhan cig sgos

215b8(rgyud): mi'i bsnu/ma dang kun du sbyar//dri ba ma dang ni dri ba mar skam//cho lor 'jug cing dga' ba dang//snga dro thig ler sbyin byas nas//rgyal po'i rigs la nyer 'gro ba//skye bo skal ldan myur du 'gyur//sa rdzas sngon
216a1(rgyud): @#//po dang por sbyar//phye ma zhib tu byas nas ni//sbrang rtsi kha ra'i cha nyid dang//rang 'dod spos su rab sbyar nas//mi dga' ba(sa) kun dga' ba//ngag gi 'dren pa nyid du 'gyur//de'i bdug pas
216a2(rgyud): bdug pa'i lus|'gro ba kun du rmongs par 'gyur//ka ko la sogs dang ldan pa'i//che ma mnyam por char byas te//tsan dan dang po thams cad kun//cha gsum du ni bya bar bya//mi byams dman zhing 
216a3(rgyud): bskur ba ni|bskal ldan mdzes par 'dod 'gyur na//de'i yan lag la byugs la//'gro ba rnam par rmongs byed yin//de'i sde tshan bdun pa ni//mar dang 'o ma dang sres nas//kun gyi dang bya sbyar byas nas//drib ma

216a5(rgyud): la sogs kun gyi cha//me la sro bar rab tu sbyar//ka ran dza'i drog ma tsam//yid du mi 'ong ba'i gnas//khar cug kun du de'i ngag//tshig gsum tshig tu brjod pa yis//rang 'dod bzhin du dbang du byed
